`products.html`'s CRM and Billing slabs already carried an eye-line badge ("02 · Customers · included with AUZsPOS", "03 · Billing · included with AUZsPOS") above their giant heading; Inventory's slab had no eye-line at all, just "Included with AUZsPOS." buried in its summary paragraph -- the one inconsistency left from the "use AUZsPOS, not the short names" open item. Added the matching eye-line (`04 · Stock · included with AUZsPOS`) as a direct child of `.slab` (the CSS already supports an `.eye` either nested in `.blk` or directly under `.slab` -- `html.fx-flow .slab>.eye,html.fx-flow .slab .blk .eye{...}` -- so no new rule was needed) and trimmed the now-redundant sentence from the paragraph. Also added the `aria-label="AUZsMob"` the other three AUZs-branded headings (POS/Pay/Ledger) already had, which AUZsMob's heading was missing. The cart button logic itself was already correct (`AUZcart`'s `INCLUDED` list already disables these three buttons client-side with "Included with AUZsPOS" text) -- this was purely a static-copy consistency fix, verified with a real-browser screenshot.

## AUZsMob vendor dues: a per-purchase FIFO breakdown, computed client-side, no new table
Vendor payable was a flat aggregate (`sum(purchases) - sum(payments)` per vendor) with no way to see which specific purchase was still unpaid -- flagged as a known simplification in the AUZsMob session log. `mob_payments` has no column tying a payment to one purchase (only a vendor-level bucket, per db/080's own schema comment), and building a real allocation table (mirroring Accounting's `acc_allocations`) would mean maintaining allocation rows across every purchase edit/void -- heavier than the ask. Instead, `vendorPurchaseDues()` in `app/public/mob/p-dues.js` computes a FIFO breakdown fresh every time, entirely client-side from data already synced to the device (purchases + vendor_due payments for that one vendor): sort the vendor's non-voided purchases oldest first, walk them consuming the vendor's total payments as a running balance, and the per-purchase `due` is whatever's left after the running balance is exhausted. No new table, no server call, works offline -- consistent with the rest of AUZsMob. Tapping a vendor in Dues now opens a detail sheet (`vendorDueDetail`) showing total purchased / paid / due plus the list of still-unpaid purchases (item, qty×rate, remaining due), with "Record payment" still one tap away. The file's own header comment is explicit that this is a best-effort traceability view, not a real ledger: editing a purchase after some of a vendor's other purchases have already "used up" the FIFO order can shuffle which purchase a payment looks allocated to, even though the vendor-level total is always exactly right either way.
- **A real bug fixed along the way**: the vendor-level aggregate itself never excluded voided purchases (`for (const p of purchases) { if (!p.vendor_id) continue; ...}` had no `p.voided` check) -- a voided purchase inflated a vendor's "you owe" total forever. Fixed in the same pass (both the aggregate and the new FIFO calc skip `p.voided`).
- New i18n keys (`due`, `totalPurchased`, `totalPaidToVendor`, `unpaidPurchases`, `paidInFull`, `oldestFirstNote`) in both English and Hindi. Bumped `?v=` on `mob/i18n.js` and `mob/p-dues.js` in `mob.html`, and `sw.js`'s `V` + precache list.
- **Verified against a real running server with seeded data**: a vendor with three purchases (₹100/₹200/₹150) and a ₹250 payment correctly shows total purchased ₹450, paid ₹250, due ₹200, with the first purchase fully settled (not listed as unpaid), the second showing ₹50 still due, the third showing its full ₹150 -- and a separately-voided ₹1,000 purchase correctly excluded from every total. `node tests/run-local.mjs mobile` (52/52, no regression).

## SESSION LOG: salesman accounts (`app/public/salesman.html`, db/137-145, PRs #278-287)

Owner request, from scratch: give the owner's salespeople their own tool to create a real trial tenant for a prospect, brand it to look built for that client, demo it, and either hand it over as a paying client or delete it if the deal falls through -- without ever touching `site/admin.html` (platform-admin only) or the owner's own credentials.

- **Foundation (`db/137`, PR #278):** `platform_salesmen` (one row per salesman login, linked to an `auth_users` row), `is_salesman()` SECURITY DEFINER check, `t_salesman_read` RLS policy on `tenants` so a salesman can see only tenants they created (`tenants.created_by_salesman`). `salesman_provision_trial(business_name, slug, niche)` creates a real `tenants` row with `is_trial=true`; `salesman_branding_get`/`salesman_branding_save` read/write the usual `settings` record fields (logo, colours, hero, gallery -- the same fields every tenant's own site already uses, no parallel schema). Admin-side: `admin_add_salesman`/`admin_list_salesmen`/`admin_set_salesman_active` so the platform admin controls who gets a salesman login.
- **Google sign-in for salesmen (PR #279)** and **routing a salesman landing on `app.auzslab.in` straight to `salesman.html`** instead of "No business yet" (PR #280, `start.js`'s `boot()` probes `salesman_my_trials()` before giving up).
- **A real starter menu, not an empty shell (PR #281, db/139):** `salesman_provision_trial` was extended to seed a 24-item café menu (5 categories) plus six dine-in tables (`T1`-`T6`) at creation time, so a brand-new trial is immediately demoable instead of showing the generic 2-item POS placeholder. **Changed its return value from a bare `uuid` to `jsonb {tenant_id, table_id}`** (needs `drop function` before `create function` -- Postgres refuses to change a function's return type via `create or replace`) so the caller has a real table id to build a QR-order demo link from. **Lesson repeated here from elsewhere in this file**: every table record needed `'id', tbl1` duplicated inside its own `data` jsonb, matching `pos/core.js`'s own `save()` convention -- `public_menu()` only ever returns the `data` column (`jsonb_agg(r.data)`), so a table/category referenced by id from outside (a QR link's `?t=`, an item's `cat` field) is invisible to the client unless its own `data` also carries that id.
- **One-tap "Open their POS" + "Open QR order demo" (PR #282, db/140):** a new `/admin/salesman-open-pos` HTTP route mints a real session for the trial's owner (reusing the owner's `auth_users` row if a login already exists, else creating a throwaway `...@trial.auzslab.in` one) via `signToken()`, then `salesman.html` builds the exact same `#auz_gt=<base64url {t,u}>` URL-fragment handoff `site/signin.html`'s Google flow already uses and opens the real POS pre-authenticated -- no separate "salesman preview mode" was built, this is a real owner session. "Open QR order demo" opens `site.html?t=<table_id>` using the `table_id` db/139 now returns. Found and fixed in the same PR: salesman-created trials from before db/139 had no tables at all, so the QR demo had nothing to link to.
- **Permanent delete for a dead prospect (PR #283):** `salesman_delete_trial(tenant_id, confirm_slug)` reuses `delete_client()`'s full purge routine (the same one admin.html's own permanent-delete button calls, db/106) but gated to a salesman's own `created_by_salesman` trials only, with a typed-slug confirmation in the UI matching every other irreversible-delete pattern in this codebase.
- **Two real bugs found and fixed chasing "the client's own logo never shows on their POS icon" (PR #284, db/141):**
  1. `appicon.js` checked `settings.logo.startsWith('/uploads/')` literally, but `logo` is stored as an absolute URL (`https://api.auzslab.in/uploads/...`) -- never matched. Fixed by parsing `new URL(logo, 'https://x').pathname` instead.
  2. The real, bigger bug underneath it: `appicon.js`'s `tenantForIcon()`/`tenantSettings()` ran bare `pool.query()` against RLS-protected `tenants`/`records` -- `pool` connects as the `app` role with no session set, so RLS silently returned **zero rows for every tenant, unconditionally, the entire time this feature has existed** -- not a salesman-specific bug, every tenant's POS icon has always ignored its own logo. This is the same bug shape this file now documents five times over (`createOrder()`, `storage.js`'s `saveSiteUpload`, the `subscriptions` table, `/auth/2fa/setup`, and now this) -- a function only exercised when someone actually requests the icon, never on a normal page load, shipping with a guaranteed failure nobody had reported. Fixed with a new SECURITY DEFINER SQL function `public_app_icon_context(p_slug)` (bundles tenant + settings in one call, same pattern as `public_menu`/`public_invoice`) and a rewritten `tenantForIconContext()` replacing both old bare queries.
  Same PR added Hero photo and Gallery photo upload fields to salesman.html's branding panel (`uploadBrandImage()`, POSTs to `/storage/salesman/:tenantId/:prefix`), using the tenant's existing `siteHero`/`siteGallery` settings fields.
- **Default café photos so a brand-new trial never looks empty while a client scans a QR code (PR #285, db/142-143):** the owner supplied 11 real café photos; `salesman_provision_trial()` was extended to set them as every category's `item.img` plus a default `siteHero`/`siteGallery`, as static files in `app/public/assets/demo-menu/` (shared defaults, same-origin on every subdomain like `/logo.png` -- no per-tenant upload needed for the starting state). A salesman can still replace any of it with the real client's own photos afterward, exactly like the logo.
- **Compressed the photos (PR #286):** `sharp`/mozjpeg, quality 72, EXIF stripped, resize-capped (688KB -> 432KB total). Caught and fixed a real swapped-file bug in the same pass: the hot- and cold-beverage photos had been saved under each other's filenames.
- **Backfill for trials created before this photo-seeding existed (PR #287, db/144):** a real report ("Mannat Cafe... there is no photos updated") was root-caused to seeding only ever applying at `salesman_provision_trial()` time, never retroactively -- a trial created earlier in the session, before db/142/143 shipped, was correctly showing the old 2-item placeholder with no photos, not a bug in the new seeding logic. `salesman_apply_demo_content(tenant_id)` is a general-purpose, idempotent backfill: if the tenant still has zero real categories, it runs the full menu+tables+photos seed; if a menu already exists, it only backfills missing photos onto items that match the known demo-menu category names, and retires the generic "Masala Tea ₹25 / Coffee ₹49" placeholder by exact name+price match (same precedent as `db/050`'s Mannat Cafe real-menu migration). A new "Menu/website looks empty? Add demo photos" button in salesman.html calls it and toasts what changed.
- **Item-specific photos, not one photo per whole category (`db/145`, this entry's own change, not yet shipped as of writing this section):** the owner's own wording: *"item wise not just one photo across all item of same categories and in gallery i will upload myself leave it empty and upload use intelligence to place photo based on requires not just randomly anything."* Three changes to both `salesman_provision_trial()` and `salesman_apply_demo_content()`:
  1. **`siteGallery` is no longer defaulted at all** (dropped from both functions' settings jsonb) -- the salesman's own gallery-upload UI (PR #284) is the only way a trial gets gallery photos now; `siteHero` is kept (cafe niche only) since the hero banner already has a genuinely matching photo.
  2. **Each of the 9 real café photos is assigned only to the specific item it actually depicts**, decided by actually looking at each photo's content rather than mapping them onto whole categories: `Masala Chai` -> the chai photo, `Cappuccino` -> the hero cappuccino photo (reused: it is also a genuine photo of a cappuccino, not just the website banner), `Cold Coffee` -> the iced-coffee photo, `Chocolate Milkshake` -> the cream-topped iced-drink gallery photo, `French Fries` -> the real fries photo, `Veg Spring Roll` -> the real spring-rolls photo, `Grilled Veg Sandwich`/`Club Sandwich` -> the real sandwich photo (same dish type, a genuine match for both), `Veg Burger`/`Chicken Burger` -> the real burger photo (same dish type, genuine match for both). Ten items total get a photo.
  3. **The other 14 items (Black Coffee, Cafe Latte, Hot Chocolate, Iced Tea, Fresh Lime Soda, Mango Shake, Chicken Wings, Paneer Tikka, Nachos with Salsa, Pasta Alfredo, Chocolate Brownie, Blueberry Cheesecake, Vanilla Ice Cream, Chocolate Lava Cake) get no `img` key at all**, rather than a mismatched or randomly-picked photo -- `site.html`'s existing `phArt(categoryName)` decorative SVG line-art fallback (already built, used whenever `item.img` is absent) is the correct, intended "no real photo yet" state, not a bug to paper over with a wrong photo. `salesman_apply_demo_content()`'s backfill branch was changed the same way: from a category-name-keyed `case` to an item-name-keyed one, only ever touching the 10 genuinely-matched item names and leaving the other 14 (and anything the salesman already set) completely untouched.
  - **Verified**: full migration chain applies clean on a scratch database; direct SQL confirms a fresh trial has exactly 10 items with a photo and 14 without, empty `siteGallery`, and a set `siteHero`; a real-browser Playwright smoke test confirms `site.html`'s Hot Beverages tab renders a real `<img>` for Masala Chai/Cappuccino and the SVG fallback (no `<img>`) for Black Coffee/Cafe Latte/Hot Chocolate; `pos` (27/27) and `marketing` (151/151) regression suites pass clean.
  - Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/145_salesman_item_specific_photos.sql` (no API rebuild -- SQL only), `git pull`.
  - **The real VPS deploy hit a genuine bug this session's own scratch-DB test never exercised, and PR #289 fixed it.** Production's `salesman_provision_trial()` still returned `uuid`, not `jsonb` -- db/139's own return-type switch (`uuid` -> `jsonb {tenant_id, table_id}`) apparently never actually reached this tenant's live database, even though the feature built on top of it (`table_id`-driven "Open QR order demo", PR #282) was already working there. Postgres's `create or replace function` refuses to change a return type outright (`ERROR: cannot change return type of existing function`), so db/145's plain redefinition failed on the real server with the exact HINT db/139 itself already names: `DROP FUNCTION salesman_provision_trial(text,text,text)` first. Fixed by adding that explicit `drop function if exists ...;` immediately before the `create function` in db/145 (same workaround db/139 used the first time this exact change was made). **Caught only because the owner ran the real migration on the real server and pasted back the actual psql output** -- the earlier "verified: full migration chain applies clean on a scratch database" claim above was true but incomplete: that scratch-DB test (built fresh from db/000 onward) always starts from db/139's jsonb-returning definition, so it could never have reproduced a server whose own history diverged from the migration chain's assumed state. **Lesson, same shape as this file's other "a dormant/lazy code path ships broken and nobody notices until a real run hits it" entries, but inverted: here the scratch-DB test itself was the dormant path** -- it is only as strong as the assumption that the target server's migration history exactly matches the chain being replayed; a server where an earlier migration silently didn't apply (or applied then got reverted by hand) is invisible to a from-scratch rebuild. Re-verified end to end afterward: full chain (correctly listing every `db/*.sql` via `ls db/*.sql | sort -V`, not a `db/0*.sql` glob that silently skips every 3-digit migration from db/100 onward -- a second, separate mistake caught and fixed in the same pass) applies clean, a real `salesman_provision_trial()` call as a seeded salesman returns the right jsonb shape, and the owner's own production run (pasted psql output) shows `DROP FUNCTION` / `CREATE FUNCTION` / `CREATE FUNCTION` with no errors.

## Scan to bill (AUZsLedger `#/scan`) and the standalone AUZsScan app (`scan.html`)
Owner request: scan products with the phone camera, create the entry and fetch details, make a bill, add stock by scanning, send the bill on WhatsApp, in the AUZslab look (Apple HIG). Owner answers: web first (a Flutter app comes later, once the flow is proven), data = the real AUZsLedger books, an unknown barcode asks the user (no outside product lookup), WhatsApp = free `wa.me` link.
- **No server change, no migration.** It uses what the invoice editor and stock screens already call: `acc_list_products` (search matches `barcode = <code>`), `acc_save_product` (new barcode), `acc_stock_adjust` (stock in, `opening:false`), `acc_save_document` with `post:true` + `payments` (gapless number, GST, stock, COGS, receivable), `acc_get_document`, `acc_share_document` (public `bill.html?t=` link), `acc_log_comm`. GST for the on-screen totals is `calcDoc()` from `p-editor.js`, so screen and server cannot disagree. Roles are the existing ones: Sell needs `acc_sales`, Add stock and new products need `acc_inventory` (a cashier gets an explanation for an unknown code instead of a failing form).
- **Files:** `ds/scanner.js` (camera overlay: `auzScanner.open({title,onCode,footer,onClose})`; uses the browser's `BarcodeDetector` where it exists, else the bundled ZXing reader `vendor/zxing/index.min.js` (Apache-2.0, lazy loaded, this is what iPhone Safari uses); torch where the phone allows, ignores the same code for 1.6 s, typing a code and a Bluetooth/USB scanner (types + Enter) both work, camera-blocked message; `variants()` treats 12-digit UPC-A and 13-digit EAN with a leading 0 as one product), `accounts/p-scan.js` (the page: Sell / Add stock, cart, line editor with price and per-item discount in % or rupees, customer or Walk-in, Cash/UPI/Bank/Credit, success sheet with WhatsApp / print or PDF / copy link), `scan.html` + `manifest-scan.json` + `icon-scan*.{svg,png}` + `logo-scan.svg` (the icon and the launch animation are generated by `tools/make-icons.mjs`: edit the `scan` mark there). `scan.html` sets `window.AUZ_SCAN = true` and loads the Accounting scripts it needs (core, shell, offline, p-docs, p-editor, p-parties, p-money, p-stock, p-scan); `accounts/shell.js` switches to the short menu (Scan / Sales, Products, Stock) and the AUZsScan name when `AUZ_SCAN` is set. `start.js` has an AUZsScan tile (same entitlement as AUZsLedger: `accounting`). The Android manifest patch in `mobile/build.mjs` now declares `CAMERA` (without it the WebView never offers the camera).
- **Rules worth knowing:** a barcode is only an ID; price, HSN and GST always come from the product. Stock cannot be sold past what is in stock unless the organisation allows negative stock (the scan screen refuses with "Only N in stock"). Credit needs a named customer. Posting needs a connection; offline, "Save on this device" keeps a draft through `queueDraft` (same offline-drafts queue as the invoice editor). The cart survives an accidental refresh (`sessionStorage`, product ids only). Camera permission needs https (already true). Torch does not exist on iPhone Safari. iPhone Safari needs the ZXing fallback and is slower than Android Chrome's built-in detector.
- **Also fixed:** `.banner[hidden]{display:none}` in `accounts.css` (the empty offline-bar pill was showing at the top of every Ledger page because `.banner{display:flex}` beat the `hidden` attribute).
- **Not built (say so, do not claim):** the Flutter app (planned next; needs the owner's choices for state management, local database and Bluetooth printer), thermal-printer receipts from this screen (the browser ESC/POS helper `ds/escpos.js` exists but is not wired here), batch/expiry/serial capture while scanning, an outside barcode database, hub tile icons, a Play listing for AUZsScan on its own (the hub app opens it).
- **Test:** `node tests/run-local.mjs scan` (14 checks: the ZXing decoder on a generated EAN-13 image, UPC/EAN equivalence, typed-barcode sale with GST split, stock cap, credit rule, posted invoice + stock + integrity check + WhatsApp link, unknown barcode creating a product, receiving stock, the camera screen with a canvas standing in for the camera, camera blocked, standalone app phone and desktop, cashier role). A real iPhone and a real printer have not been tried.
- **Deploy:** static only: `git pull`. Home-screen users add `scan.html` from their own business address (or `app.auzslab.in/scan.html`); old Accounting copies refresh through the service worker (`sw.js` V `pos-v74`).

## Salesman demo photos: one photo per item, and old trials get fixed (db/146)
Owner report: on a salesman's demo website every product in a category showed the same image. db/145 only changed NEW trials and its backfill fills only items with no photo, so trials made earlier kept the old category-wide photos; also the two sandwiches and the two burgers shared one file. `db/146_salesman_one_photo_per_item.sql` recreates `salesman_provision_trial` / `salesman_apply_demo_content` so each default photo sits on exactly one item (Grilled Veg Sandwich and Veg Burger keep theirs; Club Sandwich and Chicken Burger show the drawn icon), and runs a data fix over every existing item whose `img` starts with `/assets/demo-menu/` (reset to its own photo, or removed). A salesman's own uploaded photo is never touched. It also clears the old default `siteGallery` when every entry is one of our demo files. Safe to re-run. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/146_salesman_one_photo_per_item.sql` (no API rebuild, no `git pull` needed for the data fix itself, but pull first so the file exists).

## AUZsScan listed as a product page (SUPERSEDED: now its own product, see "AUZsScan is a sellable product" below)
`site/scan-and-bill.html` is the marketing page for Scan to bill / the AUZsScan app (see the "Scan to bill" section). It is **not sold separately and has no price or cart button**: it says "Included with AUZsLedger" and links to `accounting.html`, the same way CRM/Billing/Inventory are shown as included. It is in the Products submenu and the footer Products column of every marketing page (the canonical menu block now has eight product links: AUZsScan sits after AUZsLedger), in `products.html` (slab 11, no `data-product`, so no cart), `sitemap.html` and `sitemap.xml`. The demo button opens `demo.auzslab.in/scan.html` (the demo business has Accounting). If it is ever sold on its own it needs a feature key, a price row and the cart wiring first.

## Salesman trials follow the business type (db/147)
`salesman.html` no longer assumes a cafe. Per niche (`NICHE_APP` in the page): **cafe** = POS + QR table demo + website + demo photos (unchanged); **salon** = "Open their admin console" (signs straight into `/salon/admin/`) and "Open their website" (`/salon/`); the cafe branding form is hidden because a salon is branded inside its own console (Settings -> Branding); **mobile** = "Open their AUZsMob" (`/mob.html`, signed in; `mob_trial_seed(tid)` gives an empty trial a sample shop the first time, only when it has no items); **gym / retail / general** = POS only (no cafe website or QR). The session hand-over uses the same `#auz_gt=` fragment as the POS; `/admin/salesman-open-pos` now also returns `niche`. The salon console cannot use that token directly (cookie sessions), so `salon.js` has `POST /admin/handoff {token}` (checks the token belongs to the OWNER of this salon, then starts the console session) and `salon/admin/admin.js` `boot()` posts the fragment to it and clears it. Test: salon suite ("Salesman hand-over"). Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/147_salesman_trial_mobile_seed.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`. Not built: sample data for gym/retail trials.

## Admin: client payments (UTR) + salesman work dashboard (db/148)
`site/admin.html`. **Client payments:** `client_payments` (RLS on, no policies; only SECURITY DEFINER functions `admin_add_client_payment`, `admin_list_client_payments`, `admin_delete_client_payment`, all platform-admin only and audit-logged). Clients -> **Payments** button opens a sheet with that client's payments and a form (amount, UPI/bank/cash/cheque/card/other, UTR, date, "for", note); UPI and bank need a UTR, and a UTR can be recorded only once (unique, case-insensitive). The **Client payments** section lists everything with this-month and all-time totals. Recording a payment for a salesman's trial sets `tenants.is_trial = false` (that is what "converted" means). Payments are manual records: nothing is charged and `renewal_date` is not changed by them. **Salesman work** section: `admin_salesman_work(p_days)` per salesman: prospects created, converted, money received from their prospects, last prospect, and each prospect's status (trial / converted, owner login set, amount received); period filter 7/30/90 days/all. Prospects a salesman deleted are gone, so they are not counted. Registered in `server/src/index.js`. Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/148_client_payments_salesman_work.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`. Test: journeys suite.

## checkout.html layout: two columns on desktop
`site/checkout.html`: the bill card and the pay card (UPI QR, Razorpay button, send button) sit in `.co-cols`; from 900px wide they are two columns (bill left, pay right, pay card sticky) instead of one 640px column with an empty right half; on phones it is unchanged (one column). Static only.

## Scan to bill: MRP, editable product prices, customer name, beep
`accounts/p-scan.js` (v3) + `ds/scanner.js` (v2), `sw.js` V `pos-v75`. **MRP:** the New product sheet asks for MRP (typing it fills the selling price if empty; a selling price above MRP is refused) and every bill line shows "MRP" and warns when the price for this bill is above it. **Prices of any product, even one saved long ago:** tap a line -> the sheet has the price for THIS bill, plus a "Product prices" block (selling, purchase, MRP) with "Save product prices" which calls `acc_save_product` (needs `acc_inventory`; a cashier only sees the this-bill price). **Customer name:** a walk-in sale now asks for a name and WhatsApp number; `scEnsureParty()` finds the customer by phone or creates one (`acc_save_party`), so the invoice carries the name and a credit sale is allowed with a typed name. Offline, the name is not saved (it needs a connection) and a toast says so. **Beep:** `auzScanner.beep(ok)` (loud square tone; low double tone for a refusal) plus a vibrate; the audio context is created and resumed inside the first tap (it stayed silent when first made by the camera loop), and typed / Bluetooth scans beep through the same function. No mute switch yet. Test: scan suite (15 checks). Static only.

## Import products from Busy / Tally + beep at the moment of scanning
- **Import** (`accounts/p-data.js` v2, the existing Import wizard, now also in the AUZsScan app menu and as an "Import from Busy or Tally" button on the Products page): for Products a "Where is your list from" switch (Any spreadsheet / Busy / Tally) picks the header names that program uses (`SRC[...].hints`; matched first, then the generic names, never stealing a column already taken). It reads CSV, Excel and **Tally Masters XML** (`readTallyXml`: UTF-16 and Tally's stray control characters handled; stock items only: name, group, unit, opening qty and rate, HSN, integrated-tax GST rate, part no, alias, standard price). Items with no code get one made from the item name (`skuOf`, so the same list imported twice finds the same items); `GST 5%` / `Exempt` / `Nos.` / `1,200.00` / `10 Nos` style cells are cleaned; a "Total" row is dropped. **Opening stock** from the file's quantity column is added through `acc_import(opening_stock)` only for items that did not exist before the import (so a second import never counts stock twice) and not for very large catalogues (`S.productsPartial`). Nothing is imported until the person has run "Check the file" (dry run) and confirmed; "already imported" lists leave the Import button off. The header lists are a best guess at what the two programs export, **not verified against real Busy or Tally exports** (none were available): the column match is always shown and editable. Customers/suppliers/other entities still use the generic matching. Export (CSV/Excel/PDF/backup) already existed on the Export tab; there is no export in Busy/Tally's own import format. Test: scan suite (Busy CSV, repeat import, Tally UTF-16 XML).
- **Beep:** `auzScanner` now sounds the beep in `accept()` the instant a code is read (camera, typed, Bluetooth), before the product is looked up or saved; a low double tone follows only when the result is a refusal (out of stock, no match). `p-scan.js` no longer beeps after saving.

## AUZsScan is a sellable product (feature key `scan`, db/149)
Owner request: sell AUZsScan on its own, Rs 460 a month + 18% GST (read as "plus GST", the same as every other price; change in Admin -> Pricing if "with GST" meant inclusive). It is a `product_prices` row (`scan`), so cart, pricing page and Razorpay/Cashfree charge pick it up like any product; the standard Rs 2,179 setup fee applies to a brand-new signup (not exempt; no flag edit needed to change that later, `setup_fee_exempt` is a data column).
- **Backend:** AUZsScan runs on the AUZsLedger books. `acc_guard()` (db/149 redefines it) lets a tenant with `scan` but not `accounting` call only `acc_view, acc_sales, acc_inventory, acc_import, acc_admin, acc_cancel`; everything else (purchases, banking, journals, reports, audit) answers "part of AUZsLedger". A tenant with both gets everything. The owner's switch-off (`enabled_features.scan=false`) works the same as for accounting.
- **Apps:** `scan.html` opens for `accounting` OR `scan` (`SCANAPP` branch in `accounts/shell.js`); `accounts.html` still needs `accounting`. `start.js` shows the AUZsScan tile for either. Account page: service group, product choice, staff-login link and permission list follow `scan`.
- **Marketing/admin:** cart label, admin FEATURE_LABEL / client editor tick box / profile labels, pricing page card (live price hook), products.html slab 11 (cart button), `scan-and-bill.html` price card (`cart.html?add=scan`), `site/icon-scan.svg`. Not added: bundles, yearly price (add them in Admin -> Pricing if wanted).
- Test: scan suite (17; "AUZsScan alone" checks server refusal of a ledger report, the Ledger page blocked, scan.html opening). Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/149_scan_product.sql` (no API rebuild).

## Product import from a PDF or photo (AUZsLedger / AUZsScan Import)
Import -> Products now also takes a **PDF or a photo** of a price or stock list (`accounts/p-data.js` v3, `ds/docread.js` v2 `parseProducts`). The text is read on the device (PDF.js, or Tesseract for scans and photos, same free reader as invoice scanning), turned into a table, and then goes through the SAME column match, dry run and import as a spreadsheet (nothing is saved before "Check the file" and Import). With a heading line (Item, HSN, Qty, Rate, Cost, GST %...) the columns follow it (`SRC.pdf` hints: Rate/Price -> sale price, Qty -> opening stock); with no headings every line is name + last number as the price (+ HSN and GST % if present), and a toast says so. Items with no code get one made from the name (`autoSku`). **Opening stock needs a cost** (the books refuse a zero-value stock journal): when the list has a Qty but no Cost/Purchase price column, the products are created and the quantities are skipped with a message (add stock from the Stock page). Photos and handwriting can misread: the column match is always shown. Test: scan suite (18; includes a Chromium-made PDF imported end to end). Static only: `git pull`.

## Classic tax invoice layout + client setup script (New Book World, db/150)
- **Layout:** `accounts/taxinv.js` (`auzTaxInvoice.build(data)`) draws the Indian "tax invoice" sheet a shop already uses: logo box, TAX INVOICE heading, Invoice No./Dated, Place of Supply/Reverse Charge, Billed to / Shipped to (a walk-in prints as "Cash"), S.N. | Description | HSN | Qty | MRP | Price | Amount table, Less: Discount, Grand Total (with total qty), tax summary per rate (CGST/SGST or IGST, "Exempt" for 0%), amount in words, bank details, terms, Receiver's Signature / For <business> / Authorised Signatory. It is used by BOTH the shared WhatsApp link (`bill.js`, so customers see it) and the in-app print (`p-docs.js` `invoiceSheetNode`/`printDoc`). Turned on per business by `acc_org.settings.brand.invoiceStyle = 'classic'` (Settings -> Organisation -> "Invoice layout"); everything else keeps the standard layout. Built with createElement/textContent only.
- **Numbers:** `settings.number_style = 'plain'` makes invoices read `1/2026-27` (db/150 redefines `acc_next_no`; invoices only, other document types keep their prefix). `public_acc_document` (db/150) now also returns each line's `mrp` and the customer's `party_phone`.
- **Client setup script:** `db_data/new_book_world_setup.sql` (apply by hand, safe to re-run): finds the login `viralwechaar@gmail.com`, creates/links the business `newbookworld` (niche retail, feature `scan` only, monthly_fee 460), sets the organisation from their bill (GSTIN 08AAVFN9235H1ZL, address, phone, email, SBI bank, terms, logo, classic layout, plain numbers) and adds the sample notebook (MRP 60, price 45, GST exempt). The logo is the client's own file (supplied by the owner), 360 px PNG, embedded in the script. Stock is not invented: they add it (or import) before selling. Test: scan suite runs the script twice and checks the shared link text, logo, no sideways scroll on a phone.
- Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... < db/149_scan_product.sql` (if not run), `< db/150_classic_invoice.sql`, then `< db_data/new_book_world_setup.sql`. No API rebuild.

## Import fix: blank "Service" column made every row fail (and a stray "null" on the Import page)
A product list in the template layout (Busy export saved with our columns: blank SKU, GST, prices, and a blank `Service (true/false)` column) failed every row with `invalid input syntax for type boolean: ""`, so Check never passed and Import stayed greyed out with no explanation. `rowsFor()` in `accounts/p-data.js` now leaves out blank `is_service` / `reorder_level` cells; "Make a code for items that have none" turns itself on whenever the SKU column is unmapped or blank on any row; the Import section says "Press Check the file first"; and two native `append(..., cond ? node : null)` calls that printed the word "null" now pass `''`. Test: scan suite (600 generated rows; run with `BUSY_CSV=/path/to/file.csv` to push a real export through the same flow, 2,440 rows import in the test).

## Import is tolerant of blanks and bad rows (db/151)
The apps call `acc_import`, which since db/151 is a thin wrapper: it drops every blank-string cell from every row (a blank cell means "not given", so an empty true/false or number cell no longer fails with `invalid input syntax for type boolean: ""`) and then calls the original function, renamed `acc_import_core`. This fixes old copies of the app too, with no browser update. The wizard's "Stop if any row has an error" now starts OFF: Check lists the bad rows, Import brings in every valid row and skips the rest (turn it on for all-or-nothing). Missing columns are fine except Name (SKU is made from the name when absent). Test: scan suite ("Import (server)" sends blank cells and one bad row; run with `BUSY_CSV=file.csv` to push a real export through the screens). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/151_import_blank_cells.sql`, `git pull` (no API rebuild; run it once only, it is safe to repeat).

## Products list was capped at 2,000 (wrong RPC argument shape) -- fixed
`acc_list_products` takes ONE jsonb argument `p`, so callers must send `{ p: { limit, search, ... } }`. `products()` and `searchProducts()` in `accounts/core.js` sent `{ limit }` / `{ search, limit }` directly, so the server never saw them: it always answered with its default 2,000 rows (the Products page said "2000 items" with 2,440 imported, and the on-server search used for catalogues beyond the cap returned the first alphabetical rows instead of matches). Both now send `{ p: {...} }`: the phone holds up to `PRODUCT_CAP` = 5,000 products, anything beyond is found by the server search when online, and the Items card shows "5000+" when the list is cut off. Lesson: a jsonb-argument RPC called with the wrong wrapper does not error, it silently uses its defaults. Test: scan suite imports 2,600 products, checks > 2,000 load and a server search finds the last one. Static only.

## New Book World bill: exact A4 template, and printing the shared bill page (supersedes the "Layout" bullet of the classic tax invoice section)
The owner supplied the client's real bill template (`NBW_Invoice_Template.pdf`, A4). `accounts/taxinv.js` now draws that page exactly: the sheet is a fixed **A4 page (794 x 1123 CSS px)** with the same bands, column widths (S.N. 5.2%, Description 40.1%, HSN 8.7%, Qty 8.3%, MRP 7.2%, Price 15.5%, Amount 14.9%), a tall item area that grows to fill the page, tax block with the amount in words, bank line and the terms / signature footer. On screen it is **scaled with CSS `zoom` to the width available** (so a phone shows the same whole page, not a squeezed one; pinch to read), in print it is real A4 (`@page{size:A4;margin:0}`, one page; more items than fit make a longer page). Address prints on one line ("Near Shanichar Ji Ka Than Jodhpur"). This is the default bill for New Book World because their org has `brand.invoiceStyle='classic'` (set by `db_data/new_book_world_setup.sql`; re-running it is only needed if that was never run).
- **Real bug fixed on the way: "Print or save as PDF" on the shared bill page (`bill.html`) printed a BLANK page for every business**, because `accounts.css` hides `body>*:not(.print-only)` when printing and the bill lives in `<main id=app>`. `bill.html` now re-shows `body>main#app` in its print CSS. The scan test saves `tests/report/shots/classic-print.pdf` and asserts it is exactly one A4 page (and I looked at it).
- Test: scan suite (22).

## AUZsScan: Select all / bulk edit / bulk delete, and a new product in the middle of a bill (db/152)
- **Products page** (`accounts/p-stock.js` v5): an always-visible bar above the list with **Select all (N)** (everything the current search/filter shows), Clear, **Edit selected** (the existing bulk editor: GST, HSN, category, prices by %, status) and **Delete selected**. Delete calls `acc_delete_products(p_ids)` (db/152, `acc_inventory`, registered in `server/src/index.js`): a product nothing refers to is deleted for good; one already on a bill, in stock or otherwise referenced is **switched off** (`active=false`, cannot be sold, old bills intact); the toast says how many of each. Max 5,000 per call; audit-logged. Tested with one unused and one used product.
- **Billing** (`accounts/p-scan.js` v6): the Scan screen has a **New product** button under the search box and a "New product: <typed text>" row at the end of every search; both open the same sheet as an unknown barcode (`scNewProduct('')`, name pre-filled, optional barcode, SKU made as `NP-<time>`), save the product and add it to the current bill (or the receive list in Add-stock mode). Needs `acc_inventory` (a cashier gets "your role cannot add products").
- **Real bug found by the test and fixed (same one for the barcode path):** saving a new product with a stock quantity but NO purchase price made the stock step fail with "A journal needs at least two lines" (the books refuse a zero-value stock entry) after the product itself had been saved. The sheet now asks for the purchase price first when the number in stock is above 0.
- Test: scan suite (23). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/152_delete_products.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (RPC list changed), `git pull`.

## Stock page lists every product, including ones with no stock yet (db/153)
`acc_stock_summary` (Stock -> Valuation) only listed products that had stock moves, so a freshly imported catalogue (all quantities 0) made the Stock page look empty ("imported 2,440 items but they are not in inventory"). db/153 adds `include_zero` (the page sends it by default; a "Show items with no stock yet" checkbox turns it off; up to 5,000 rows), returns `in_stock` (count with stock) so the "Items in stock" card stays honest, and changes the permission to `acc_view` + (`acc_reports` OR `acc_inventory`) so an AUZsScan-only business (which has `acc_inventory` but not `acc_reports`) can open it. Stock quantities still come only from stock entries (Add stock, Adjust stock, opening stock with a cost); an import without quantities creates products at 0. Test: scan suite (24). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/153_stock_all_items.sql`, `git pull` (no API rebuild).

## Invoice table lines vanished on iPhone (taxinv.js v7)
On a real iPhone the item table's lines (under the heading row and between the columns) were missing and the MRP number wrapped ("165.0 / 0"), although the same page in headless Chromium drew them. The page is scaled down with CSS `zoom` (about 0.45 on a phone), which turned the 1px `border-collapse` table borders into sub-pixel lines that Safari drops. Fixes: the table uses `border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:0` (it then draws borders like ordinary boxes) and its line width is the CSS variable `--bw`, set by `fit()` to `1.1 / zoom` (1 to 3 px), so a line is about one screen pixel at any scale; number cells are `white-space:nowrap` and the MRP column is wider. The scan test asserts the computed line width at phone scale is >= 1.5px and that no number wraps. **Not confirmed on a real iPhone from here: ask for a new screenshot.** If lines are still missing, the next step is to draw the column rules with background gradients instead of borders.


## AUZsScan is offline-first (db/154, `accounts/scan-sync.js`) -- supersedes "Needs a connection to POST" above
Scanning, selling, adding a NEW product and receiving stock all work with no connection (or one too slow to answer in 10 s). Each action is written to the phone first (IndexedDB `acc1`, store `sq`, opened as version 2 in `offline.js`) and sent later; nothing is lost on refresh.
- **Server:** `acc_offline_apply({op, kind, payload})` (db/154, registered in `server/src/index.js`) is idempotent on the client-made `op` (table `acc_offline_ops`, first result returned again with `duplicate:true`). kinds: `sale` (= `acc_save_document` with `post:true` and payments; a typed customer name/phone becomes or finds a customer; invoice gets `client_op` and `meta.offline_sale/offline_ref`), `product` (`acc_save_product`), `stock` (`acc_stock_adjust`). Online sales and stock receipts now ALSO go through it (same op, so a lost reply can never make a second bill). A sale marked `offline:true` is not refused for stock: `acc_stock_move` (redefined from db/066) skips the "insufficient stock" check when the transaction-local setting `acc.offline_sale` is set, so a real sale that already happened is never rejected because another phone sold the last unit; stock may go below zero until counted. The gapless invoice number is given when it syncs (order = order phones sync, not time of sale).
- **Phone (`scan-sync.js`, loaded after `offline.js`, before `p-scan.js`):** `sqEnqueue/sqFlush/sqRetry/sqDiscard`, provisional bill numbers `OFF-<3 letters>-<n>` (localStorage `scan.dev`/`scan.seq`), temp product ids `local:<uuid>` swapped for real ids at flush (`scan.map` in kv), `sqOverlay()` applies waiting sales/stock/new products on top of the last server product list (`SQ.base`, set by `products()` in `core.js` through `sqSetBase`) so local stock stays right across refreshes; refused items (period locked, no permission...) stay in the list with Retry/Discard, never silently dropped. Flush runs on the `online` event, every 60 s and from "Send now". The pending card is on the Scan page (`scPendingCard`), the top bar counts them.
- **Offline receipt:** a WhatsApp text (items, total, provisional number) -- the public bill link and the printable tax invoice need the real number, so they exist only after sync. New products made offline need the purchase price when stock > 0 (same rule as online).
- **The real invoice link used to just evaporate once an offline sale synced -- `sqFlush()` fetched the real `{id,number}` from the server (`sqRemember`) but nothing ever did anything with it beyond a toast ("N sales sent to your books with final invoice numbers").** The customer was stuck with the plain-text provisional receipt forever unless the cashier remembered to go find that sale in Sales and share it by hand. Fixed: `sqEnqueue('sale', ...)`'s meta now also carries the customer's `phone` (from `scSell()`'s already-resolved `pphone`), `sqRemember` also stores the synced doc's `id` and that `phone`, and the Scan page shows a **"Ready to send: the real invoice link"** card (`scSyncedCard()` in `p-scan.js`, refreshed on the same `scan:queue` event `scPendingCard` already listens to) for any synced sale that has a phone and hasn't been sent yet -- one tap builds the real `shareLink()` URL and opens the WhatsApp message, then marks it sent (`sqMarkSent`, a `scan.done` entry's own `sent` flag) so it doesn't keep nagging. Uses the same "open a blank tab inside the tap, point it at the URL once fetched" trick as `scDone()`, for the same iPhone popup-blocking reason.
- **Still needs a connection (said honestly):** changing saved product prices from a scan line, importing, bulk edit/delete, reports, cancelling an invoice, the tax-invoice PDF/share link, first sign-in on a phone (the copy of setup/products is saved after a first online visit).
- **Gotcha found by the test:** a top-level `const f = () => ...` in a classic script is NOT a `window` property, so `window.f` checks are always false; use `function f()` for anything looked up through `window`.
- **Also fixed:** PR #311 merged `taxinv.js` v7 but not the `?v=7` bumps in `scan.html`/`accounts.html`/`bill.html`/`sw.js`, so phones kept the old cached file. Bumped now (sw `V` = `pos-v90`).
- Test: scan suite (26 checks; the offline checks cut the network, queue a sale + new product + stock, reconnect, assert exactly-once, real numbers, stock, integrity, replay = duplicate, and a sale that would overdraw stock is still posted). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/154_scan_offline_first.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (new RPC), `git pull`.

## Request-first onboarding: cart sends a request, admin quotes, client pays, admin bills (db/155)
Owner request: a visitor no longer picks products and pays in the cart. Flow: **cart (request only, no prices or payment) -> client details form -> admin "Client requests" reviews (status Under verification / Confirmed) -> admin builds a quote -> WhatsApp payment-page link -> client reads terms, ticks the box, pays, uploads a payment screenshot -> admin confirms the payment -> GST bill (A4, PAID stamp) sent on WhatsApp.**
- **Tables** (db/155, RLS on, no policies, only SECURITY DEFINER functions): `client_orders` (details, status `new|verifying|confirmed|link_sent|payment_submitted|paid|rejected`, quote jsonb, totals, token, proof, invoice no.), invoice counter per Indian financial year. Invoice numbers are gapless `AUZ/2026-27/0001`, given only when the admin confirms payment. Totals are computed on the server (`client_order_totals`): lines (price, discount, GST %), setup fee on/off (default Rs 2,190, editable), custom charges, CGST+SGST for Rajasthan (state 08 or blank) else IGST. Renewal dates are per product (monthly = +1 month, yearly = +1 year from the payment date).
- **RPCs** (all registered in `server/src/index.js`): `submit_client_order`, `my_client_orders` (signed-in client), public by unguessable token: `public_client_order` (hides quote and totals until status is link_sent / payment_submitted / paid) and `public_order_submit_payment`; admin: `admin_list_client_orders`, `admin_get_client_order`, `admin_set_order_status`, `admin_update_client_order`, `admin_save_order_quote`, `admin_confirm_order_payment` (returns invoice no. and tenant id), `admin_mark_order_bill_sent`, `admin_open_client_orders_count`. Payment screenshots: `POST /storage/order-proof` (token, magic-byte check, rate limit) and admin-only `GET` using `admin_order_proof`.
- **Pages:** `site/cart.html` (rewritten: request only), `site/pay.html` (quote with GST breakdown, terms from `site/orderterms.js`, UNCHECKED agree box, UPI details, screenshot upload), `site/inv.html` (exact A4 bill, minimal Swiss layout from the owner's reference: big "Invoice" + number, From/To, Payment, Date, Services, totals, renewal table, refund / modification-charge / free bug fix / backup (no failover guarantee) / support-call charges / GST / renewal terms, signature, PAID stamp). `site/checkout.html` now only redirects. `site/admin.html` has the **Client requests** section (list, detail modal with status buttons, quote builder, WhatsApp message template, confirm payment, send bill). `site/company.js` exposes `window.AUZ_CO`.
- **Wording is in one place:** `site/orderterms.js` (terms, refund policy, change charges, bank/UPI details). Bank details are EMPTY until the owner supplies them; UPI id/name there are placeholders to confirm. SAC 9983 on the bill is to be checked with the accountant. Call/extra-support charge wording has no numbers (owner to set).
- **Left dormant, not removed:** the old Razorpay/Cashfree server path and `site/cart.html`'s old pricing code are gone from the cart, but the server routes remain.
- Pricing list the owner pasted (AuzsPos Rs 1,090 etc.) is NOT applied: waiting for the owner's answer.
- **Gotchas found by the tests:** `innerText` respects `text-transform:uppercase`, so assert text case-insensitively; plpgsql variables need a `v_` prefix; keep the bill on ONE A4 page (`min-height:1118px`, compact CSS; the orders suite counts PDF pages).
- Test: `node tests/run-local.mjs orders` (9 checks, incl. the PDF page count); journeys has two rewritten checks. Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/155_client_orders.sql` (after `set -a; source .env; set +a`), `docker compose up -d --build api`.
