## AUZsScan/AUZsLedger WhatsApp bill link: real invoice link + shorter URL (db/167)
Owner report: "when bill is sent to customer its only now a text message... put actual designed invoice bill link in it, make url as short as possible." The ONLINE/synced sale path (`scDone()` in `accounts/p-scan.js`) already builds and sends a real designed-invoice link via `shareLink()` -- unaffected. The OFFLINE sale receipt stays plain text by design (no real invoice number exists until it syncs, see "AUZsScan is offline-first" above) -- not a bug, not changed.
Two real things fixed: `shareLink()` in `app/public/accounts/p-docs.js` used bare `location.origin`, the exact same wrong-domain bug `printQR()` had (see the Mannat Cafe QR section) -- breaks when the app is opened via the shared `app.auzslab.in` address. Fixed with a `tenantOrigin()` helper added to `app/public/accounts/core.js` (reads `S.ctx.tenant.slug` from `acc_context()`'s own `tenant` object, mirrors console's `core.js` helper). `acc_share_document`'s `share_token` was 64 hex characters (two concatenated UUIDs) -- shortened to 32 (one UUID, already this project's own convention for the same purpose, `client_orders.token`). `public_acc_document`'s length guard loosened from `< 40` to `< 30` so already-sent 64-char links keep working; only new shares get the shorter token. The `accounts` suite's own share-link test asserted `tok.length >= 40` (written for the old length) -- updated to `>= 30`. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/167_shorter_share_token.sql`, `git pull` (no API rebuild).

## QR ordering was table-only: no takeaway path existed at all (`site.html`)
Owner report (Mannat Cafe): "not able to place order... not even takeaway order neither table QR scan order", then "there is no add to cart there." Root cause, found by reading the actual code, not guessed: `site.html`'s item cards only rendered Add/quantity buttons `if(tb)` -- `tb` is the table matched from the QR's own `?t=` id. **Visiting the site with no table (a general link, or "takeaway") showed prices with zero way to add anything to a cart** -- this was never built, not a regression. Separately, the button-gate used `tb` instead of the already-computed `selfOrderOn` flag, so if a table matched but the business had self-order switched off, Add buttons still rendered and a customer could fill a cart that `place_order` would then always reject ("self-order is currently turned off") -- explains "there's an Add button but placing the order doesn't work" independent of the takeaway gap.
Fixed: item cards and the floating cart bar now gate on `selfOrderOn` alone, not `tb` -- a takeaway visitor (no table) gets the same Add/cart/checkout flow, just labelled "Takeaway" instead of the table name, and `place_order` is called with `t=null` (the RPC already just stores whatever `t` is given as `guest_orders.tbl`, never required a real table). POS side: `acceptG()` in `pos/shell.js` used to always create a `Dine-in` order even for a tableless guest order; now creates a proper `Takeaway` order when `g.tbl` is empty, and the guest-order banner reads "Takeaway" instead of "Table" in that case.
**If a tenant still can't place any order after this ships, check `tenant_settings.features->>'self_order'` / `enabled_features->>'self_order'` for that tenant directly** -- the banner text ("please order at the counter") already told the truth; the bug was only that the buttons didn't honestly reflect it. Verified: `node tests/run-local.mjs cafe` (6/6) and `pos` (27/27) both clean. Deploy: static only, `git pull`.
Owner follow-up (Mannat Cafe, after this deployed): Add buttons now show (confirms the fix above is live), but tapping Add visibly does nothing on their real phone -- a real device/browser (not a plain desktop test) this session has no network access to reproduce. Could not rule out a real-device-only issue (CLAUDE.md's own standing lesson: "never claim an iPhone fix works from a desktop screenshot alone") such as the floating `.cartbar` review pill (`position:fixed;bottom:14px`) rendering behind that phone's own browser toolbar, invisible. Added a top-of-screen `toastMsg()` ("X added - N items in cart", `.toastmsg`, z-index 96, independent of the bottom cart bar) that fires on every successful add, so there is always a visible, hard-to-miss confirmation regardless of where the bottom bar ends up.
**The toast also never showed** -- ruling out a visibility-only problem. Added a page-wide `window.onerror` handler (first script in `<head>`) that `alert()`s the literal error and line number on any throw; the owner's next real tap surfaced `TypeError: undefined is not an object (evaluating 'i.price')` -- the actual root cause, confirmed and fixed, `window.onerror` handler then removed again (it was a diagnostic, not a permanent feature).

**Real root cause (db/168_backfill_missing_record_ids.sql): the Mannat Cafe real-menu import never stamped `id` into its own ITEM records' `data` jsonb.** Every reader of `records` (the POS's own `save()` in `pos/core.js`: `data.id = id` before every write; `site.html`'s cart; the console) assumes a record's `data` always carries its own id, since `public_menu()` and most other readers return `data` alone, never joined with the row's real `records.id`. db/165's category inserts correctly did `jsonb_build_object('id', cat_tea, ...)`, but every item insert right after it only built `jsonb_build_object('cat', cat_tea, 'name', ..., 'price', ..., 'sizes', ...)` -- `gen_random_uuid()` was passed straight as the `records.id` column value with no variable kept to also embed in `data`. Every imported item's client-side `.id` was therefore `undefined`, so `site.html`'s cart key (`i.id+'|'+sz`) collapsed EVERY item onto the identical broken string `"undefined|"` -- `priceOf()`'s `items.find(x=>x.id==k.slice(0,j))` then never matched (`undefined == 'undefined'` is `false` in JS), returning `i=undefined`, and the very next line's `i.price` threw. This broke Add-to-cart for literally every item on the menu, dine-in or takeaway, for the tenant's entire life -- not something introduced by this session's ordering-path fixes, just finally hit once those fixes made the Add button reachable at all for a takeaway visitor.
Fix is a general, safe, idempotent backfill (not Mannat-specific, since this is a reusable footgun for any one-off import migration): `update records set data = data || jsonb_build_object('id', id) where kind in ('item','cat','table') and (data->>'id') is distinct from id`. Verified against a scratch DB built from the full migration chain: found 34 real rows across the system missing a matching id (all Mannat Cafe's items, confirmed by tenant), fixed all 34, confirmed idempotent (`UPDATE 0` on a second run). **If this exact crash shape (`undefined is not an object`, cart/ordering breaking only for one specific tenant) ever recurs for a different business, suspect this same pattern first** -- check whether that tenant's menu was ever loaded via a hand-written SQL migration instead of the normal app UI, and whether that migration's `jsonb_build_object(...)` included `'id'`.

## AUZsScan's top-bar "Send now" never flushed the sale queue -- a real reason "no invoice link ever appears"
Owner report, after the "send the real invoice once an offline sale syncs" feature shipped: tapping "Send now" on the top offline-status bar did nothing, and offline sales never got a real invoice link. Root cause in `accounts/offline.js`'s `updateOffBar()`: its displayed count already combines the AUZsLedger draft queue AND AUZsScan's own sale queue (`(await draftQueue()).concat(window.sqItems ? sqItems() : [])`), but the "Send now" button action only ever called `flushDrafts()` -- never `sqFlush()`. A sale queued by "Charge" (not "Save draft") sits in the `sq` store only; tapping that top banner's "Send now" silently did nothing for it, forever, while the SEPARATE "On this phone, not yet in your books" card lower on the Scan page (`scPendingCard`, its own correct "Send now" that does call `sqFlush()`) was easy to miss sitting right next to an identically-worded, broken button. Since the sale never actually reached the books, it never got a real invoice number either, so the new "Ready to send: the real invoice link" card (previous section) never had anything to show -- not a bug in that feature, just starved of input by this one.
Fixed: the top bar's action is now `Promise.all([flushDrafts(), window.sqFlush ? sqFlush() : null])`, matching what the `online` event listener and the 60s interval already correctly did. Deploy: static only, `git pull` (bumped `offline.js?v=4`, `sw.js` `pos-v94`).

## AUZslab's contact number changed: +91 9251250829 (was +91 8005673683)
Owner instruction: remove the old number everywhere, update to the new one. This was hardcoded in far more places than `site/company.js`/`ds/help.js`'s `SUPPORT` object alone (that's the documented single source of truth from the "Bill stamp..." section above, but it's a last-updated-once value, not something every page reads live) -- 32 files total had `tel:+918005673683` links or copies of the number baked into static HTML, found with a repo-wide grep across three digit formats (`8005673683`, `91 8005673683`, `918005673683`) and fixed in one pass.
**One real near-miss worth remembering: the same digit string also appeared as part of a UPI payment VPA** (`site/orderterms.js`'s `upi.id: '8005673683-2@ybl'`, the id the payment QR pays to) -- a blind find-and-replace would have silently pointed every customer's payment QR at a different, likely-invalid UPI handle. Caught by reviewing every changed line before committing (not by the grep itself, which has no way to tell a phone number from a VPA built from one), reverted that one line and left a comment explaining why, and left the UPI id as a separate, explicit decision for the owner to make if they actually want it changed too -- changing a live payment destination is not something to infer from "update the contact number."
Deploy: static only, `git pull` (bumped `company.js?v=4`, `ds/help.js?v=2`/`tool/help.js?v=2`, `sw.js` `pos-v94`).

## AUZsScan: an expired session silently killed Send now forever, with zero indication why
Owner follow-up, still after the previous "Send now" fix: still no bill link, "Send now" still does nothing. Root cause, found by actually reading `sb-client.js`'s `request()`: a 401 on any call **clears the stored session immediately** (`session = null; saveSession(null)`) so it's never resent against a token that will only ever fail again -- correct in isolation, but `core.js`'s `api()` wraps that into an `Error` with `e.status = 401`, and `sqTransient(e)` (`scan-sync.js`) treats EVERY 401 as "transient, just retry later" alongside real network blips. Once the session dies, every retry -- the manual "Send now" tap, the 60s background interval, the `online` event -- hits the same dead token, gets the same 401, and silently does nothing, forever, since nothing ever told the person their session had expired and they needed to sign in again. `shell.js`'s own session check only ever runs once at boot (`boot()`'s `if (!session) return showLogin()`), never reactively, so a session dying mid-use left the app sitting on the Scan screen looking completely normal while every write silently failed underneath it.
Fixed: `api()` in `core.js` now calls `showLogin('Your session has expired. Please sign in again.')` the first time it sees a 401 (guarded by a `sessionExpiredShown` flag so it fires once, not on every background retry). Separately, both "Send now" buttons (the top offline-status bar in `offline.js`, and the "On this phone, not yet in your books" card's own button in `p-scan.js`) now toast an explicit message when a manual tap makes zero progress and nothing was refused either -- "still offline" or "still could not reach your books" -- instead of the previous total silence, which looked identical to the session-expiry case from the tester's side and made this whole bug much harder to tell apart from "it's just not working."
**If a report like this recurs ("Send now does nothing, no error, no bill link") after this ships, ask the person to try signing out and back in first** -- that's now the one thing this fix cannot auto-recover from (it bounces to the login screen but still needs a real re-auth with a working connection). Deploy: static only, `git pull` (bumped `core.js?v=8`, `offline.js?v=5`, `p-scan.js?v=10`, `sw.js` `pos-v95`).

## QR ordering / takeaway: pay-by-UPI QR for the customer's order (not a payment gateway)
Owner asked whether to build our own payment gateway (no -- that needs an RBI Payment Aggregator licence, PCI-DSS, direct bank/network certification, years and crores of net worth; explained this directly and steered toward what they actually meant). What they actually wanted: when a customer orders via `site.html` (table QR or takeaway) and picks UPI, show a QR already filled in with the exact amount, paying straight to that business's own UPI ID -- the same scan-and-pay a shop's counter sticker already does. No gateway, no licence, no server-side payment confirmation (same honesty as the existing cart.html/accounts UPI QR routes: a human checks the bank app before handing over the order).
- **Console**: Management -> (POS's "QR Ordering" page, `console/p-site.js` `PAGES['qr/ordering']`) gained a "Pay by UPI" card -- `upiId`/`upiName` fields, same generic `siteField()`/`saveSettings()` mechanism every other site-content field already uses (no DB migration: it's just two more keys in the tenant's existing `settings` record, which `public_menu()` already returns as `cfg` in full).
- **`site.html`**: now loads the vendored `/vendor/qrcode.js` (no CDN, same library already used by cart.html, the console's table-QR print and Accounting's pay-now QR). The checkout sheet shows a "Pay at counter" / "Pay by UPI" toggle only when `cfg.upiId` is set (invisible, zero behavior change, for every tenant that hasn't filled it in); picking UPI reveals a QR built the identical way `p-docs.js`'s `upiQr()` already does for invoices (`upi://pay?pa=<id>&pn=<name>&am=<exact total>&cu=INR&tn=<note>`). Placing the order still calls the same `place_order` RPC unchanged -- UPI is a note on the order ("Paid by UPI (self-reported, please verify)"), not a new order state, so staff just read it off the guest-order banner like any other note.
Deploy: static only, `git pull` (bumped `console/p-site.js?v=4`, `sw.js` `pos-v96`).

## Real regression caught self-directed: every takeaway order has been failing outright (db/169)
Found while investigating an unrelated question (whether to build a payment gateway), not reported by the owner -- worth fixing immediately rather than waiting to be asked. The earlier "QR site: add a takeaway ordering path" fix (PR #346) made `site.html` call `place_order(tenant_slug, t, ...)` with `t = null` for any visitor with no table (a general link, genuine takeaway). `guest_orders.tbl` has been `text not null` since the very first migration (db/002, written back when every guest order was assumed to be at a real table) -- `place_order`'s own `insert into guest_orders (tenant_id, tbl, ...) values (tid, t, ...)` therefore violated the not-null constraint on every single takeaway attempt, surfaced to the customer as the generic "Could not place the order. Please try again or call our staff." Confirmed by reproducing it directly against a scratch database (`ERROR: null value in column "tbl" of relation "guest_orders" violates not-null constraint`) before writing the fix, not guessed.
Fix (`db/169_guest_orders_nullable_table.sql`): `alter table guest_orders alter column tbl drop not null`. Verified against the same scratch DB: a takeaway order (`t=null`) now succeeds, a real table order (`t='tbl-1'`) still succeeds, `node tests/run-local.mjs cafe` (6/6) stays clean. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/169_guest_orders_nullable_table.sql` (no API rebuild). **This one needs the database migration run, not just `git pull`** -- the code fix shipped in PR #346 was always correct, only the schema was never updated to match it.

## SESSION LOG (2026-10-11/12, admin panel redesign + POS features + invoice template galleries): PRs #364-#376

A long session, mostly run as several parallel background agents (own git worktrees, own PRs, nothing merged without the owner's explicit go-ahead) once the work had enough independent, well-scoped pieces. All PRs below are squash-merged to `main`.

1. **Clarification-first protocol (PR #364).** The owner pasted a 10-rule discovery-first protocol and asked for it as a standing CLAUDE.md rule for every session/account -- see the section above this one ("Clarification-first protocol"). Note for next time: this PR sat open, unmerged, for most of the session despite an earlier summary claiming it was already merged -- always verify a PR's actual `merged`/`state` via the API rather than trusting a carried-over claim from earlier context.
2. **Admin panel (`site/admin.html`), two passes.**
   - First pass: nav regrouped into labelled sections (Requests to review / Team & sales / Money / Oversight / Tools), the Clients tab rebuilt as a dedicated `.client-card`/`.cc-*` component (bold name + status pill, big two-column renewal-days-left/fee stats, collapsed services count with a "View all" toggle, quiet text-link actions -- no bill buttons, those stay in Client payments & bills only), Services chips filtered to real top-level products (`PRODUCT_KEYS`: pos, self_order, payroll, accounting, mobile, scan, salon, website_builder) instead of every bundled sub-feature.
   - Second pass (owner: "make this kind of change across the complete admin panel... tight spacing, not big cards with empty space"): every remaining tab (Leads, customer profiles, Signup/Add-on requests, Client setup queue, Pricing, Users, Salesmen) rebuilt into new shared card components (`.req-card`, `.price-card`, `.list-card`) matching the same minimal language, all built compact from the start per the owner's spacing note. Client payments/bills, Audit log, Salesman work and Privacy requests were deliberately left as `.admin-table` (genuinely tabular/ledger data where row comparison matters) -- a judgement call, not an oversight.
3. **Homepage + products list.** Homepage drawer nav font matched to the client dashboard menu (21px/800 -> 16px/600), `.sidebar-foot`'s dead space below Sign up/Let's talk fixed (`margin-top:auto`), AUZslab Salon added to `products.html`'s slab list, Website Builder and Custom build removed entirely (pages deleted, every nav-dropdown/footer/sitemap cross-reference repo-wide updated or redirected -- `website_builder` stays a real, sellable feature key, only its dedicated marketing page is gone).
4. **POS staff/manager permissions + a staff-only Inventory view.** `console/p-menu.js`'s Order Preferences/Discounts/Taxes pages now disable every input for non-owner, matching Configuration's existing pattern (server-side `kind='settings'` was already owner-only; this closed a UI gap where a manager could type into fields that silently failed to save). New `app/public/pos/inventory.js` (`V.inventory`): a stock list with low/out-of-stock flags and a tap-to-adjust sheet, reachable by every staffer (gated on `can('inventory')`, not role) via a new "Inventory" row in Staff & settings -> Business tools -- **not** through the Admin console.
   - **Real bug found right after shipping, now fixed (PR #376):** tapping Inventory bounced straight back to the default tab (Tables). `render()` in `pos/shell.js` reset `S.tab` whenever it wasn't one of `navItems()`'s own entries (the visible tab bar/sidebar list) -- but `V.inventory` was deliberately never added there, since it's only reachable via the Business-tools link, not the main nav. Fixed by checking `!V[S.tab]` (does the view exist at all) instead of navItems() membership. If a future view is added that's reachable only through a side link rather than the tab bar, it will hit this exact same trap unless it's a real `V[...]` entry (which is the only thing the fixed guard checks).
5. **BOGO ("Buy X Get Y") offers (PR #370, `db/172_pos_offers.sql`).** New `kind='offer'` record (category-or-item scope, date window, buy-N-get-M, % off), added to `pos_kind_ok()`'s manager-or-owner allow-list. Billing math stays entirely inside `tot()` in `pos/core.js` (per this file's own standing rule) -- `offerActive()`/`offerMatches()` compute the match there and the result renders as its own visible line ("Offer: Buy N Get M -- name") on the cart, the printed receipt and the public invoice, never a silent price change. No stacking (one offer per line, item-scope beats category-scope). Verified against a real scratch database driving the actual `push_record`/`tot()` code paths, not just read.
6. **Louder, repeating alerts (PR #367).** `pos/shell.js`'s `alertSound()` gain raised to full and now **re-rings every 15s** while a new order sits unacknowledged, plus a mute toggle (a shop needs to be able to turn this off). Push notifications get `requireInteraction:true`. Honest findings, not new fixes: Web Push is opt-in and plausibly never turned on by any real shop; **iOS Safari cannot do web push at all unless the site was first Added to Home Screen** (a real Apple platform limit, not a bug here); Android native push (FCM, `db/119`) is already wired correctly server-side end to end, still never confirmed on a real device.
7. **Admin console: back button + Dashboard hierarchy (PR #368).** `topBar()` gained a real back control + breadcrumb (`history.back()`, falls back to Dashboard), using the existing hash router -- no NAV/routing changes, so none of the ~74 existing deep links moved. Dashboard: Today's Revenue given a clearly bigger `.big-rev` treatment than Total Orders/payment strip (`.big-sec`), and the two purely decorative zero-data banners ("Boost your menu visibility", the fake "AI Assistant" CTA -- CLAUDE.md's own Intelligence section already notes no real AI insight engine exists) removed outright. Every real-data card kept, just tighter padding.
8. **Invoice/receipt template galleries, one per app, owner: "I want all systems [to] have their own separate template design... so they can choose and change their invoice design anytime."**
   - **AUZsLedger** (PR #371, pre-existing `taxinv.js`/`invoiceStyle` mechanism extended): 5 new designs alongside `classic` -- `noir`, `playful`, `bold`, `minimal`, `stamped` (`accounts/invoice-templates.js`, `window.auzInvoiceTemplates`). Picked in Settings -> Organisation -> "Invoice layout", with a "Preview with sample data" button. Every template keeps the real GST fields (GSTIN, HSN, CGST/SGST/IGST, amount in words) -- only the skin changes. Business name is the dominant bold element in every one (including `classic`, which already satisfied this).
   - **AUZsScan** (PR #373): did **not** get a second/parallel storage -- it runs on the exact same `acc_org` books as AUZsLedger, so forking the setting would let one business's invoice disagree with itself across apps. Instead, the existing picker+preview code was extracted into one shared function and a new lightweight "Invoice layout" page was added to AUZsScan's own short nav (Settings group), reachable without needing to open AUZsLedger. Verified end to end against a scan-only tenant (no `accounting` entitlement).
   - **POS** (PR #374): a POS bill is a different, simpler document than an A4 GST tax invoice -- new `pos/bill-templates.js` (`window.auzBillTemplates`) with 5 designs (Ticket/thermal, Modern, Brand/colour-band, Ruled/letterhead, Soft) appropriate to that shape. `cfg().billStyle`, picked from Console -> Management -> Configuration -> "Look & invoice", wired through both the in-POS print path (`pos/print.js`) and the public `i.html` link; falls back to the pre-existing default design when unset.
   - **AUZsMob** (PR #375, `db/173_mob_receipt_templates.sql`): had **no styled receipt at all** before this -- sales only ever shared as plain WhatsApp text. Built from scratch: a real public receipt page (`app/public/mobbill.html` + `mob/receiptpage.js`), 5 templates (`mob/receipt-templates.js`: thermal/default, modern, bold, classic, stamped -- deliberately simpler than AUZsLedger's, no GST/HSN, since AUZsMob is GST-exempt), `features.receiptStyle` validated in `mob_save_settings` (same pattern as `db/105`'s `nav`), new `mob_share_sale`/`public_mob_sale` SECURITY DEFINER functions (token-only public read, mirroring `acc_share_document`/`public_acc_document`), picker in `mob/p-settings.js`, and `waShareSale` now sends the real styled link instead of only plain text.
   - Every template-gallery PR touched `sw.js`'s shared precache array, so each one hit the documented squash-merge-SHA-divergence conflict on merge -- resolved each time the same way (merge `origin/main`, take the union of both sides' array entries, keep whichever version number is newer when the same file appears on both sides, never just pick one side wholesale).

**The tenant-data cross-contamination bug** (reported mid-session: Mannat Cafe's tables appearing in the demo account) was investigated here far enough to locate the shared-origin `pos3` IndexedDB and a user-id-only cache-wipe guard as likely contributors, then explicitly paused by the owner before a fix was written. **A separate, concurrent session fixed it properly in the meantime** -- see "Another business's tables/areas showing up after signing in on a shared address" immediately below: the real root cause was narrower and more precise than the hypotheses noted here (`boot()` loaded the in-memory `R` from the previous login's stored copy and never cleared it on a login change, independent of the IndexedDB naming), with a real fix and two new pos-suite tests. No further action needed on this one.

**Deploy for this session's own work** (separate from the fix above, which has its own deploy note):
```bash
cd auzslabs-infra && git pull origin main && git log -1 --oneline
set -a; source .env; set +a
docker compose exec -T postgres psql -U "$POSTGRES_USER" -d "$POSTGRES_DB" -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/172_pos_offers.sql
docker compose exec -T postgres psql -U "$POSTGRES_USER" -d "$POSTGRES_DB" -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/173_mob_receipt_templates.sql
docker compose up -d --build api
```
(`db/172`/`db/173` are each safe to run even if one was already applied earlier -- both are written `create or replace`/idempotent. If one was already run, psql just reports no new objects.) Everything else is static files, live the moment `git pull` finishes.

## Another business's tables/areas showing up after signing in on a shared address; items silently missing from Sell (POS `shell.js`/`core.js`/`sell.js`)
Owner report: tables and areas edited in Mannat Cafe appeared in the demo account, and a Cappuccino in the menu was not in the Sell tab. The database was NOT leaking (RLS scopes every read to the login's business, covered by the security suite). The leak was the phone/browser: shared addresses (`app.`, `auzspos.auzslab.in`) keep ONE IndexedDB `pos3` per address, and `boot()` runs once before sign-in (it loaded the last business's saved records into the in-memory `R`), then again after sign-in. The second run emptied the stored copy when the login changed but never emptied `R`, so the previous business's tables, areas, items and ingredients stayed on screen next to the new business's own (and `localStorage.outlet`/`seeded` also carried over).
- Fix: `boot()` now empties `R` first, loads the local copy only for a signed-in login, and on a login change also removes `outlet`/`seeded` and the open order. A business's unsent changes are dropped when a different login signs in on the same browser (same as before for the stored copy). The console (`dash2`) and the other apps load their copy only after sign-in, so they were not affected in memory.
- Items: `itemOffWhy()` in `core.js` says why an item cannot be sold (sold out, outside its selling hours, hidden from this order type, or a recipe ingredient at zero stock; ingredients are matched by NAME, so a leaked/duplicate ingredient hid items). Sell now lists such items dimmed with the reason and a tap explains it, instead of hiding them. If an item is "missing" check its Availability fields and its recipe ingredients' stock first.
- Tests: pos suite (29): "A different login on the same address never sees the previous login's local records" and "Sell lists a sold-out item dimmed with the reason". Deploy: static only, `git pull`; each phone should reload once (sw `pos-v101`). Sentry's "Could not reach the server" from `auzspos.auzslab.in` in the same message was an offline phone, not a bug.

## KPI cards: one compact layout in every staff app (`ds/kpi.js` + `ds/kpi.css`)
Owner request: metric cards were full-width, one number each. Now every `.kpis` group in the POS, console, AUZsLedger/AUZsScan, AUZsPay and AUZsMob is laid out the same way, layout only (colours, fonts, radius, shadow, labels, data unchanged): the first KPI of a screen is the HERO (accent card, label + context left, number right at about 2x size, 34px phone / 40px desktop), the rest are compact tiles (3 columns, 2 when a value is longer than 9 characters, 8px gaps), and with more than 5 tiles the extras fold into a "More metrics" row. **One hero per screen:** a group only gets a hero if no `.k-hero` exists yet in the same `main/.content/#app`, and only with 3 or more KPIs (2 stay as tiles). Empty values show an em dash.
- `ds/kpi.js` exposes `auzKpi.hero/tile/grid` (components that build the markup) and `auzKpi.enhance`; a MutationObserver enhances every existing `.kpis` that the apps already build with their own `kpi()` helpers, so no screen is restyled one by one and new screens get it for free. `ds/kpi.css` handles each app's class names (`.l/.v/.s`, `.k/.n/.d`, POS `b/span`). Linked at the end of `<head>` in index, dashboard, accounts, payroll, mob, scan; precached by `sw.js` (`pos-v102`). Bump `?v=` there when editing.
- Hero choice is simply the first KPI the screen builds; to choose another, put it first. Not changed: the toolbar chips (already one scrolling row in the console), the hand-built console Home dashboard cards, the Back Office/marketing/admin pages.
- Tests: design 25/25, apps 22/22, payroll 33/33, mobile 52/52, pos 29/29.
- **KPI layout, second pass (owner: no empty cells, no cut-off text):** the tile grid is now 6 columns and `spans()` in `ds/kpi.js` gives each row 3 (or 2 when a value is longer than 9 characters, or when there are 4 tiles) equal tiles, with a short last row stretching to full width, so no empty cell is ever left. Labels, sub-lines and values wrap instead of being clamped or ellipsised (only the hero number stays on one line). Tile value is 20px/800, label 12px muted above it. Files at `?v=3`, sw `pos-v103`.

## Native AUZsPOS (Flutter, `native/auzspos/`), first of five native apps
Owner chose truly native apps, one at a time: AUZsPOS (+ QR guest orders) first, then AUZsPay, AUZsLedger, AUZsMob, AUZsScan. Same API and `records` data as the website POS (no server change). `lib/core`: `billing.dart` (port of `tot()`, parity tests from vectors generated by `native/auzspos/tool/make_vectors.mjs` out of the real web code: regenerate after any billing change), `api.dart`, `store.dart`/`sqlite_store.dart` (per-login db file), `repo.dart` (outbox + push_record + pull, mirrors `doSync()`), `session.dart`. UI in `lib/ui`: login, Sell, Orders, QR orders. APK is built only by the GitHub workflow `native-pos.yml` (no Android SDK in the sandbox); package `app.auzslab.pos`. Not built: tables map, kitchen, offers, approvals/PIN, printing, push, reports. Never confirmed on a real phone yet. Tests: `cd native/auzspos && flutter test` (18).

Native AUZsPOS rounds so far: round 1 = Tables floor, Kitchen screen, dine-in/table cleaning (`lib/ui/floor.dart`); round 2 = Buy-N-Get-M offers ported into `billing.dart` (parity vectors include offers), manager approval (`pos_sign_approval` / `pos_verify_pin`, `lib/ui/actions.dart`), discount, coupon, complimentary, customer and note, split bill, cancel order, paid-bill refund and cancel. Role/permissions come from `profiles`/`roles` (`Session.can`). Test builds are published by the workflow as the GitHub release `native-pos-latest` (file AUZsPOS.apk). Round 3 = WhatsApp bill link and PDF print (`lib/ui/receipt.dart`, system print dialog), Register with cash in/out and close (`lib/ui/more.dart`), today report, reservations (bookings table). Round 4 = Reports (today/7/30 days), Settings (owner), Attendance + approval PIN (`lib/ui/more2.dart`), in-app sound+vibration alert for new QR orders (polling every 20 s, only while the app is open; true background push needs a Firebase project + `google-services.json`, not built), launch animation (`lib/ui/launch.dart`). Still to build: direct Bluetooth ESC/POS printing, background push, Hindi, tablet layout. Test APKs are signed with the fixed key `android/app/test-release.jks` (Play will use its own secret key).

Native AUZsPOS round 5 (owner: "raw skeleton, want full web-like app"): **Sign in** now matches the website (`ui/login.dart`): tabs Log in / Sign up / Staff, Continue with Google, forgot password, 2-step code, show/hide password; sign-up = Google with the same consent boxes. Google runs in the phone's browser (Google blocks WebViews): `url_launcher` opens `https://auzslab.in/signin.html?app=pos&return=auzspos://signin`, `site/signin.html` now accepts the exact return `auzspos://signin` and hands back `#auz_gt=...`, the app catches it with `app_links` (Android intent filter on scheme `auzspos` host `signin`). A Google account with no business shows `ui/nobusiness.dart`. **Admin console inside the app** (`ui/admin.dart`, the 6th tab "Admin" for owner/manager; staff keep "More"): Dashboard, Menu (items + categories CRUD), Tables and QR (areas, bulk add, QR per table with `qr_flutter`), QR ordering and website (link, QR, Pay-by-UPI settings), Taxes, Stock, Customers, Expenses, Team (list; adding staff opens the website), plus Register/Reports/Reservations/Attendance/Settings tiles. **QR scan** (`ui/scan.dart`, camera via `mobile_scanner`, camera permission + minSdk 24): a table QR of this business starts a dine-in order on that table; other QRs offer Open/Copy. Shared `ui/kit.dart` (form sheet, Empty state, Stat). Deploy of the web half: `git pull` (signin.html). Not built: native Google sign-in dialog, push, Bluetooth printing, Hindi, tablet layout, sizes/add-ons editing in the app menu editor (kept as-is on edit), staff creation in-app.

Native AUZsPOS round 6 (owner: "UI and menu are totally different from the web POS"): the app was restyled to copy the website POS. Tokens in `ui/theme.dart` are the website's `ds/auz.css` values (warm off-white, white cards, hairlines, charcoal text; the accent is the business colour from settings; the app is forced to light). Building blocks in `ui/ds.dart` (`PageHead`, `Seg` iOS segmented control, `PillBtn`, `Cat` chips, `Group` + `Li` grouped list rows with tinted icon tiles, `NavBar` with sync status and bell). Navigation now matches the web phone tab bar: Tables, Sell, Orders, Kitchen, More (Tables only when the business has tables); QR guest orders appear at the top of Orders with a badge, not as a separate tab; owner/manager Admin console lives in More > Business tools. Sell has Dine-in/Takeaway/Delivery + Table pill, search, category chips with counts, photo cards with the reason an item cannot be sold (`Session.itemOffWhy`, port of the web function: `off`, `chOff`, recipe ingredient at zero). Order detail sheet matches the web (number + Done, status pills, created, items, totals, actions, Open order). Sign in copies the web card (Google, Email/Staff switch, Forgot password, Create an account link). Not matched yet: dark mode, tablet/wide layouts, item sizes/add-ons editor, the web's Appearance switch.

Native AUZsPOS round 7 (owner: "a separate admin console in the POS app like the web console, all features, exact UI, not a browser"): `ui/console.dart` is the in-app Admin console (More > Business tools > Admin console). Same structure as `console/core.js` NAV: hamburger drawer (rounded, groups expand: Inventory, Reports, Menu Management, CRM, Team, Finance, Marketing, Integrations, Management, Quick Links), top bar with menu / title / search (page finder) / bell (alerts: low stock, dues, open tickets), date chips + dark accent hero KPI + tiles on report pages. `ui/console_pages.dart` maps every menu id to a page: generic record CRUD (`CrudCfg`/`CrudPage`: raw materials, vendors, variants, add-ons, coupons, offers, gift cards, membership plans, tickets, tasks, cash movements, settlements, campaigns, segments, platforms, cancellation reasons, outlets, devices, custom links), Live/All/Online/Due orders, KOT, Purchases (adds stock), Recipes, Consumption, Reports (sales, orders, items, inventory, customers, staff, analytics, GST), and the earlier native pages embedded without their own app bar (`embedded: true`). Not in the app yet (the web console has them): WhatsApp/promotions/loyalty campaign senders, aggregator order entry per platform, ONDC/Dunzo/Uber Eats pages, audit log, logs, hardware, Explore apps, favourites/shortcuts, reconciliation, accounting links, bulk menu edit/import, item variants/add-ons assignment, per-outlet prices, role/permission editor, staff creation, payroll link, loyalty, feedback. Add them by extending `consolePageFor` and `consoleNav`.

Native AUZsPOS round 8: the missing console pages were added in `ui/console_more.dart`: Marketing (Campaigns with send, Segments, Offers, WhatsApp quick send, Promotions, Loyalty Campaigns; messages go one customer at a time through free `wa.me` links, `sendCenter()`), Integrations (Swiggy, Zomato, ONDC, Dunzo, Uber Eats pages with manual online-order entry and commission, Platform Settings), Quick Links (Explore Apps, Favourites, Shortcuts, Custom Links), Management (Hardware switches, Audit Trail from `pos_audit`, Logs), CRM (Loyalty rules and members, Feedback), Finance (Cash with day close, Reconciliation, Accounting profit and loss + GST), Team (Users & Roles with staff creation by username + PIN through `staff_create`, PIN reset, login on/off, role editor; Payroll link), Menu (Buy X Get Y, Availability switches, Bulk price change). Customer lists, tiers (Silver 3,000 / Gold 10,000) and segments are derived on the phone like the web console. Still web-only: bulk menu import/images, per-outlet prices, physical menu files, item variant and add-on assignment, data management page, the full Accounting and Payroll apps.

Native AUZsPOS round 9 (owner screenshots from an Android phone): the whole app is drawn at 0.84 scale on Android (`_Fit` in `native/auzspos/lib/main.dart`: lays out at size/0.84, scales with Transform, fixes MediaQuery padding/insets, ignores the system font scale); iOS is untouched. The sign-in logo and launch icon are re-rendered from the current web `logo-pos.svg` / `icon-pos.svg` (`assets/logo-pos.png`, `assets/icon.png`; re-render them if the web marks change). The launch animation (`ui/launch.dart`) is now 3.2 s, starts after the first frame, and follows the web one: mark rises, glow, name letter by letter, running bar, lift. Not confirmed on a real phone yet.

Native AUZsPOS entitlement gate (owner report: an AUZsMob-only business's staff PIN login opened the POS in the app): the website POS had `ds/gate.js`, the native app had NO check, so any login of any business opened the till. `Session.posAllowed` (set in `_loadRole` from `my_dashboard` features + the owner's switch, remembered per login in SharedPreferences for offline, unknown = allowed) now routes to `ui/notonplan.dart` ("AUZsPOS is not part of your plan", with buttons for the apps the business does own, plus Sign out). Still browser/app-side only: the generic `records` API itself does not check the `pos` entitlement (POS data is not in the `pos_*`/`mob_*` function tables), so a technical user calling the API directly with a staff token is not stopped; a server-side entitlement check on `records` writes is an open decision (it must not break salon/website-builder/other kinds that also use `records`).

## Server-side POS entitlement on `records` (db/174)
Closes the gap noted above: `records_entitled(kind)` + a RESTRICTIVE RLS policy `r_entitled` on `records` (read and write). A business without `features.pos` (or whose owner switched POS off) can no longer read or write POS data even by calling the API with a valid staff token; `website_builder` tenants keep `sitepage`; the `settings` record and `hr_*` records stay allowed for every app; a tenant with no entitlement row fails open. Verified: pos 29/29, mobile 52/52, and a direct SQL check as the `app` role (a testmob staffer sees 0 rows and cannot insert). If a legitimate product ever needs a new `records` kind without POS, add it to `records_entitled`. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/174_records_entitlement.sql` (no API rebuild).

Native AUZsPOS round 10 (owner: "complete the build"): **Bluetooth thermal printing** (`core/thermal.dart` ESC/POS bytes, `ui/printer.dart` picker in More > Receipt printer, 58 or 80 mm, saved per phone; "Print on thermal printer" button on every bill; packages `print_bluetooth_thermal`, `permission_handler`; manifest has the BLUETOOTH_* permissions; byte output is unit-tested, a real printer has NOT been tried). **Force-update screen** (`core/version.dart` `kBuild` + `site/native-version.json` `{pos:{min,latest,url,note}}`; raise `min` there to force old builds to update; bump `kBuild` with every release). **Item sizes and add-on groups** can be edited in the app menu editor (sizes as `Name = price` lines, add-on groups as chips), and Sell now opens the web-style option sheet (size, add-on groups with min/max, own extras, kitchen note); notes show in the cart and on kitchen tickets. **Tablets:** grids use max-extent columns and the 0.84 phone scale only applies below 600 dp. Still not built: dark mode, Hindi, Firebase push (needs `google-services.json`), Play signing/listing, bulk menu import/images, per-outlet prices, physical menu files, data-management page.

Native AUZsPOS round 11: **dark mode** and **Hindi**. Dark mode: `Tk` colours in `ui/theme.dart` are now getters that follow `Tk.dark` (More > This device > Appearance: Auto / Light / Dark, saved per phone, Auto follows the phone); the whole app is rebuilt (keyed on mode + language) when it changes. A hard-coded `Colors.white` surface must use `Tk.card` (QR codes stay white on purpose). **Hindi:** `ui/i18n.dart` provides `Lang` (More > This device > Language, also on the sign-in card) and a drop-in `Text` widget (every `ui/*.dart` file imports material with `hide Text` + `i18n.dart`) that looks fixed labels up in `ui/hi_map.dart` (about 510 labels: navigation, Sell, Orders, Tables, Kitchen, payment, More, reports, common admin forms; form labels go through `tr()`). Anything not in the map (long help paragraphs, text with names or numbers in it) stays English. New screens: add the English text to `hi_map.dart` with its Hindi, and use `Text`, not `m.Text`. Build number is 3 (`site/native-version.json` latest 3). Neither dark mode nor Hindi has been seen on a real phone; Hindi wording is mine and should be reviewed by a Hindi speaker.

Native AUZsPOS alerts fix (owner: "alert sounds not working when an order arrives"): the old alert used `SystemSound.play(alert)`, which Android plays as nothing or a tiny click. `core/alerts.dart` now plays a real 2-tone beep (`assets/alert.wav`, 3 repeats, `audioplayers`), vibrates, and posts a high-importance notification (`flutter_local_notifications`, channel `orders`, Android 13 notification permission requested, core-library desugaring enabled in `android/app/build.gradle.kts`). QR orders / waiter calls are polled every 10 s. More > This device > "Test the alert sound" plays it on demand. **Limit, said plainly:** polling runs only while Android keeps the app alive; with the app swiped away or the phone dozing, only push (Firebase `google-services.json`, FCM sender already exists server-side in `server/src/fcm.js`) can wake it, and the native app does not register for push yet. Build number 4.

## Native AUZsMob (Flutter, `native/auzsmob/`), second native app
Owner: "build complete auzsmob native app". Native equivalent of the web AUZsMob (mobile-phone shop: sell, purchase, repairs, stock, dues, reports, settings), same server functions, no server change. Package `app.auzslab.mob`, deep link `auzsmob://signin` (Google sign-in goes through `site/signin.html`, which now accepts `auzsmob://signin` as well as `auzspos://signin`), test APK from `.github/workflows/native-mob.yml` (release `native-mob-latest`, file AUZsMob.apk), signed with the same fixed test key as AUZsPOS. Force-update: `site/native-version.json` now has `pos` and `mob` entries (`kBuild` in `lib/core/version.dart`, build 1).
- **Foundations copied from AUZsPOS** (kept as separate copies, like the web apps): `core/api.dart` (+`syncBatch` for `POST /sync`), `ui/theme.dart` (Tk tokens, dark mode), `ds.dart` widgets, `i18n.dart` (Hindi/English through `tr()`/`trv()`), login (Email / Staff username+PIN / Google), launch animation, update screen. `ui/hi_map.dart` is GENERATED from the website's `app/public/mob/i18n.js` plus the POS labels (`/tmp/gen_hi.js` style: en text -> hi text); regenerate it when the website's AUZsMob texts change.
- **Data layer = the website's sync.js ported** (`core/store.dart`, `core/repo.dart`): per-login sqflite file with ten document stores (items, units, vendors, customers, purchases, sales, repairs, repairEvents, payments, stockMovements), an outbox, and `mob_sync_pull` with a cursor. Same rules: writes show at once ("saved on phone"), are idempotent on a phone-made id, go in one `POST /sync` batch when there are several; a temporary failure (no answer, 5xx, 408, 429) NEVER drops a queued write (retry with back-off 5 s to 5 min, tappable "Not synced" pill); only a real 4xx refusal is rolled back off the phone (`rejectLocal`: sale, purchase, repair event) and shown as "Not saved: ...". `autoSync=false` exists for tests.
- **Session** (`core/session.dart`): `my_dashboard` (role, tenant, features) + `mob_context` (perms, settings, features, rates) are cached on the phone so the app opens offline; `mobAllowed` (feature `mobile`, owner switch) gates the app (`ui/notmob.dart`); menu rules (`allNav`, `tabs`, `moreList`, owner's custom `features.nav`, Settings always reachable, My sales always kept for staff) are the web `shell.js` rules; a different `mob_data_epoch` (owner cleared all data) wipes the phone copy; the language chosen at sign-in is carried in until a real preference is saved.
- **Screens** (`ui/`): shell (bottom bar + More sheet, sync pill, refusal toasts), Home (4 big actions, my work today, owner dashboard), Sell (price asked on every line, cost override for people who may see rates, optional vendor with add-on-the-spot, IMEI search + camera scan, "New: ..." quick-add with an opening purchase, simple-repair service line, customer, discount, cash/UPI/card/credit, sale history with bulk void, WhatsApp bill + styled receipt link via `mob_share_sale`, print/PDF), Purchase (several items per bill, new item/vendor on the spot, serial/IMEI, used-phone buy, history), Repairs (job cards with status, parts, payments), Stock (catalog/units/vendors, add/edit), Dues (customer udhaar settle, vendor FIFO breakdown), Reports (dashboard, staff ledger cards, staff updates, period picker, profit sharing, CSV share), My sales, Settings (language, appearance, shop details, feature switches + presets, profit sharing, menu buttons, receipt style, staff rates switch, staff list, health check, backup/clear all data).
- **Tests** (`cd native/auzsmob && flutter test`, 29): money format, stock, vendor FIFO, periods, profit sharing, CSV; sync rules with a scripted API (temporary failure keeps the queue, refusal rolls back, batch, pull); session menu/permissions/offline open/data-epoch wipe; widget tests that open every tab on a phone-sized screen (they found three real overflows: Home tiles, More sheet, top bar), sell a case end to end into the outbox, Hindi pass, repair ticket, purchase with a new item.
- **Not built / unverified:** nothing has been run on a real phone; camera scanning, share sheet, printing and the Google redirect are only wired, not tried. Not in the app yet: day close with cash count, customers list page, simple-repair part purchase, receipt template preview, Bluetooth thermal printing, push alerts. Deploy of the web half: `git pull` (signin.html, native-version.json).
- **Fixed in AUZsPOS on the way (build 5):** `Tk.bar` in `ui/theme.dart` returned itself (infinite recursion, introduced by the dark-mode round) and would have crashed the top bar; it is the light off-white / dark charcoal pair now.

## Native AUZsScan, AUZsPay and AUZsLedger (Flutter, `native/auzsscan|auzspay|auzsledger/`), apps 3-5 of 5
Owner: "Build native app for rest of apps". Same recipe as AUZsMob: separate copies of the foundations (api, store, theme, ds, i18n, mk, launch, login with Google via `site/signin.html` deep link `auzs<x>://signin`, update screen), no server change, force-update through `site/native-version.json` (`scan`, `pay`, `ledger`), test APK per app from `.github/workflows/native-<x>.yml` (releases `native-scan-latest`, `native-pay-latest`, `native-ledger-latest`, files AUZsScan.apk / AUZsPay.apk / AUZsLedger.apk, same fixed test key, `git add -f` the `.jks`). Never run on a real phone: camera, share sheet, printing, Google redirect and Hindi wording are unverified.
- **AUZsScan** (`app.auzslab.scan`): Scan to sell / Add stock with the camera (`mobile_scanner`), typed or Bluetooth codes, search, new product on the spot, per-line price/discount/GST for this bill, customer name+phone, cash/UPI/bank/credit, offline-first queue (`acc_offline_apply`, idempotent op ids, provisional `OFF-xxx-n` numbers, "Ready to send: the real invoice link" card after sync, refused entries stay with Retry/Discard), Products (stock, edit, adjust), Sales list with WhatsApp link. Allowed with feature `scan` OR `accounting`. 17 tests.
- **AUZsPay** (`app.auzslab.pay`, feature `payroll`): employee side = Today with the big Clock in/out button (geofence via `geolocator` only when the org has a site), the ONE offline exception (queue in meta `punchq`, sent in order through `pay_me_punch_offline`, kept on any no-answer/5xx/401/408/429, a real 4xx is dropped and shown), My time (month, fix a day = `pay_me_regularize`), My leave (apply/cancel), My pay (payslips via `pay_get_item`, advance, expense claim without photo), Profile (details, bank change request); HR side (when `pay_bootstrap.hr`) = HR today (`pay_dashboard`), Approvals (leave, correction, loan, claim, bank), People (+ person, payslips), Payroll runs read-only, My team for line managers. Not built: running/finalising payroll, adding people, kiosk, documents, tax sheet, claim receipt photo, attendance grid, reports. Hindi labels in `ui/hi_pay.dart` (mine, to review). 6 tests.
- **AUZsLedger** (`app.auzslab.ledger`, feature `accounting` only): Home (`acc_dashboard`), Sell (the AUZsScan screen), Documents (sales / purchases / expenses lists, WhatsApp link for sales), Products, More > Customers and suppliers (`acc_save_party`), New purchase bill / expense (`acc_save_document` type bill/expense, `post:true`, pay later/cash/bank; needs a connection, no offline draft). Everything else (reports, banking, GST returns, journals, receipts and payments, credit notes, import, settings) stays on the website. 19 tests; the bill-posting happy path is NOT covered by a widget test (only that the screen opens and refuses an empty bill).
- Bug fixed in AUZsScan on the way: `refreshParties` called `acc_list_parties` without the `{p:{...}}` wrapper (silently used defaults). The website's `core.js` still does the same for its parties list (left alone).
- Owner install: Releases page of the repo, `native-<x>-latest`, open the .apk on the phone.

## Play Store launch notes (Oct 2026, nothing submitted yet)
- **Account type decides the path.** Play Console asks for a **D-U-N-S number** for an Organisation account (a GSTIN is not accepted). The owner applied to D&B (free route linked from Google's Play D-U-N-S help page, NOT the dnb.com customer service centre); business name and address must match the GST certificate exactly. Without the number: a **Personal** account works but needs a closed test with 12+ testers for 14 days before production, shows the owner's name/address publicly, and apps can later be transferred to an organisation account (check Google's current transfer rules first). Decision A (wait) vs B (personal now) is the owner's; not yet made.
- **The native apps only build test APKs** (`flutter build apk`, shared test key `android/app/test-release.jks`). For Play each workflow needs a signed `.aab` (`flutter build appbundle`) with the owner's own upload key as GitHub secrets, plus a version code that rises every release (`pubspec.yaml` `version: x.y.z+N`). Not built yet. An `.aab` is the same code, screens and features as the APK; only the signing key (a phone with the test APK must uninstall it first, local data is lost), version code and download size differ.
- **Launch one app first** (AUZsPOS) to avoid Play's near-identical-listing spam flags. Listing text and assets: `docs/STORE_LISTING.md`, `store/`. Reviewer needs a working demo login (`demo@auzslab.in` / `Auzslab@Demo`, email + password: Google sign-in is hidden inside WebView apps and opens the browser in the native apps); declare camera, location (AUZsPay clock-in), notifications and Bluetooth permissions; account deletion link `auzslab.in/account.html`.
- Firebase `google-services.json` is still needed for closed-app alerts (native push is not built).
- Release links (test APKs, GitHub pre-releases): `native-pos-latest`, `native-mob-latest`, `native-scan-latest`, `native-pay-latest`, `native-ledger-latest`. All five builds succeeded on CI as of this note (AUZsScan re-ran after a small fix). Branch `native-pos-release` holds the native work and is not merged to `main`.

## AUZslab social links
Footer "Connect" column (and the contact page) on every `site/*.html` lists Instagram `https://www.instagram.com/auzslab.in` and Facebook `https://www.facebook.com/share/1FMd3FoFyZ/?mibextid=wwXIfr`. Change them with a script across all 25 pages. Per-client Instagram fields (salon, Chapter One, console) are separate and untouched.

## Play `.aab` builds
All five `native-*.yml` workflows also build `flutter build appbundle` and publish `AUZs<X>.aab` next to the APK. They sign with the test key unless the secrets `ANDROID_UPLOAD_KEYSTORE_B64`, `..._STORE_PASSWORD`, `..._KEY_ALIAS`, `..._KEY_PASSWORD` exist (then the owner's upload key is used). Do not upload test-key AABs to Play.

## Native apps: gaps closed (Oct 2026, PRs #383-#384 and the follow-up branch)
- **Print fixed in all four apps that print** (`ui/receipt.dart` in POS, Mob, Ledger, Scan): the PDF used an infinitely tall page (`PdfPageFormat(..., double.infinity)`), which the pdf package may refuse; it is now a 297 mm `MultiPage` that flows onto more pages, the built-in PDF font has no rupee or multiplication sign so `_pt()` prints "Rs" and "x", and a failed print dialog throws a readable error. Not seen on a real phone.
- **AUZsPOS:** multi-outlet (`Repo.outlet`, outlet picker, pinned outlet from `profiles.outlet_id`, per-outlet item price/off), Website & Booking page, Physical menu / Bulk photos (gallery-only `image_picker`, upload `POST /storage/site/:prefix`), menu CSV import, My data export, **push registration** (`core/push.dart`, `PushRegistration.register` after sign-in; does nothing without a Firebase file; the Gradle plugin is applied only if `android/app/google-services.json` exists, and the workflow writes it from the base64 secret `GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON`). The push path and the Gradle plugin with AGP 9 are untested until a real Firebase file exists. Only POS registers: the server sends push for guest orders only. Web `push_subs` is untouched.
- **AUZsMob:** Customers page, Day close with cash count (`DayCash`), Bluetooth thermal printing (`core/thermal.dart`), receipt style preview (web `mobbill.html?sample=1`).
- **AUZsPay:** run payroll (create, calculate, submit, approve, send back, finalise, lock, cancel, bank batch, hold; `ui/p_run.dart`), Add person (`p_addperson.dart`), receipt photo on expense claims (`ApiClient.uploadDoc` -> `/storage/doc?empId=`, the path goes in `pay_me_claim.attachment`), Reports (`pay_report` generic viewer, CSV share) and the monthly Attendance grid (`pay_attendance_grid`) in `ui/p_reports.dart`. Still web-only: kiosk, employee documents, tax sheet.
- **AUZsLedger:** Reports page (`ui/p_reports.dart`: P&L, balance sheet, trial balance, GST summary, receivables, payables, cash flow; Indian financial year periods in `reportPeriod`; CSV share). Still web-only: banking, GST returns filing helpers, journals, receipts and payments, credit notes, import, settings. AUZsScan had no gaps found beyond the shared items.
- **Hindi:** about 70 new labels were added to every app's `hi_map.dart` / `hi_pay.dart`; the wording is mine and needs a Hindi speaker's review. Duplicate keys in a const map are a compile error, so append only keys that are absent.
- **Versions:** POS build 6, the other four build 2 (`version.dart` kBuild, pubspec, `site/native-version.json`).
- **Not verified on a real phone:** printing, camera, share sheet, Google redirect, offline sync, push, Hindi. Launch steps remain the owner's list (Firebase file, Play account, upload key and secrets, store listing).

## Native apps: second gaps round (Oct 2026): AUZsPay kiosk/documents/tax, AUZsLedger books, targeted push (db/175)
- **AUZsPay** (`ui/p_extra.dart`): Clock in kiosk (`pay_kiosk_list` / `pay_kiosk_punch`, PIN sheet; needs `pay_time`; unlike the web kiosk there is no password to leave it), My documents (`pay_me_documents`, files opened by downloading `GET /storage/doc/<path>` to a temp file and handing it to the share sheet), HR can add a document to a person from the gallery (`uploadDoc` then `pay_add_document`) and see/edit a person's income-tax declaration, and My income tax (`pay_me_tax` / `pay_me_tax_save`, `taxPayload()` in `calc.dart`). Employee documents from a camera/PDF are not supported yet (gallery photos only). Still web-only: asset/company items, announcements, bulk import, statutory settings.
- **AUZsLedger** (`ui/p_books.dart`, entries in the More sheet by permission): Receipts and payments (`acc_save_payment`, oldest-first allocation `allocateOldestFirst`), Credit note (`acc_get_document` then `acc_save_document` type `credit_note` with `creditLines`; convert is NOT possible for credit notes), Banking (`file_picker` CSV import with `guessBankColumns`/`bankRows`, match, record from a row, ignore, reconcile), Journals and transfers (`acc_save_voucher` journal/contra, day book), GST returns (summary, the eight registers with CSV share, exceptions, mark filed), Import (customers, suppliers, products, opening stock, party balances from CSV: `guessImportColumns`, dry run then import with `p_strict:false`), Business settings (`acc_save_org`, invoice layout). `file_picker` is a new dependency (the plugin build on CI is unverified). Excel files must be saved as CSV first; no bank-format guesses beyond common header names. Still web-only: fixed assets, budgets, recurring documents, e-invoice/e-way, stock transfers, audit log.
- **Targeted push (db/175, `server/src/push.js`)**: `notify_push_users(tenant, user_ids[], title, body)` puts `user_ids` in the `push_events` envelope; `handlePushEvent` then sends only to those users' `fcm_tokens` (browser Web Push is tenant-wide and skipped). Triggers on `pay_leave_requests`, `pay_claims`, `pay_loans`, `pay_regularizations`: a new request notifies the owner and managers, an approval/decline/payment notifies the employee. Triggers swallow errors so an alert can never block a request. Verified on a scratch DB with LISTEN (both notifications produced). **AUZsPay now registers its phone like AUZsPOS** (`core/push.dart`, same dormant-without-`google-services.json` behaviour; workflow `native-pay.yml` writes the file from the `GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON` secret). AUZsMob, AUZsScan and AUZsLedger do not register and nothing in the server notifies them yet.
- **Deploy:** `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/175_targeted_push.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (push.js changed). Versions: AUZsLedger build 3, AUZsPay build 3 (POS 6, Mob 2, Scan 2).
## AUZslab team: roles, leads, client setups, tracking (db/176, `salesman.html`, admin -> Team)
Owner request: hire staff beyond salesmen (client setup, support, managers), track their work and lead updates, redesign the salesman login page. `platform_salesmen` is now the team table (`role`: `salesman | setup | support | manager`, `phone`).
- **Who may do what (server-side, SECURITY DEFINER):** `is_salesman()` = role salesman only (the old trial/branding functions keep working only for salesmen); `is_team_member()`, `team_role()`, `team_can_manage()` (platform admin or manager). Salesman: add/update own leads, create trials. Support: work assigned leads. Setup staff: only the setups assigned to them. Manager: sees everyone and every lead, assigns leads and setups, never money, pricing or deletes. Platform admin adds people (`admin_add_team_member`, `admin_set_team_role`; `admin_add_salesman` is a wrapper).
- **Leads** (`leads` gained `assigned_to, created_by, next_followup, source, updated_at`; stages new / contacted / demo / quoted / converted / declined; `lead_notes` is the history). RPCs: `team_my_leads, team_all_leads, team_add_lead(p jsonb), team_update_lead(p_id, p jsonb), team_lead_notes, team_assign_lead`. Website contact-form leads land unassigned in admin -> Leads, where the admin or a manager assigns them.
- **Client setups** (`setup_assignments`: checklist of 6 steps, access expiry 14 days by default, one open assignment per business): `team_assign_setup(p_tenant, p_user, p_days)`, `team_my_setups, team_list_setups, team_setup_update, team_setup_cancel`. "Open their business" goes through `/admin/salesman-open-pos` -> `team_setup_open_context`, which mints a **limited session** (`signToken(..., {limited:true})`: 8 hours, JWT claim `lim:1`; `/auth/refresh` answers 403 for it) and logs `setup_open`. Access ends when the assignment expires, is finished or cancelled.
- **Tracking:** `team_activity` is an append-only feed (lead_added/stage/note/followup/assigned, trial_created via a trigger on `tenants`, setup_assigned/open/checklist/done). `team_overview(p_days)` (admin/manager), `team_activity_feed`, `team_my_work`, `team_clients`.
- **Apps:** `site/admin.html` "Team" section replaces "Salesmen" (add member, role, turn off/on, stats, recent work, client setups block); Leads now show assignee / stage / follow-up / history. The assignee list loads only when a select is opened (the "admin sign-in makes few requests" journeys check caps requests at 9). `app/public/salesman.html` is rewritten as the team workspace on the `ds/auz.css` tokens with a bottom tab bar per role (salesman Home/Leads/Trials/Me, setup Home/Setups/Me, support Home/Leads/Me, manager Home/Leads/Setups/Team/Me); the old trial and branding code lives on the Trials tab. `start.js` probes `team_me` (was `salesman_my_trials`) before the "no business" screen. New RPCs are all registered in `server/src/index.js`.
- Test: `node tests/run-local.mjs team` (11 checks). Deploy: `git pull origin main`, `set -a; source .env; set +a`, `docker compose exec -T postgres psql -U "$POSTGRES_USER" -d "$POSTGRES_DB" -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/176_team_staff.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`. If PR #385 (native apps) also adds a db/175, run 175 first.
- Not built: pay/commission tracking for team members, push alerts for follow-ups, a salesman self-service lead import.

## Backend split: `server/src/index.js` is now small (map in `docs/BACKEND_MAP.md`)
Pure move, no behaviour change. `index.js` only identifies the caller and offers the request to route modules in order (`routes/system.js`, `auth.js`, `payments.js`, `data.js`, `storage.js`, `admin.js`; each exports `handle(ctx)` and returns `false` when none of its addresses matched). Shared pieces: `config.js` (PORT/DOMAIN/SITE_URL/`safeResetBase`/demo logins), `lib/http.js` (`HttpError`, rate limit, CORS, body readers, `httpFromError`, `send`), `lib/guards.js`, `data/tables.js` (generic `/db` whitelist), **`data/rpc-registry.js` (the `RPC` list: every new database function the apps call must be added HERE; wherever this file says "register it in `server/src/index.js`'s `RPC`" it now means this file)**, `data/queries.js`. Check after editing: `eslint` with `no-undef` over `server/src` (a missing import only fails when that address is called; the first attempt of this split missed `origin` in the route context and broke the order-proof download until the tests caught it). Test baseline at the split: security 16, signup 13, team 11, journeys 14, salon 27, cafe 6, mobile 52, payroll 33, scan 27, offline 20, dpdp 11 all pass; `pos` (1 network-abort flake) and `orders` (1 admin-screen timeout) fail identically on the code before the split. Deploy: `docker compose up -d --build api`, nothing else.

## Join the AUZslab team: public application, admin approval (db/177)
`site/join-team.html` (linked as "Join our team" in the Company column of the footer on every marketing page, plus `sitemap.html` / `sitemap.xml`) sends `submit_team_application` (public, honeypot, 3 per email per day). Rows live in `team_applications` (RLS on, no policies). Admin -> Team -> **Applications**: Approve with a role (`admin_decide_team_application`) or Reject. If the applicant already has an AUZslab login with a verified email they join the team at once; otherwise `team_me()` (db/177 redefines it) turns the approval into a seat the first time they sign in with Google using the same email, and `start.js` then opens their workspace. A platform-admin account is never turned into a team member. **Finished (db/178):** the decision returns the applicant's email/name/role and `server/src/routes/data.js` emails them (`sendTeamDecisionEmail`: approved = sign in with Google at app.auzslab.in using the same email, rejected = polite note), emails a receipt to the applicant and a notice to `ADMIN_NOTIFY_EMAIL` (default helloauzslab@gmail.com) on a new application (all quiet no-ops without `RESEND_API_KEY`; per-IP limit 5/h on the form); an application can be decided only once; the Team menu shows a badge of waiting applications. The badge comes from `admin_open_counts()` (privacy + team in ONE request) because the journeys suite caps the admin sign-in at 9 requests: add future badges there, not as new calls. Test: team suite (12). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/177_team_applications.sql` (after 176), `docker compose up -d --build api` (RPCs registered), `git pull`.

Where earlier sections say "`salon.js` ..." read the matching file in that folder (`mutate`/`loadSite`/caches = `storage.js`, `admin()`/`member()` guards and every address = `router.js`). ## Table QR: dialog first, print that works, and the same page in the app
Owner report (iPhone console): the QR button on Tables & Areas did nothing and "Print all QR" printed nothing. Cause: both called `window.print()` straight away, which does nothing in an iOS home-screen app (and the single QR never showed on screen). `printQR(ts)` in `console/p-menu.js` now opens a dialog that SHOWS the code(s) (`qrCardNode`) with **Copy link**, **Share / Save image** (`qrShare`: a PNG per table made on a canvas, shared through the phone's share sheet, or downloaded) and **Print** (`qrPrint`, prints in place; inside an iOS home-screen app it says printing is not available and points at Share / Save image). Console CSS `?v=6`, `p-menu.js?v=8`. Test: apps suite (22; QR dialog, Print all QR, PNG made).
Native AUZsPOS (build 6): `TablesAdmin` and `QrWebsitePage` in `ui/admin.dart` were restyled to match the web console (big title + sub, Print all QR / Add several / Add table buttons, search with "N tables · N areas", one rounded card per area with Rename area / Add table here and a row per table with QR / Edit; QR Ordering page as cards). The old QR sheet is replaced by `showQrSheet` (shows the codes, Copy link, Share link, **Print or save PDF** through `printQrCodes` in `ui/receipt.dart`, an A4 sheet of QR cards via `QrPainter` -> PDF image). **Not compiled or run here (no Flutter SDK in the sandbox): the GitHub build is the first real check.**

`server/src/auth.js` is now only `export { ... } from './auth/<module>.js'` lines, so no importer changed; the 805 lines were moved unchanged into `auth/config.js`, `sessions.js`, `twofa.js`, `password.js`, `email.js`, `identity.js`, `staffpin.js` (map in `docs/BACKEND_MAP.md`). Owner request: a web-app icon for the salesmen / team login, a floating nav like the admin panel's, and some visual depth. `team` is a new mark in `tools/make-icons.mjs` (three figures, ink tile, wine) that generates `icon-team.svg|-180.png|-512.png` and the launch animation entry (`app/public/ds/launch.js`, `data-app="team"`, now linked as `?v=3`); `manifest-team.json` (id/scope `/salesman.html`), apple-touch icon and iOS web-app meta tags are in the page head. ## `team_probe()`: asking "am I on the team?" without an HTTP 400 (db/179)
`team_me()` raises `not a team member` for everyone who is not on the team, which showed up as an `HTTP 400` in the browser every time an ordinary customer signed in on the website or the hub (and failed the dpdp suite's "no script errors" check). `team_probe()` (db/179, registered in `server/src/data/rpc-registry.js`) wraps it and answers `{member:false}` instead; `site/signup.html` `goTeam()` and `app/public/start.js` use it (start.js `?v=5`). `salesman.html` still calls `team_me()` itself (it wants the error to show "not on the team yet"). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/179_team_probe.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (new RPC), `git pull`.

## The hub app (phase 1): one sign-in that routes by role (`app/public/start.html` + `start.js`, db/180)
Owner request: ONE main app where a client signs in, creates an account, adds purchases and sees the marketing pages, where a team member and the AUZslab admin also sign in and reach their own dashboards, plus separate apps per product (those already exist). Built on the existing hub `app.auzslab.in/start.html`; the marketing site, `account.html` and `admin.html` stay on `https://auzslab.in` EXACTLY as they are (owner: "exact frontend ... as it is").
- **`hub_me()`** (db/180, registered in `server/src/data/rpc-registry.js`) answers `{email, admin, team, business}` in one call, never an HTTP 400 (admin = `platform_admins`, team = role from `team_me()`, business = has a `profiles` row). `start.js` `boot()` routes: admin -> "AUZslab admin" tiles (Admin panel + explore); team member without a business -> `/salesman.html`; no business -> "Welcome" tiles (My account and purchases, My cart, Explore AUZslab, Products and pricing); business owner/staff -> their app tiles as before, PLUS My account and purchases + Explore, and a "Team workspace" tile if they are also on the team (a single-app user is still sent straight into the app).
- **Hand-over to the site:** the tiles that open `https://auzslab.in/...` build the URL at click time with `#auz_gt=<base64url {t,u}>` (same fragment hand-off as Google sign-in; `site/sb-client.js` and `app/public/sb-client.js` both take it), so the person arrives already signed in. Browsers never send the fragment to a server, and Playwright's `request.url()` hides it: tests check `page.url()` after letting the route fulfil, not the request.
- Test: team suite check "The hub sends each kind of person to the right place" (customer, admin, pure team member, team + business, business owner). Not yet done: the in-app shell (no bounce, back-to-hub pill), iOS shell, Windows/Mac desktop apps (see the next section when it exists). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/180_hub_me.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (new RPC), `git pull`.

## Hub app shells: Android, iOS, Windows, Mac (branch hub-app, db/180) -- see `docs/HUB_APP_RELEASE.md`
- `hub_me()` (db/180, registered) is the single role probe the hub (`start.js`) uses: admin / team / business / customer, so no HTTP 400 noise. Customers and admins get their own home tiles; marketing site, account and admin open on `auzslab.in` with the session handed over in `#auz_gt`.
- `ds/appshell.js` (identical copy `site/appshell.js`, keep in sync with `cp`) runs inside wrapped apps (UA `AUZslabApp` / `AUZslabDesktop`, or `?inapp=1|ios` in tests): app-feel CSS (no bounce/pull-to-refresh), hub pill, `takeLink()` for the sign-in return, and on iOS hides buying. Loaded from `site/theme.js`, `site/site.js` copies and the hub/team pages.

- `desktop/` (Electron), `mobile/build-ios.mjs`, workflows `desktop.yml` / `ios.yml`. Not run on real devices. iOS: no buying, Sign in with Apple still to build.
- Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/180_hub_me.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`.

## Hub "My requests" (`app/public/start.js`, no migration)
Signed-in people (customer, business owner) get a **My requests** tile in the hub: each request from `my_client_orders` with a 5-step bar (Asked, Checked, Pay, Confirmed, Active), a plain status line, **Read quote and pay** (opens `https://auzslab.in/pay.html?t=<token>` in the same window) when the status is `link_sent`, **View bill** and **Open my apps** when paid, **Ask for more products** (cart), Refresh and a 30 s auto-refresh. **iPhone/iPad app (`html.ios-app`): no pay or cart links**, only "open auzslab.in in your browser" (Apple's rules; a bill may still be viewed). Payment is still confirmed by the admin; automatic confirmation needs the Cashfree (or Razorpay) keys and a webhook that marks `client_orders` paid and runs the same access/bill step as `admin_confirm_order_payment`: not built. Test: team suite ("Hub My requests"). Deploy: static only, `git pull` (hub `start.js?v=8`).

## Hub opening animation + option tiles (`app/public/ds/launch-hub.js`, `start.html`)
The hub has its own hand-written opening (the per-app `ds/launch.js` is generated by `tools/make-icons.mjs`; this one is NOT): five app tiles orbit in and collapse into a spark, two rings pulse out, the pixel AUZslab wordmark (`/logo.png`, inverted) scans in left to right with a wine scan bar, tagline "All your apps. One place.", then a round wipe reveals the hub (min 2.9 s, waits for `#box` to leave "Loading", max 4.5 s; about 0.4 s under reduced motion). While it plays `html.auz-wait` pauses the page animations; when the wipe starts the option tiles (`.tile`, `--i` set in `set()` of `start.js`) rise in one by one (70 ms steps), the card and brand fade up, tiles lift on hover and their chevron slides, press scales down. All animation is CSS transform/opacity/clip-path only and switched off by `prefers-reduced-motion`. Tests click tiles only after the overlay is gone, so the team suite takes about 25 s longer. Static only: `git pull` (`start.js?v=9`, `launch-hub.js?v=1`); the Android/desktop shells load the live site, so no new build is needed.

## Marketing-site opening animation (`site/launch.js`)
Same motion as the hub's (`app/public/ds/launch-hub.js`), a separate copy, loaded first in `<head>` of the 26 marketing pages (every page with `footer-dark`; tool pages account/admin/cart/pay/inv/signup are excluded). Plays once per browser session (`sessionStorage auz.intro`) on the first marketing page, lifts when the page has loaded (about 2.6 s, max 4 s). Skipped inside the store/desktop apps (UA `AUZslabApp`/`AUZslabDesktop`) and for automated browsers (`navigator.webdriver`) unless the address has `?intro=1` (how the team suite tests it). A new marketing page needs `<script src="/launch.js?v=1"></script>` right after `<head>`. Static only: `git pull`.

## Which trial a salesman gets per business type (`salesman.html` `NICHE_APP`, `salesman_provision_trial`)
The trial gets the niche's default features (`niche_presets`): cafe = POS + QR ordering + kitchen + CRM + billing (menu, 6 tables and photos seeded; opens POS, QR demo, website); salon = bookings + CRM + billing + the Salon app (opens the salon admin console and website); mobile = AUZsMob (sample phone, sale and repair seeded); retail and general = POS + CRM + billing, nothing seeded; gym = bookings + CRM + billing with POS OFF, yet the salesman page opens the POS for gym, so a gym trial shows "not part of your plan" (known gap, owner to decide: give gym trials POS or remove gym). No trial includes Payroll, Ledger or Scan unless an admin ticks them in Clients -> Edit.
