Niches now: cafe, salon, retail (Retail / General), mobile, custom. Retail's preset enables only `accounting` (AUZsLedger): no POS, CRM or billing module. db/181 relabels existing gym/general tenants and leads to `retail` WITHOUT touching their features, tightens `tenants_niche_check`, and adds a trigger (`niche_normalise`) so older code that still passes 'general'/'gym' is stored as 'retail'. The `gym` and `general` rows stay in `niche_presets` only as hidden aliases (older functions default to 'general' and look the preset up). Pickers updated: salesman.html (trial type, lead type, `NICHE_APP.retail` opens `/accounts.html`), admin onboarding, cart request form. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/181_niche_retail_ledger.sql`, `git pull` (no API rebuild).

## Push registration in all five native apps + hub (Firebase project `auzslab-ea7c6`)
Firebase project created (Spark plan) with six Android apps registered: `in.auzslab.app` (hub), `app.auzslab.pos|pay|mob|scan|ledger`. One `google-services.json` (base64) is the GitHub secret `GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON`; the server key is in `.env` as `FCM_PROJECT_ID` + `FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON`. All five native apps now carry `core/push.dart` (`PushRegistration.register`, called when a login opens; silent no-op without the Firebase file), the `firebase_core`/`firebase_messaging` dependencies, the conditional Gradle plugin and a "Firebase file" workflow step. **Registration only: the server still sends alerts for QR guest orders (POS) and AUZsPay requests (db/175); nothing notifies AUZsMob, AUZsScan or AUZsLedger yet** (needs an owner decision on which events). Not compiled here (no Flutter SDK): the GitHub build is the first check. If a new app is added to Firebase later, download `google-services.json` again and update the secret.

## Web Push key (POS in the browser)
`VAPID_PUBLIC` in `app/public/pos/core.js` must be the public half of the server's `VAPID_PRIVATE` (`.env`). The key was regenerated on 2026-10-13 after the `.env` loss (new public key `BD-oWPPz...`, in code and on the server). Anyone who turned alerts on with the OLD key must tap "Notifications" in Staff & settings again (old browser subscriptions are for the old key). To rotate again: run `node server/scripts/generate-vapid-keys.js` on the server, put all three lines in `.env`, change the public key in `pos/core.js`, bump `?v=` and `sw.js` `V`.
## AUZsLedger native build: `file_picker` is pinned to 10.3.11 (Oct 2026)
The Ledger Android build failed at `flutter build apk` with `cannot find symbol FilePickerPlugin`: file_picker 11 skips applying the Kotlin plugin on AGP 9 and relies on built-in Kotlin, but the native apps keep `android.builtInKotlin=false` (other plugins need it), so its Kotlin code was never compiled. Pinned to 10.3.10 (10.3.11 was RETRACTED on pub.dev and `flutter pub get` refuses it; it applies the Kotlin plugin itself) and the call is `FilePicker.platform.pickFiles` (10.x API). Before upgrading file_picker, check the app builds in CI. Only the Ledger app uses it.
## Hub alerts for the admin and the team (db/182)
The Android hub app now wakes the AUZslab admin and team members (people with no business, so not in `fcm_tokens`). `platform_fcm_tokens` (no RLS; written only by `register_platform_push(p_token)`, admin or active team member; read/pruned by `server/src/push.js`). The hub (`start.js`, after `hub_me`) and `salesman.html` call `auzNativePush.enablePlatform(sb)` (`ds/nativepush.js` v2). `handlePushEvent` has a `platform` branch (events from `notify_platform_push(user_ids, title, body)`).
- **Admin + active managers** are alerted by: every platform notification (a `notifications` row with `tenant_id` null: new client request, payment submitted, renewals ...) and team work (`team_activity` kinds `lead_added`, `lead_stage`, `trial_created`, `setup_done`, never the actor themselves).
- **A team member** is alerted when a lead or a setup is assigned to them, and for **notices**: admin -> Team -> "Notice to the team" (`admin_post_team_notice(title, body, audience)`, audience all / one role; admin or manager only) which also shows under Notices on the team Home (`team_list_notices`). Triggers swallow their own errors.
- Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/182_platform_push.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (push.js + 3 RPCs), `git pull`, then rebuild the hub APK (the Firebase file secret is already set). Test: team suite check "Hub alerts..." (18). Not verified on a real phone.

## Browser alerts for the admin and the team on PC and iPhone (db/183, Web Push)
Same events as the hub alerts (db/182) but for browsers: `platform_web_push` (one row per browser endpoint, no RLS; written only by `register_platform_web_push(p_endpoint, p_p256dh, p_auth)`, admin or active team member, https endpoints only, re-registering moves the row). `handlePushEvent` (`server/src/push.js`) platform branch now sends Web Push to those rows (dead 404/410 endpoints are deleted) as well as FCM. `ds/nativepush.js` (v3) has `webState()` and `enablePlatformWeb(sb, ask)` using the public VAPID key; the hub (`start.js` v11) re-registers silently if permission is already granted and shows a "Turn on alerts on this device" tile for the admin; `salesman.html` has an Alerts block on the Me tab. iPhone: only works from a Home Screen web app (Apple rule), the tile says so. Click opens `/start.html` (`notificationclick` in `sw.js`).
- **Real bug fixed on the way:** in `app/public/sw.js` a `//` comment had lost its newline, so the whole `self.addEventListener('push', ...)` line was a comment since commit 8dfa309: NO browser push (POS included) ever showed a notification. The team suite now checks that the push and notificationclick listeners each start their own line.
- Not covered: `site/admin.html` itself does not register (it lives on the site origin, no service worker); admins register through the hub.
- Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/183_platform_web_push.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`, `git pull`; then on each device open the hub or team workspace and allow notifications (POS users tap Notifications again).

## WhatsApp automation (db/184, `server/src/whatsapp.js`, `docs/WHATSAPP.md`)
One sending layer, official WhatsApp Business Cloud API, **templates only**. `wa_outbox` is queue AND log; `wa_enqueue(tenant|null, phone, template_key, params[], ref)` (SECURITY DEFINER, deliberately NOT in the RPC allow-list) is the one entry point, idempotent per tenant + template + ref; a worker in `whatsapp.js` (every 10 s, `WA_WORKER_MS`) sends, retries 5xx/429 with back-off, marks permanent failures. Sender per message: the shop's own `wa_accounts` row (token encrypted with its own HKDF subkey, never returned to a browser), else the AUZslab number (`WA_PHONE_NUMBER_ID` / `WA_ACCESS_TOKEN` env) only if the admin set `wa_tenant_settings.allow_platform`, else `skipped`. Shop messages need the owner's `wa_tenant_settings.enabled`; AUZslab's own need `platform_flags.whatsapp_platform_auto = 'on'` (admin switch). Webhook `/whatsapp/webhook` (GET handshake with `WA_VERIFY_TOKEN`; POST signed with `WA_APP_SECRET`) updates delivery status (never backwards) and records STOP replies in `wa_optouts`.
- Template catalogue = `wa_templates` (name, exact body, ordered params); the same texts must be created/approved in Meta WhatsApp Manager. Admin -> WhatsApp and Console -> Management -> WhatsApp list them.
- Wired events: `client_orders` status triggers (request update, quote link, GST bill), `bookings` confirmed, AUZsMob repair `ready`, and (db/190, below) the `invoice_link` bill-ready message from POS/AUZsLedger/AUZsScan/AUZsMob sales and Salon bills. **Not wired yet (templates exist):** order ready, booking reminder (needs a scheduler), payment due, payslip, renewal reminder (add to `runRenewals()`), welcome.
- Tests: `node tests/run-local.mjs whatsapp` (14 checks, fake Meta server in `tests/lib/stack.mjs`, `stack.wa`). Never contacted the real Meta API from here: the first real send is the first true end-to-end test.
- Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/184_whatsapp.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (new route + worker + RPCs), `git pull`; then the .env steps in `docs/WHATSAPP.md`.

## WhatsApp "your bill is ready" wired to every real sale/invoice completion (db/190)
The `invoice_link` template (seeded in db/184, never used before this) now fires the moment a real sale/invoice finishes, platform-wide. Three plain `records`/table triggers, one direct JS call, same discipline as every existing wa trigger (`exception when others then null;` so a message can never block the real sale; idempotent on the sale/document id via `wa_outbox`'s own unique index; respects the shop's own `wa_tenant_settings.enabled` switch exactly as every other trigger does, nothing bypasses or duplicates that gate).
- **POS** (`records`, `kind='order'`): `wa_pos_bill_msg()` fires `after insert or update` when `data->>'status'` transitions to `'paid'` (guarded the same short-circuited `tg_op='UPDATE' and old...` way db/189 already documents, since OLD is not safely accessible on an INSERT firing). Customer from `data->'cust'`, link = the same `i.html?o=<tok|no>` scheme `pos/print.js`'s own staff-initiated `wa()` share button already builds.
- **AUZsLedger / AUZsScan** (`acc_documents`, one shared `acc_*` books): `wa_acc_invoice_msg()` fires `after update of status` when a `doc_type='invoice'` document's status becomes `'posted'` (and wasn't already -- a reprint/correction never re-fires, since a posted document is locked). Customer from `acc_parties`; link = `bill.html?t=<share_token>` (mints one if the doc has none yet, same 32-hex scheme as db/167).
- **AUZsMob** (`mob_sales`): `wa_mob_sale_msg()` fires `after insert` (the table's own `mob_push_sale`, db/107, already refuses a second insert for the same client-generated id, so this is naturally exactly-once); skipped when there's no `customer_phone` (matches every other `mob_*` trigger's `if new.phone is null ... return` pattern) or the sale is voided. Link = `mobbill.html?t=<share_token>`.
- **Salon**: not a trigger (bills are a JSON blob in `salon_store`, not a row). `server/src/whatsapp.js` exports `enqueueWa(tenantId, phone, templateKey, params, ref)` (a plain `pool.query('select wa_enqueue(...)')`, try/catch, never throws); `server/src/salon/router.js`'s `POST /admin/invoices` handler calls it right after `addInvoice()` succeeds. Link = `/i/<token>` (the same public WhatsApp-invoice-link Caddy already rewrites to `salon/invoice.html`).
- New shared SQL helpers (db/190): `wa_money(numeric)` (plain "123.45" text) and `wa_tenant_link(tenant_id, path)` (builds `https://<slug>.<public_site_url's domain><path>` from `wa_site_url()`, db/184).
- Tests: `node tests/run-local.mjs whatsapp` (17 checks: 3 new, one per DB-trigger path, each proving exactly-once via a replay/re-post attempt and the `enabled=false` gate suppressing it) and `node tests/run-local.mjs salon` (28 checks: 1 new, driven through the real salon HTTP API, not mocked).
- Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/190_wa_sale_invoice_links.sql` (after 184-189), `docker compose up -d --build api` (salon router changed), `git pull`.

## Android wrapper: Firebase file only for registered apps; AUZsScan wrapper (`in.auzslab.scan`)
`mobile/build.mjs` writes `google-services.json` only when it contains the app's own package id, because the google-services Gradle plugin fails the whole build for an id that is not in the file (pos/mob/payroll/accounts failed this way: only the hub `in.auzslab.app` and the native `app.auzslab.*` ids are registered). Other apps build without push. To give a wrapper app push: add its package id as an Android app in Firebase, re-download the file, update the `GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON` secret. `mobile/apps.json` now also has `scan` (AUZsScan, `https://app.auzslab.in/scan.html`, icon-scan) and the "Android build" workflow's `all` includes it. The native Flutter AUZsScan (`native/auzsscan`, release `native-scan-latest`) is separate and is the fuller app.

## Team workspace on desktop: salon-console style (app/public/salesman.html)
From 900px wide the floating rail becomes a labelled white sidebar card (brand + name, nav with the active item as a dark pill, Sign out at the bottom) and the content fills the screen (Home = hero + KPI tiles in a row, follow-ups and recent work in two columns). Under 900px nothing changed (floating pill bar). Home markup is wrapped in `.hm-top` / `.hm-cols` for this. The team suite's hub check now waits for `.tile:visible` because the "My requests" tile hides itself when empty (race).
## Admin panel round 3: rounded forms, bundle editor, two-column onboarding (site/admin.html)
All inputs/selects/textareas inside `.a-page` and `.modal` are one soft rounded style (12px, focus ring) from the "Round 3 polish" CSS block; pills for buttons; bundles are `.bn-card` (name/badge row, product chips, four money fields, switches); Onboard new is `.onb-card` (two columns from 900px); Team forms are full-width cards; client-card actions are a visible pill grid; the Payments sheet shows `.pay-card`s (payTable with `withClient=false`) so buttons are never cut off. Checked: JS syntax + journeys suite (14); not seen on a real screen.

## Hub app Google sign-in on Android: coming back to the app (site/signin.html, ds/appshell.js)
Owner report: after "Continue with Google" in the Android hub app, the app showed the login page again. Cause (most likely, not seen on a device): `signin.html` finished by redirecting to `auzslab://signin#auz_gt=...` from script with no tap, and Chrome/Custom Tabs often refuse to open an app that way, so the session never reached the app. Fixes: `signin.html` now also shows an **Open the app** button (a tap always works) for app-scheme returns, and `appshell.js` (both copies) also reads `App.getLaunchUrl()` so a sign-in link that cold-starts the app is not missed. `appshell.js?v=2`. Deploy: static, `git pull`; no new APK needed (the app loads the live site).

## auzsscan.auzslab.in (shared product address, db/185)
`auzsscan.auzslab.in` was missing from the shared product addresses, so it was read as a business called "auzsscan" and fell back to the POS. Now in `tenant.js` (`?v=3` on every page, sw `pos-v109`), `land.html` `HOME` (-> `/scan.html`), `server/src/appicon.js` RESERVED and the reserved-slug check (`db/185_reserve_auzsscan_slug.sql`). A new product address needs all four places; the mobile suite checks each host. Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/185_reserve_auzsscan_slug.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (appicon.js).
- **Hub app Google sign-in, second fix (the real one):** when the sign-in link `auzslab://signin#auz_gt=...` arrives while the hub is already open, `takeLink()` used to set `location.href = start.html#auz_gt=...`; a change that only touches the part after `#` does not reload the page, so `sb-client.js` (which reads the hand-off at load) never saw it and the hub stayed on the login page. Now, on `app.*` addresses, it saves the session in `localStorage.auz_session` itself and loads `/start.html?s=<time>` fresh. `appshell.js?v=3`. Test: team suite ("a Google sign-in link that arrives while the hub is open").
- **Hub app reload loop (third fix, caused by the cold-start addition):** `App.getLaunchUrl()` keeps returning the link that launched the app on EVERY page load, so after one Google sign-in the hub kept reloading itself (opening animation, then closing/crashing). `takeLink()` in `appshell.js` (both copies, `?v=4`) now remembers the used link in `sessionStorage['auz.takeLink']` and ignores a repeat. The team-suite check now fakes the app user agent and a persistent launch link and fails without the guard. If a phone is stuck in the loop: force-stop the app (or clear its data) after this is live.

## Native AUZslab hub (Flutter, `native/auzshub/`), stage 1 of 4
Owner: "native Flutter app for the hub, complete features, exact same UI, including the admin panel" (chosen scope: everything native, built in stages). Package `in.auzslab.app` (the Firebase hub id; Kotlin namespace `app.auzslab.hubapp` because `in` is a Kotlin keyword), deep link `auzslab://signin`, test APK from `.github/workflows/native-hub.yml` (release `native-hub-latest`, file AUZslab.apk), force-update key `hub` in `site/native-version.json` (`kBuild` in `lib/core/version.dart`). Foundations copied from AUZsPay (api, theme, ds, mk, kit, i18n, launch, login with Google/Staff/2-step, update screen); no local database.
- **Stage 1 (this PR):** sign-in, `HubSession` (`hub_me` + `my_dashboard`, cached for offline open), role-based home (customer / admin / team / business) with the same tiles as `app/public/start.js`, **My requests** (5-step bar, same statuses), Settings (language, appearance), alerts registration (`register_platform_push` for admin/team, `fcm_tokens` for business users; dormant without the Firebase file). **Product tiles open the native apps** through their deep link `auzs<x>://signin#auz_gt=<session>` (the apps' existing Google hand-off, so no second password); if an app is not installed its release page opens. The five apps only take that link while they show their sign-in screen (an already signed-in app ignores it).
- **Not native yet (open in the browser with the session handed over `#auz_gt`):** Team workspace, Admin panel, Admin console, Website Builder, Salon console. Stages 2-4: account + cart + pay + bill; Team workspace; Admin panel (leads, clients, requests, quotes, payments, bills, pricing, bundles, users, team, privacy, audit, ...). (Explore and Products/pricing were also browser-handoff here; see the inserted entry below — they are native as of a later PR, ahead of stage 3.)
- **Never compiled or run here (no Flutter SDK in the sandbox): the GitHub build is the first check.** Tests: `cd native/auzshub && flutter test` (session kinds, entitlement, hand-over format, request steps, rupee format, home tiles per role, My requests page).

## Native AUZslab hub, stage 2: cart, payment page, bill, account (`native/auzshub/lib/ui/cart.dart|pay.dart|account.dart`)
The hub's "My cart", "My account and purchases" and each request in "My requests" are native screens now (no browser). `CartPage` = the website cart (product checkboxes kept on the phone by `Cart` in `core/orderdata.dart`, business fields only for people without a business, GSTIN fills the state, `submit_client_order` with the same payload, then opens the request). `PayPage` reads `public_client_order(p_token)`: status, quote with GST split, the terms (copied from `site/orderterms.js` into `core/orderdata.dart`: change both when the wording changes), UPI id/name (placeholders, same as the site), an UNTICKED agree box, screenshot from the gallery (`ApiClient.uploadProof` -> `POST /storage/order-proof/<token>`), optional UTR, `public_order_submit_payment`; when paid it shows the bill lines natively plus "Open printable bill (A4)" (opens `inv.html`). `AccountPage` = profile (`my_profile` / `save_my_profile`), products the business owns, link to requests and cart. Still on the website: privacy requests, active sessions, two-step setup, delete account, plan renewal. Not compiled here (no Flutter SDK): the GitHub build is the first check. Stage 3 = Team workspace, stage 4 = Admin panel.

## Native AUZslab hub, stages 3 and 4: Team workspace and Admin panel (`native/auzshub/lib/ui/team.dart|admin.dart|admin_money.dart`)
- **Team workspace** (`TeamWorkspace`, opened from the hub's "Team workspace" tile for team members and from business logins that are also on the team): tabs per role like `salesman.html` (salesman Home/Leads/Trials/Me, setup Home/Setups/Me, support Home/Leads/Me, manager Home/Leads/Setups/Team/Me). Same RPCs: `team_me`, `team_my_work`, `team_my_leads|team_all_leads`, `team_add_lead`, `team_update_lead`, `team_lead_notes`, `team_assign_lead`, `team_list_setups|team_my_setups`, `team_assign_setup`, `team_setup_update`, `team_overview`, `team_activity_feed`, `team_list_notices`, `salesman_my_trials`, `salesman_provision_trial`. "Open their business" calls `POST /admin/salesman-open-pos` (`ApiClient.postJson`) and opens `https://<slug>.auzslab.in<page>#auz_gt=...` in the browser (limited 8 h session, logged). Not native yet: trial branding editor and trial delete (the Trials sheet links to the website team page).
- **Admin panel** (`AdminWorkspace`, tabs Requests / Clients / Leads / Money / More): Requests = the whole request-first flow (`admin_list_client_orders`, `admin_get_client_order`, status buttons, details edit, quote builder with `calcQuote()` (a port of the page's `calcQuote`, same arithmetic as `client_order_totals()`: the saved totals still come from the database), `admin_save_order_quote`, WhatsApp payment link, payment screenshot via `GET /storage/order-proof/<id>`, `admin_confirm_order_payment`, WhatsApp bill). Clients (`list_clients`, `update_client`, reset owner password), Leads (`team_all_leads`, stage and assignee, delete), Money (payments and bills lists, view and send a bill), More = Team (members, role, on/off, notices), Team applications, Pricing (monthly price), Users (suspend), Privacy requests. Still on the website only (More -> Full admin panel opens it signed in): bundles, yearly and add-on prices, client features editor, onboarding of a new client, Make invoice for a hand-recorded payment, record a payment, client messages, delete client/user, audit log, WhatsApp, system resources, salesman work.

## Native AUZslab hub: an Explore section with its own table of contents, plus Products/pricing (`native/auzshub/lib/ui/explore.dart`, `lib/ui/explore/`, `lib/ui/pricing.dart`) -- inserted ahead of stage 3
Owner asked for the marketing site's full real content to end up native, every page -- not a literal clone of the site's scroll/particle animations, just the same ink/wine brand look and the actual copy, as clean native screens. Too much for one pass, so this PR builds the navigation shell and the first three pages, and leaves everything else as named, reachable placeholders for later passes to fill in one at a time.
- **`exploreSections` registry (`lib/ui/explore/explore_sections.dart`) -- THE one file a later pass touches to add a page.** A plain `List<ExploreSection>`, one entry per site section: `(id, title, sub, icon, tone, {builder, sitePath})`. `builder` is `Widget Function(HubSession)?` -- give it a real screen and that section is native; leave it null and `ExplorePage` pushes `ComingSoonPage` instead, which just opens `sitePath` on the website (`openSite`, the same `#auz_gt` hand-off every other browser tile already uses) with an honest "coming soon, natively" message. Nothing else (`ExplorePage`, `home.dart`, tests) needs to change to add a section: append to this list, and once its `builder` exists, point it there.
- **`ExplorePage` (`lib/ui/explore.dart`)** is now a plain table of contents: one `Li` row per `exploreSections` entry, in a `Group` (same list chrome as the rest of the app). It replaces the old single-page "why us" screen from the previous PR.
- **Native this pass:** `HomePage` (`lib/ui/explore/home_page.dart`, `site/index.html`'s real hero + "Start/Shape/Grow" + connected-platform + Intelligence copy), `WhyAuzslabPage` (`lib/ui/explore/why_page.dart`, `site/platform.html`'s real "Businesses differ, software should too" pitch, the five business-type blurbs as plain cards -- not yet their own pages, just the real words -- "how it works" steps, and the independence section), `ProductsPricingPage` (unchanged from the prior PR, still the live-RPC pricing screen, now also reachable from this table of contents as well as its own home tile). Each ends in CTAs into the others, so the three native pages cross-link exactly like the site's own nav.
- **Still placeholders (named `exploreSections` entries, `ComingSoonPage` -> website):** `products` (the ~8 individual product pages: pos/crm/billing/inventory/qr-ordering/payroll/accounting/scan-and-bill, plus `products.html` itself), `business` (the 5 business-type pages), `studio` (`studio.html`), `intelligence` (`intelligence.html`), `learn` (`learn.html` + the 3 resources pages), `legal` (terms/privacy/refund/cookies/security/acceptable-use), `contact` (`contact.html`).
- `home.dart`'s "Explore AUZslab" tile still pushes `ExplorePage` (now the table of contents); "Products and pricing" still pushes `ProductsPricingPage` directly, unchanged. Every other tile (Team workspace, Admin panel, Admin console, Website Builder, Salon console) is untouched.
- `ProductsPricingPage` itself (prior PR, unchanged here): reads the SAME public, no-login RPCs the website pricing page reads (`public_product_prices`, `public_bundles`, `public_addon_price_overrides`, plus `GET /payments/config` for the live GST on/off switch via `ApiClient.paymentConfig()`) so nothing is a hardcoded product price; per-product `gst_exempt`/`setup_fee_exempt` flags (AUZsMob, AUZsScan) are respected; the onboarding fee (Rs 1,460) and AUZsPOS/QR-only setup fee (Rs 2,190) are shown as constants because they are plain, non-admin-editable constants server-side too (`server/src/payments.js` `SETUP_FEE`). "Add to cart"/"Get `<bundle>`" writes into the SAME on-phone `Cart` (`core/orderdata.dart`) `CartPage` reads, then opens `CartPage`.
- Not compiled here (no Flutter SDK): the GitHub build is the first check. Tests: `cd native/auzshub && flutter test` (the table of contents lists every section; "AUZslab" and "Why AUZslab" push their real native pages; an unbuilt section falls through to `ComingSoonPage`; pricing still renders live prices/bundles and add-to-cart still lands in the shared `Cart`).
