## Razorpay for request-first orders (db/200, `payments.js` `createClientOrderPayment`, `pay.html`)
Closes the loop on the request-first flow: on `pay.html` the client can press **Pay online** (cards, UPI, net banking) beside the UPI-QR-and-screenshot route, which stays as the fallback. Dormant until `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID`, `RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET` and `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET` are in `.env` (then `/payments/config` says `enabled` and the button appears).
- **Flow:** `POST /payments/order/create {token, terms:true}` (public; the unguessable pay-link token is the credential; terms must be ticked, recorded in `client_orders.payment`) -> `order_online_start()` returns the saved quote total -> Razorpay order for EXACTLY that total (paise) -> a `payments` row with `client_order_id` (db/200 also made `user_id` nullable and `payments_exactly_one_target` accept the third target). The page opens Razorpay Checkout; its success callback only starts polling `public_client_order` (never trusted).
- **Only the signed webhook marks anything paid:** `handleWebhookEvent` (`payment.captured` / `order.paid`) -> `online_confirm_order_payment(order, razorpay_payment_id, amount)` (SECURITY DEFINER, server-only, NOT in the RPC list, never raises). It checks the amount equals the quote (else the order stays unpaid and the admin gets a notification), then runs the admin's own `admin_confirm_order_payment` as the first platform admin for that one transaction (via `set_config('app.uid', ...)`, restored after; the inner functions all check `is_platform_admin()`), so access, the gapless `AUZ/<FY>/nnnn` bill number, renewal dates, `client_payments` row and the WhatsApp bill trigger are identical to a hand confirm. The payment is recorded with mode `other` and the Razorpay payment id as its reference. A replay does nothing (already paid -> duplicate). Audit-log entries for this show the admin's id, not a separate actor.
- **Not handled (ignored on purpose):** `payment.failed`, refunds, disputes; a second capture on an order already paid (admin must refund in Razorpay). Auto-renewal is NOT this: the recurring code is Cashfree (db/116, dormant); Razorpay Subscriptions is not built.
- `RAZORPAY_API_URL` (env, default `https://api.razorpay.com/v1`) lets tests point at a fake. Test: `node tests/run-local.mjs razorpay` (6 checks with a fake Razorpay in `tests/lib/stack.mjs`: terms enforced, exact amount, bad/unsigned webhook refused, wrong amount held, good webhook confirms once, replay safe, pay page opens Checkout with the right amount). Not tried against real Razorpay yet: first real Test Mode payment is the true end-to-end check.
- Webhook in the Razorpay dashboard: `https://api.auzslab.in/payments/webhook`, events `payment.captured` and `order.paid`, secret = `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (make it on the server with `openssl rand -hex 24`, never paste it in chat).
- Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/200_razorpay_client_orders.sql`, add the three `.env` values (back up `.env` first; quote values), `docker compose up -d --build api`.

## Razorpay part 2: failed payments, renew-now, auto-renewal (db/201)
Built on db/200. Everything is server-only SQL (`renewal_quote`, `renewal_order_create`, `renewal_settle`, `online_payment_failed`, `subscription_event`, `next_client_bill_no`) called from `payments.js`; none is an RPC.
- **Scope decision (owner, asked directly):** only the manual PAYMENT confirmation is removed. The admin still reviews requests and builds the quote (setup fees, custom charges differ per client). A fully self-serve flow was offered and not chosen.
- **Failed payments:** `payment.failed` -> `payments.status='failed'` + `failure_reason`; a later good payment still works; 3 failures on one order in a day -> an admin notification ("A client online payment keeps failing"). The pay page already shows Razorpay's own error text and lets the client retry; UPI QR stays as the fallback.
- **What a renewal costs (`renewal_quote(tenant)`):** every product still switched on, at the rate it was last billed (latest paid `client_orders` row per product), no setup or custom lines, GST by the saved state. Null (never billed through an order, e.g. hand-provisioned Showoff) or `{error:'mixed'}` (monthly and yearly products together) -> renewal stays by hand and the owner is told to contact us.
- **Renew now:** `POST /payments/renew/create` (owner only) -> `renewal_order_create` makes (or reuses) a `client_orders` row `kind='renewal'`, `status='link_sent'`; the app's Plan & account sheet opens `pay.html?t=<token>` ("Pay renewal online", `ds/plan.js` `renewBox`). The same pay page and webhook run it; `online_confirm_order_payment` sends `kind='renewal'` to `renewal_settle` (extends `tenants.renewal_date` from the later of today and the current date by a month or a year, gapless bill number, `client_payments` row, notices). Idempotent on the Razorpay payment id; an amount that differs from the bill is held and the admin told.
- **Auto-renewal = Razorpay Subscriptions:** `POST /payments/autorenew/start` (owner) creates a plan for the renewal total and a subscription (`total_count` 120 monthly / 10 yearly, first debit at the renewal date when it is 24 h+ away, else on authorisation) and returns Razorpay's `short_url` for the owner to approve UPI Autopay/card; stored in `subscriptions` (`provider='razorpay'`, db/116 table widened: period month|year, `provider_plan_id`, `failed_count`). Webhook events: `subscription.authenticated/activated` -> active; `subscription.charged` -> `renewal_settle(..., null order)` makes a paid renewal order + bill + extends the plan (once per payment id); `subscription.pending/halted` -> `payment_failed`, a notice to the client, an admin notification and (if the owner's phone is known) the renewal_reminder WhatsApp; **a failed charge never suspends access** (standing owner decision); `subscription.cancelled/completed` -> cancelled. `POST /payments/autorenew/cancel` cancels at Razorpay then marks the row. Cashfree (db/116) stays as the fallback only when Razorpay is not configured. The apps' `sb.autorenew` now calls the provider-agnostic `/payments/autorenew/*` routes (`sb-client.js?v=6`, `plan.js?v=3`, sw `pos-v113`).
- **Reminders:** `renewal_notify_run` (redefined, db/201) skips a business on an ACTIVE auto-renewal until it has actually lapsed (stage `expired` still notifies).
- **Razorpay dashboard:** add the events `subscription.authenticated, subscription.activated, subscription.charged, subscription.pending, subscription.halted, subscription.cancelled, subscription.completed, payment.failed` to the same webhook (URL `https://api.auzslab.in/payments/webhook`, same secret). Razorpay Subscriptions may need to be enabled on the account (Test Mode works for trying).
- **Not verified against real Razorpay:** plan/subscription payload shapes and event payloads follow Razorpay's documented API (fake server in tests). First real Test Mode subscription and webhook is the true check. Known limits: no proration, no plan change after starting (cancel and start again after adding products), the first auto-debit uses the amount quoted at start.
- Test: `node tests/run-local.mjs razorpay` (10 checks: renew-now, failed payments, auto-renewal lifecycle, reminders skip). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/201_razorpay_renewals.sql` (after db/200), `docker compose up -d --build api`, `git pull`.

## "Remind on WhatsApp" for payments due (db/203)
`payment_due` (db/184 template) is now sent by a button, not an event. `wa_due_send()` is the shared helper; `acc_send_due_reminder(p_party_id)` (AUZsLedger/AUZsScan: sums the customer's overdue posted invoices, needs `acc_sales`) and `mob_send_due_reminder(p_sale_id)` (AUZsMob: one credit sale's remaining balance, needs `mob_sell`, staff only for their own sales unless `mob_reports`) work out the amount and phone on the SERVER from the shop's records; the browser sends only an id. One reminder per customer/sale per day (`wa_outbox` ref); answers `{sent, reason: off|no_phone|already_sent, amount}`; `off` = the shop has not turned WhatsApp on (Console -> Management -> WhatsApp). UI: AUZsLedger Payment reminders sheet "Send automatically" (the older wa.me button stays), AUZsMob Dues -> tap a customer sale -> "Remind on WhatsApp" (needs the sale's customer phone). Registered in `server/src/data/rpc-registry.js`. Scratch-DB tested (off, first send, same-day repeat, params); not tried against real Meta. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/203_wa_payment_due_reminder.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (two RPCs), `git pull`.

## Small automations that save manual chasing (db/205, `server/src/maintenance.js`)
All server-side, quiet no-ops when WhatsApp is off. `quote_followup_run()` (daily): a quote in status `link_sent` for 2 days gets a `request_update` WhatsApp with the pay link, again at 5 days (ref `<order>-qf1|qf2`), and the admin gets a `quote_followup` notification. `admin_daily_summary_run()` (daily): a `daily_summary` notification (new requests, unpaid quotes, renewals due in 7 days; reaches the admin as a push through db/182) and, when `platform_flags.admin_phone` is set (Admin -> WhatsApp, `admin_set_admin_phone`), a WhatsApp with the new template `admin_daily_summary` (`auzslab_admin_daily_summary`; **the owner must create and get it approved in WhatsApp Manager**, body in `wa_templates`). Trigger `client_orders_setup_task`: a paid NEW business creates a team task "Set up <business>" for every active `setup` person (once per tenant, source `auto:payment_setup`). Trigger `payments_failed_retry`: a failed online payment sends the client a "try again" link (`request_update`, ref `<payment>-retry`). Already existing and not changed: the quote payment link is sent by WhatsApp the moment the admin sets `link_sent` (db/184); a contact-form lead notifies the admin (`t_notify_lead`). Test: orders suite ("Automations"). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/205_automation_followups.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`, `git pull origin main`.
- Cart (`site/cart.html`) shows a 3-step "how it works" strip (`.steps3`: request, check and quote, pay and start) above the form and a version with step 1 ticked after sending. Keep prices and the word "GST" out of the cart text (the journeys suite asserts the cart shows no prices). Retail, clothing and mobile-repair pages already use the same plain engine and look as the cafe page (checked by screenshot); only the hero heading size was aligned to 44px. Not built from the friction list: real product screenshots, in-app first-run checklists.

## Trial cafe website could not take any order: seeded items had no own id (db/207)
Same root cause as the Mannat Cafe bug (db/168): `salesman_provision_trial` (db/139-146) inserted `item` records without `id` inside `data`, so on `site.html` every cart key became `undefined|` (Add did nothing, no name prompt, no order). `db/207_records_stamp_own_id.sql` backfills every item/cat/table and adds a `BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE` trigger on `records` that stamps `data.id = id` for those kinds, so no seed or import can repeat it. The migration applied cleanly on a scratch chain; the trigger itself and a real order were NOT exercised end to end here. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/207_records_stamp_own_id.sql` (no API rebuild, no `git pull` needed for the fix itself).

## Demo (salesman trial) menu items carry no photos (db/208)
Owner: remove the photos from the demo POS items. `db/208_no_demo_item_photos.sql` strips `img` from every item whose photo starts with `/assets/demo-menu/` (a photo a salesman uploaded is never touched) and adds a `BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE` trigger on `records` that drops such an `img` as the row is written, so `salesman_provision_trial` / `salesman_apply_demo_content` (db/139-146, left unchanged) can no longer put one back. The site then shows its drawn icon fallback. The demo hero banner (`siteHero`) and the asset files are untouched. Scratch-DB tested (demo photo removed, own upload kept). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/208_no_demo_item_photos.sql` (no restart).

## QR ordering website: two designs, "Classic" and "Kiln" (owner's template), chosen per business (db/209)
Owner supplied a finished design ("Kiln Bakery and Café", warm paper tones, side index, item pop-ups) and asked for it exactly, selectable per business, with more templates to follow. **`app/public/site-kiln.html`** is that file kept verbatim (CSS, markup, drawn-in-code illustrations, Unsplash photos, copy) with only its script wired to the platform: it loads `public_menu` (categories, items with sizes, tables, `cfg`), maps POS items onto the template's product shape (first size = base price, other sizes as price differences; an item photo `img` replaces the drawn illustration, otherwise a template illustration is picked by category/name; add-on groups are not supported on the web ordering pages, same as `site.html`), uses `place_order` (table from `?t=` or the table list, takeaway sends no table, sizes by label, pickup time in the note), `public_create_reservation` for the reservation form, wa.me for the contact message, an optional UPI QR after the order (`cfg.upiId`), and fills the brand name, headline, about text, hours, address, phone, Instagram and hero photo from the business's settings (the template's bakery-only blocks are hidden for live businesses; with no live menu it stays a working demo). Cart key is per business (`kiln-cart-<slug>`). Ordering respects the `self_order` switch like `site.html`.

Table QR codes keep pointing at `site.html`, so they follow the choice. Owner picks it in the console (QR Ordering page and Website & Booking page, `designCard()` in `console/p-site.js`, with Preview links). Team members set it in the trial's branding form (`salesman.html`, "Website design"); `salesman_branding_get/save` allow and validate `siteTemplate` (db/209).
- **Adding a third design later:** add the page, add its id to `SITE_DESIGNS` in `console/p-site.js`, the `<option>` in `salesman.html`, the check in `site.html`, and the allowed list in `salesman_branding_save`.

## Per-design website editor (`site-edit.html`, `site-schemas.js`) -- REQUIRED for every new website design
Each QR-ordering website design has its own editor instead of one shared content panel. The owner opens `<business>.auzslab.in/site-edit.html` (linked as "Website login" in the Kiln footer), signs in with the normal owner login (email + password, 2-step supported; owner role only, must match the business address), and gets a form built from the design's own markers. Values are saved in `settings.siteData` (`{key: text or image address}`, through `push_record`, owner only), and the design applies them last, so they win over every other setting (`applyEd()` in `site-kiln.html`).
- **Adding a design:** mark each editable thing in the page (`data-ed="page.key" data-ed-label=".." data-ed-group="Page" data-ed-type="text|long"`, or `data-ed-img="page.key"` on an `<img>`), apply `cfg.siteData` at load, and add one line to `window.SITE_SCHEMAS` in `site-schemas.js` (id = `settings.siteTemplate` value). The editor fetches the design file and reads the markers, so nothing else changes. The classic `site.html` design is still edited in Admin console -> Website & Booking.
- Kiln fields: home (name, line, story, gallery heading, dine in / takeaway), menu intro, Our story page (texts + 4 photos), **Founder page** (name, photo, note; the Founder link shows only once a founder field has been filled, so no placeholder text goes live), footer tagline. Photos are shrunk in the browser to 1600 px wide WebP (JPEG fallback) before upload; key images load lazily.
- Kiln address: home hero and footer show a short link (`siteArea` setting, else the last two comma parts with digits removed) that opens Google Maps (`mapUrl` setting, else a maps search); the Contact page keeps the full address. `siteArea` / `mapUrl` have no editor field yet (database only).
- Test: cafe suite ("Kiln website editor"). Static only: `git pull` (sw `pos-v128`).

## Blank home-screen icons on iPhone (fix, Oct 2026)
Team, POS and a business website showed a plain letter tile. Causes: `/app-icon/<n>.png` answered a 302 redirect (or an error) when no business was found, and iOS shows a blank icon for a redirected icon; `site.html` / `site-kiln.html` had no per-business touch icon. Now `/app-icon/*` ALWAYS answers 200 with image bytes (business logo icon, else the default icons in `server/src/assets/`), the QR website pages link `/app-icon/180.png?v=3`, and Team and the shared-address POS link their static icon with `?v=3` (iOS can remember a failed icon address, so a new address forces a fresh fetch). Phones must delete the old icon and add it again after the update. Needs `docker compose up -d --build api`. Not confirmed on a real phone.

## POS Tables: floor summary + filters; POS logo follows the app's own theme (Oct 2026)
From the owner's prototype (`auzslab_pos_ui_prototype.html`), only the useful parts were taken. `V.tables` (`pos/tables.js`) now has status filter chips with counts (`S.tblFilter`: all / available / seated / ready / cleaning / reserved) and, from 1100px wide, a right-hand panel (stacked under the tiles on phones): Available / Seated / Guests / Running bills, **Needs attention** (tables ready to serve, seated 60+ min, needing cleaning with a one-tap "Mark clean") and **Coming up today** (next bookings). Tapping a table still behaves as before (opens/seats). Not copied: the prototype's selected-table detail panel, because it would change what a tap does and the POS tests depend on that.
Logo: `posLogo()` now draws both wordmarks and CSS picks one by the app's theme (`[data-theme]` first, then the phone's setting), because the old `<picture>` followed only the phone's setting, so a phone in dark mode with the app in light showed a white name on white. Desktop sidebar logo 30 -> 42px, sign-in logo 64px. Test: pos suite (29). Static only (`sw` pos-v130).

Full-width, no side gutters: page list on the left (buttons with item counts, scrolling chips on phones), the fields of the chosen page in the middle (auto-fit grid of cards, long text and photos full-width cards, per-field Reset / Use original), and from 1100px a live preview of the site on the right (iframe of the design file, jumps to the page being edited, reloads after Save). ## Home-screen icons, second fix: icon inside the page (Oct 2026)
The first fix (icon address always 200 + `?v=3`) did not cure the blank icons on the owner's iPhone, and the sandbox cannot reach production to see why. So the touch icon no longer needs a download: `salesman.html`, `index.html` (shared addresses and the default), `site.html` and `site-kiln.html` carry the 180px icon as a `data:` URI in `<link rel=apple-touch-icon sizes=180x180>` (Team also links the 512px PNG). Business pages (POS on a business address, QR websites) start from that default and, when `/app-icon/180.png` answers, swap in the business's own icon as a data URI before the owner taps Add to Home Screen. Regenerate the data URIs if `icon-team-180.png` / `icon-pos-180.png` change. If icons are still blank after this, the cause is not the page: ask the owner to run `curl -sI https://<address>/icon-team-180.png` on the server and read the status line.

## AUZslab hub wrapper and native app removed (owner decision, Oct 2026)
The packaged hub -- `desktop/` (Electron, Windows/Mac), the `"hub"` entry in `mobile/apps.json` (Capacitor Android/iOS), `.github/workflows/desktop.yml`, `.github/workflows/native-hub.yml`, `native/auzshub/` (the Flutter native hub, including its own extracted `assets/site/pages.json` marketing-page renderer) and `docs/HUB_APP_RELEASE.md` / `docs/HUB_WEBVIEW_PILOT.md` / `docs/IOS_RELEASE.md` -- is deleted entirely, owner's explicit instruction. **The live web hub itself (`app.auzslab.in/start.html`, `app/public/start.js`, `start.html`, `ds/launch-hub.js`) is untouched** -- this was only ever the five packaged-app shells around it plus the separate native rebuild, not the hub experience itself, which every browser (and every wrapped product app's own sign-in handoff) still uses exactly as before.
- `mobile/apps.json` now lists only the five product apps (pos, mob, payroll, accounts, scan); `mobile/build.mjs`/`build-ios.mjs` require an explicit `<app>` argument instead of defaulting to `'hub'`, and the hub-only `auzslab://signin` Android intent-filter block (dead code once `'hub'` can never match) is gone.
- `.github/workflows/android.yml`'s `app` input default list dropped `hub`; `.github/workflows/ios.yml` was rewritten to a single always-unsigned `pos` simulator check (that was the only other app it ever built) -- the hub's TestFlight signing pipeline is gone with it, since hub was the only bundle id this project ever had real Apple signing secrets/a provisioning profile for.
- `site/native-version.json` lost its `"hub"` key (nothing will ever check it again); `site/download-apps.html` lost the "AUZslab" / "Start here" APK card (hub was built as `wrapper-hub-latest`, which no longer exists).
- **Not touched, deliberately**: the other five native Flutter apps (`native/auzspos`, `auzsmob`, `auzspay`, `auzsledger`, `auzsscan`) and their own wrapped/Capacitor builds -- only the hub (wrapper and native) was asked for.
- A few reference-only docs (`docs/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md`, `docs/APP_INVENTORY.md`, `docs/AUZSPOS_LAUNCH_CHECKLIST.md`, `.claude/skills/global-fix/SKILL.md`) still mention the hub app/`auzshub` in passing from when it existed -- harmless (they're prose, not code that runs), not scrubbed line-by-line; update them if you're in one of those files anyway.

## Marketing-site menu: "Products" vs "Business types" label mismatch, and a desktop click-through bug (Oct 2026)
Two real, confirmed bugs in one owner report, found by actually running the rendered page in a browser rather than reading the markup alone:
- **The niche-links group (Cafés & restaurants, Salons & studios, Retail & bookstores, Clothing & apparel, Mobile retail & repair, Custom software (Studio)) was internally still called "Products"** everywhere except the desktop drawer, which showed "Business types" only because of a fragile `.nav-group:nth-of-type(1) .nav-submenu::before{content:"Business types"}` CSS override in `menu.css` -- the phone drawer and the topbar dropdown both showed the real, un-overridden text, "Products", for the exact same niche links. This is what read as "business types are missing from the header" and "products are listed under business types" in the owner's reports: the SAME group had two different names depending on which menu you looked at. Fixed at the source instead of patching the CSS further: the real button/link text (`tbn-top` in the topbar, `nav-group-toggle`'s `<span>` in both drawers) now says **"Business types"** everywhere, so phone, desktop and the topbar hover agree. The CSS override rule was left in place (harmless, now redundant-but-correct) rather than removed, matching the same pattern already used for the second group ("Resources" is both the real button text and the CSS-generated panel heading). The genuine "Products" footer column (AUZsPOS/AUZsPay/AUZsLedger/.../All products) and the in-page "Details" buttons on `products.html` were untouched -- they are genuinely about products, not niches, and were correctly labeled already.
- **Desktop "Let's talk" (and anything else under the invisible top strip of the open drawer) was unclickable**, confirmed with `document.elementFromPoint()`: `.drawer-topbar` has no explicit height in `menu.css`'s desktop block, so it stretches to its own natural content box (just the logo, since the close button and icon row are both pulled out via `position:absolute`) across the FULL WIDTH of the drawer at the top -- an invisible, transparent div that still intercepts every click across that whole area, including where the "Let's talk" button (`.sidebar-foot .btn:nth-child(2)`, itself `position:absolute;top:26px;right:36px` to sit top-right per the owner's reference) visually renders. Fixed with `pointer-events:none` on `.drawer-topbar` and `pointer-events:auto` on its real interactive children (`.drawer-topbar-logo`, `.drawer-close`, `.drawer-topbar-icons`) -- confirmed fixed the same way it was confirmed broken, via a real headless-browser click plus `elementFromPoint`, not by inspection alone. The Close button was never actually broken by this (it has its own `pointer-events:auto` now, same as before this bug mattered to it) -- if an owner report says Close still doesn't work after this ships, the real next step is confirming they're on the deployed commit and have forced their browser to drop the old cached `menu.css` (bump `?v=` again), not re-debugging the CSS.
- Verified with a real headless Chromium click-through at both 390px and 1440px: the drawer's Close button and "Let's talk" button both work, and the "Business types" label is now identical at every width. `menu.css` bumped to `?v=112` on every page that links it.

Owner request: let a customer rate and review on the invoice, and let the business link their real Google review page so a happy customer can post it publicly. `i.html` already had a private star-rating feedback box (`submit_feedback`, `kind='feedback'`, db/020) -- this adds one new setting and a conditional CTA on top of it, no new table or RPC.
- **Console**: Management -> Website & Booking -> Website card gained **"Google review link (optional)"** (`googleReviewUrl`, a plain `settings` field saved through the same `push_record` path every other site field already uses -- next to the existing "Google Maps link", which is a directions link, a different purpose).
- **`i.html`**: `public_invoice()` already returns the whole `settings` row as `cfg` (db/124's own comment), so `googleReviewUrl` needs no server change to reach the page. Picking 4 or 5 stars reveals "Would you share it on Google too? Leave a Google review ↗" inline (hidden again if they tap back down to 3 or fewer); it also reappears in the thank-you message after submitting, so the link survives the private feedback being sent. 1-3 star ratings never show it -- only the private comment box, so an unhappy customer is never pushed toward a public review. Wired into all three existing invoice render branches (chapterone bespoke, the POS bill-design gallery, the generic one) since they all share the one `feedbackHtml` template and `wireFeedback()` function.
- **Not done, scope-limited to what was asked**: AUZsLedger/AUZsScan's `bill.html`, AUZsMob's `mobbill.html` and the salon console's own invoice have no feedback box at all today (a separate, larger piece of work -- different RPCs, different storage); only `i.html` (POS/general invoices) got this.

## POS Web Push has never actually delivered a notification, the entire time it has existed (db/212)

Owner report (iPhone): turned on POS order alerts, allowed every permission, even a fresh subscribe after the `sw.js` dropped-listener fix and a full site-data clear -- still nothing off-device, while in-app alerts (the POS noticing a new order on its own next pull) worked fine. Root-caused by adding temporary logging to the live server rather than guessing: `[push] sending to 0 subscription(s) for tenant ...` printed even though `push_subs` had 5 real rows for that exact tenant, confirmed by a direct `select` against the table.

- **Real cause, same bug shape this file now documents many times over** (`createOrder()`'s RLS read, `storage.js`'s `saveSiteUpload`, the `subscriptions` table, `/auth/2fa/setup`, `appicon.js`'s `tenantForIconContext`): `handlePushEvent()` in `server/src/push.js` read `push_subs` (and `fcm_tokens`, same shape) with a bare `pool.query`, never wrapped in `withAuth()`. This function runs as a background job fired by a Postgres `NOTIFY` (`push_events`, see `realtime.js`) -- there is no logged-in caller, so `app.uid` is never set, `app_uid()` resolves to null, and both tables' owner-only RLS policies (`user_id = app_uid()`, db/002 and db/119) silently hide every row. No error anywhere -- the query just always returned zero rows, for every tenant, since Web Push and native FCM push were both built. **This means POS push notifications have essentially never worked for anyone** -- salon's push worked throughout because `salon_push_subs` (a different table, `server/src/salon/router.js`'s `salonPush()`) has no RLS at all, so its identical bare-`pool.query` pattern was never broken.
- **Fix (`db/212_push_subs_trusted_read.sql`):** four small trusted-backend-only SECURITY DEFINER functions, deliberately NOT registered in `server/src/data/rpc-registry.js`'s RPC allow-list (same discipline as `provision_from_payment`/`addon_request_pricing_context`), since this job legitimately has no user to authenticate as -- it's the server itself acting on a signal it already trusts: `push_subs_for_tenant(tenant_id, user_ids=null)`, `fcm_tokens_for_tenant(tenant_id, user_ids=null)`, `push_subs_delete_dead(id)`, `fcm_tokens_delete_dead(id)`. `push.js`'s `handlePushEvent` now calls these instead of the bare queries for both the Web Push and native-Android-FCM paths (the platform-admin/team alert paths, `platform_web_push`/`platform_fcm_tokens`, have no RLS and were never affected).
- **Verified against a real scratch database, as the actual non-superuser `app` role** (`set role app`, confirmed `rolbypassrls = false`, same role push.js's own connection pool uses): the bare `select ... from push_subs where tenant_id = $1` returned 0 rows for a tenant seeded with exactly 1 real subscription -- reproducing the bug outside of guesswork; `push_subs_for_tenant(tenant_id)` correctly returned the 1 real row; `push_subs_delete_dead(id)` correctly deleted it (a bare `delete` would have silently matched zero rows too, same root cause). `realtime.js` also gained a `.catch()` on the LISTEN client's `connect()` (was a bare `.then()` with no rejection handler -- a real, separate latent bug: a failed connect would have silently never listened, with not even an unhandled-rejection trace reliably surfacing) and two small permanent log lines (`[realtime] connected and listening on ...`, `[realtime] push_events NOTIFY received ...`) kept from the diagnostic pass, since they're cheap and were exactly what made this bug findable at all.
- **If a push report like this recurs for a different table/feature**: suspect this exact shape immediately if the table has owner-only RLS and the code reading it runs from a background job, a cron, a trigger side-effect, or anything else with no real logged-in caller -- grep for `pool.query` against that table and confirm it goes through `withAuth`/`withoutAuth` or a dedicated SECURITY DEFINER function, never a bare query, the same check this file has recommended since the Razorpay RLS bug.
- Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/212_push_subs_trusted_read.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (push.js + realtime.js changed). After deploying, test with a real order (send to kitchen, or mark ready) rather than the manual `notify_push()` SQL call used to diagnose this -- that call sends to every subscription for the tenant including stale/dead ones, which is fine for diagnosis but not representative of a real alert.
- **Follow-up 1, same session, confirmed on the owner's real device**: once delivery worked, tapping the notification opened the hub (`/start.html`) instead of the POS. `sw.js`'s `push` listener reads `data.url` from the payload and falls back to `/start.html` when absent -- the tenant Web Push payload in `handlePushEvent` never set one. Fixed: `sendWebPush(..., { title, body, url: '/index.html' })` for the tenant (non-platform, non-targeted) branch, so tapping an order alert reopens the POS itself. The platform-admin/team branch already correctly sets its own `url: '/start.html'` (that one really should open the hub). FCM (native Android) sends no `url`/click-data at all and was left unchanged -- the wrapped app has only the one screen, so its default notification-tap behavior already opens the right place.
- **Follow-up 2, same session, confirmed on the owner's real device**: after the url fix, tapping the notification opened Safari (the plain browser) instead of the installed Home Screen app. `sw.js` had **two separate `notificationclick` listeners** registered (both fire on every tap, listeners don't replace each other) -- one older, hardcoded to always open `/index.html` and never reading `data.url` at all, one newer and correct. Both called `clients.matchAll({type:'window',includeUncontrolled:true})` and `.focus()`ed the first match before ever calling `openWindow` -- on iOS that match can be an ordinary background Safari tab sitting on the same origin, which is exactly what got focused instead of the installed app. Fixed: deleted the duplicate, and the one remaining handler no longer matches/focuses an existing client at all -- it always calls `clients.openWindow(u)` directly, which is what iOS actually routes correctly to the installed PWA the push subscription belongs to; trying to "reuse" a found window is not reliable since there is no way from inside the service worker to tell a standalone-app window apart from a plain browser tab. `sw.js` bumped to `pos-v147`.

## Online order invoice links, staged return/exchange, a bell that actually shows something, an A4 bill (db/245, db/246)

Four owner asks landed together, each real and independently verified against a scratch database (see below) before shipping.

- **Online (QR table / takeaway website) customers can open their own bill on the website, not only through WhatsApp (db/245).** `place_order`'s return type changed from `void` to `text` (drop+create, same workaround this file has needed before for a return-type change): it now hands back a real token, generated the moment the order is created -- `guest_orders` gained its own `tok` column (`encode(gen_random_bytes(6),'hex')` default, same 12-hex-char shape `pos/core.js`'s own `tok()` already uses). `pos/shell.js`'s `acceptG()` carries that exact token forward into the real `records` order it creates (`o.tok = o.tok || g.tok`), so the one link the customer was given at checkout keeps working once the bill is actually billed -- no second token, no server round-trip. `site.html` and `site-kiln.html`'s order-confirmation screens now show a "View my bill" link to `/i.html?o=<tok>` (and remember it in `localStorage`); `i.html`'s "not found" message was softened to say a bill may just not be ready yet, since that link is valid (and correctly empty) from the moment of ordering, before staff have even accepted it.
- **Return & exchange is now a real, server-enforced staged workflow, not an instant one-tap action (db/246).** Owner report: the old retail "Return or exchange" button (`pos/pay.js`) applied the refund/exchange effect the instant someone tapped it -- there was no decision stage to "change" because it was never staged in the first place. New `kind='returnreq'` record with a **stage graph the server itself enforces** (`pos_returnreq_guard` trigger, mirroring `pos_order_guard`'s existing split of responsibility): `pending -> approved | rejected -> received -> refunded | exchanged`, each transition one-way; a decided/finished request's `stage` can never move again, and the raw ask (item, qty, amount, reason) is frozen the moment the request exists. Raising a request needs no approval (`pos/pay.js`'s `returnLineSheet`/`exchangePick` now call `requestReturn()` instead of the old instant `doReturn()`); approving/rejecting and finishing a refund or exchange both go through the existing `approve('return'|'refund', ...)` manager-PIN gate, same as every other money-moving POS action. New `app/public/pos/returns.js`: `V.returns` (a filterable list, reachable from Staff & settings -> Business tools, retail businesses only, same gate the original action used) and the actual effect-applying code (`completeReturn`, moved out of the old `doReturn` and now only reachable once a request is genuinely `received`). `returnableQty()` now also subtracts a line's still-open (pending/approved/received) requests, not just already-completed ones, so the same stock can't be requested back twice at once.
- **Verified against a real scratch database (full migration chain, the same 4 pre-existing one-off Mannat Cafe files this session's own precedent already skips), not just read**: `place_order` returns a real token and the carried-forward token correctly resolves through `public_invoice`; the closed-store refusal still fires correctly. The full return/exchange stage graph was driven end to end through the real `push_record` RPC as two different logged-in roles (a cashier and an owner): a cashier can raise a pending request but is correctly refused self-approving it ("Only a manager can approve or reject..."); an owner approving it works (owner/manager bypass the PIN-token check, exactly like `pos_order_guard` already does for refunds); skipping straight from `approved` to `refunded` is correctly refused ("...one step at a time"); `received` -> `refunded` succeeds; trying to change an already-`refunded` request is correctly refused ("...already refunded and cannot be changed"); and a tamper attempt that tries to also sneak `qty`/`amt` changes through an `approve` call is correctly reverted to the original frozen values.
- **The POS bell icon was already built (notification badge + click handler) but did nothing useful** -- it only ever counted unread platform `notifications` rows and, on tap, navigated away to `account.html` on a different domain, losing the POS session's place. It now also counts pending website/QR guest orders (`G`, already polled every 15s) and open return/exchange requests, and tapping it opens an in-POS sheet (`notifSheet()` in `pos/shell.js`) listing all three groups with one-tap actions (Accept a guest order, open a return request's own detail sheet) instead of leaving the app.
- **AUZslab POS bills can now print as a real A4 page, not just a receipt-width slip.** `pos/bill-templates.js` gained a sixth design, `a4` ("A4 page" in the Bill design picker, Settings -> Configuration -> Look & invoice -- it reads the gallery's own `list` array, so no console change was needed): a full `210mm` page with a real `@page{size:A4;margin:0}` print rule, formal header/totals/signature layout, same iPhone-safe bordered-table rules (`border-collapse:separate`, a `--bw` line-width variable) the existing "ruled" design already follows.
- `pos/bill-templates.js?v=2`, `pos/pay.js?v=4`, `pos/orders.js?v=4` (unchanged logic, just the version bump for cache-busting), `pos/staff.js?v=12`, `pos/shell.js?v=12`, new `pos/returns.js?v=1`; `sw.js` bumped to `pos-v148`.
- **Not done, flagged rather than guessed at**: a fourth owner report ("nav shows after scrolling past the hero picture belongs on the bottom footer nav, not the header bar") could not be found anywhere in the marketing site, the QR-ordering templates (`site.html`/`site-kiln.html`), or the POS after a real search for any scroll-triggered nav show/hide behavior -- none exists in the code as it stands. The owner named "the AUZstyle website" when asked which page, but neither this file nor the repo has a page or tenant by a name matching that closely enough to identify which template it runs on. Needs a screenshot or the exact page URL before touching anything, rather than guessing and editing the wrong file.
- Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/245_online_order_invoice_link.sql`, `< db/246_return_exchange_stages.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (no server code changed by either migration, but `place_order`'s return type changed -- restart is cheap insurance; skip it if the server never caches function signatures, which it doesn't, so a plain `git pull` with no rebuild is actually sufficient here). **Not run through the full Playwright test suite** (`node tests/run-local.mjs pos`/`cafe`) in this session -- only the SQL lifecycle and every touched JS file's syntax were verified directly; ask for real-device confirmation of the new "View my bill" link, the Returns page, the notification bell, and the A4 print layout before treating them as fully proven.

## Desktop drawer menu: niche-link group redesign, stuck-open drawer, and a flex-stretch width bug (`site/menu.css`)

A multi-part fix to the shared desktop drawer markup/CSS (`site/menu.css`, the 5 `business-*.html` pages plus `index.html`/`products.html`), found and fixed in one pass while chasing the owner's screenshots of a garbled, permanently-open, uncloseable desktop menu on `business-cafes.html`.

- **3-group vs 2-group pages.** `index.html`/`products.html` have 2 nav groups (the niche-links group absorbs the old "Products" slot, plus Resources); the 5 `business-*.html` pages genuinely have 3 (a real Products group, the niche-links group, and Resources). CSS that picked the niche-links group by `nth-of-type(1)`/`nth-of-type(2)` therefore pointed at the wrong group on the 3-group pages, producing the overlapping/garbled submenu text in the owner's screenshots. Fixed by marking the real niche-links group with an explicit `.nav-biz` class in markup (never inferred from position) across all 7 pages, and giving each group's submenu an explicit `<div class="nav-submenu-head">Label</div>` first child instead of a `::before{content:"..."}` CSS trick keyed to `nth-of-type`.
- **The drawer was permanently, unconditionally open on every page except `index.html`.** `theme.css`'s base desktop rules (`.topbar{display:none}`, `.sidebar{transform:none}`) are an old "always-visible narrow sidebar, no toggle" design; only `index.html` carries its own inline override restoring a real slide-in/out drawer (`.topbar{display:flex}`, `.sidebar{transform:translateX(100%)}`, `.sidebar.open{transform:translateX(0)}`). Every other page loading the shared `menu.css` turned the sidebar into a full-screen overlay but never re-added that open/close toggle, so `theme.css`'s `transform:none` stayed in effect -- the drawer was on screen from first paint with no way to close it, not a click/z-index bug. Fixed by replicating `index.html`'s override inside `menu.css`'s own desktop block (`.topbar{display:flex!important}`, `#sidebarDrawer.sidebar{transform:translateX(100%)!important}`, `.sidebar.open{transform:translateX(0)!important}`), plus `.shell{flex-direction:column!important}`/`.content{margin-right:0!important}` (`theme.css`'s base `.shell{flex-direction:row}` was stretching `.topbar` to the content column's full height via `align-items:stretch`, rendering the hamburger ~2300px down the page instead of at the top).
- **A `.nav-group-toggle` button (Products/Resources) rendered at nearly the drawer's full width (~1195px), intercepting hover/clicks meant for the always-visible `.nav-biz` panel sitting behind it.** Confirmed with `document.elementFromPoint()` that hovering "Resources" actually hit the pinned Business-types panel's own text. The existing `width:auto` override on `.nav-group-toggle` (menu.css) was present and *should* have beaten `theme.css`'s `.nav-group-toggle{width:100%}` on cascade order alone -- but neither rule was actually the cause: `.nav-group{display:flex;flex-direction:column}` (theme.css, base rule, applies at every width) defaults to `align-items:stretch`, which stretches a flex child along the cross axis (width, for a column) to fill the parent regardless of the child's own `width` property -- `width:auto` never overrides flex-stretch sizing, only `align-self`/`align-items` can. Fixed with one added property: `.nav-group{...;align-items:flex-start}` inside menu.css's own desktop block, which lets each toggle button size to its own content again. Verified by reading the actual matched CSS rules and computed width in a real headless-browser session (1195px before, ~170-290px after, correctly sized per label) before and after the fix -- never guessed from the CSS alone.
- **Verified end to end with Playwright** on `business-cafes.html`, `business-salons.html` (3-group) and `products.html`, `index.html` (2-group) at desktop width: drawer closed by default, hamburger at the top, click opens it, hovering Products/Resources shows each one's own submenu with no interception, the Business-types panel stays pinned/visible throughout, Close actually closes it back to the off-screen state. Phone width (390px) re-checked unaffected (all these changes are inside `@media(min-width:861px)`).
- `menu.css?v=` bumped to 116 on every page that links it (29 files).

## Standing instruction (owner, Oct 2026): ship automatically, then give only the deploy commands
Do not wait for the owner to type "Merge". When a piece of work is finished and its tests pass: commit, push the branch, open the PR, squash-merge it into `main`, and then end the reply with just the deploy commands (the exact copy-paste block, `git log -1 --oneline` as the check, plus any `psql` / `docker compose up -d --build api` / "reload" step that change needs). Keep the reply short. Still true: if `main` has moved (another session pushes there too), merge it in and resolve conflicts by reading both sides; never overwrite someone else's work with `--ours`. Say plainly what was not tested on a real device.

## AUZsStyle (feature key `style`, db/214-218, `app/public/style.html` + `app/public/style/`): clothing and footwear shops

Owner request (from a prototype file): a complete POS for clothing and shoe shops, restaurant-POS-like till, Apple HIG, a small/premium phone UI, an admin side inside it, and analytics that are about VARIANTS (which product, category, size and colour sells first and how much). E-commerce website is planned for later and is NOT built. Built like AUZsMob/Payroll/Accounting: its own real `sty_*` tables, RLS on with no policies, every access through SECURITY DEFINER functions that start with `sty_guard(perm)` (entitlement `features.style` AND the owner switch AND the role); registered in `server/src/data/rpc-registry.js`; business-rule errors use SQLSTATE `STnnn` (HTTP 400, added to the regex in `server/src/lib/http.js`).
- **Model:** a STYLE (name, category, brand, gender, HSN, tax %, price, MRP) owns VARIANTS (colour x size, own barcode, average cost). Stock = SUM of append-only `sty_stock_moves` per branch (kinds opening/purchase/sale/return/exchange_in/adjust/damage/lost/transfer_out/transfer_in/void/count), never a counter; damaged returns go to `dmg`, not saleable stock. Bills (`sty_sales` + `sty_sale_lines`) carry a client-made uuid (idempotent), a gapless number `<prefix>-00001`, and a snapshot of style/colour/size/price/cost so analytics never change when a style is renamed. Weighted-average cost moves on `sty_receive`.
- **Money rules (server, `sty_sell`):** price/MRP/tax per line (style tax %, else the settings slabs by unit price, else default), prices include tax by default, line discount + bill discount spread over lines, round-off, loyalty points earned/redeemed, store credit, due balance needs a customer, change comes out of cash. A plain staffer cannot exceed `max_discount_pct` or change a price (manager needed), returns older than `return_days` need a manager, stock cannot go below zero unless allowed (an `offline:true` sale is never refused for stock). Returns: cash/UPI/card/store credit/exchange (exchange = credit used as a payment on the next bill); a bill with returns cannot be cancelled. Day close (`sty_day_close`) locks bills on or before a date. Transfers between branches/warehouses (out then in). Trial-room holds, cash shifts (expected = opening + cash sales), monthly staff targets/commission.
- **Analytics (db/217):** `sty_dashboard`, `sty_analytics` (by category, style with best size/colour, size, colour, category x size heat map, weekday/hour, variant velocity: per day, sell-through of what was received, cover days, days to first sale, days to sell out, returns by size, staff, customers) and `sty_insights` (reorder suggestions from 30-day velocity and lead/cover days, out-of-stock-but-selling, slow stock, overstock, size mix selling vs holding). Dates are `Asia/Kolkata`; revenue/profit exclude cancelled bills and the returned part of a line. Profit/cost are hidden from staff unless the owner allows (`staff_see_cost`). `sty_integrity_check()` proves bill totals, stock moves and returns agree (the test suite runs it after each step).
- **App:** plain scripts (no build), copied AUZsMob kit (`style/core.js`, CSS) + `style.fine.css` (the till, cards, grids, receipt, tags, small premium density). `data.js` = API + catalogue cache + an unsent-sales queue in localStorage (selling works offline: the sale waits, stock in the cached catalogue goes down, sent on `online`; a 4xx refusal is shown and dropped, a dead connection keeps it). Pages: Sell (style grid, category chips, search/barcode field that takes Enter like a hardware scanner, camera scan via `ds/scanner.js`, colour+size picker with stock, bill pane on the right / bottom bar on phones, discount, hold, pay split, receipt with print and WhatsApp text link), Overview, Stock (matrix per style, adjust/count, style editor, barcode tags printed as Code 128 SVG from `barcode.js`, branch transfers), Analytics (5 tabs), Bills (find, reprint, return/exchange, cancel, collect due), Buy (receive by size run, suppliers and what is owed), People (customers, staff targets), Settings (shop, selling rules, tax slabs, loyalty, branches, day close, health check, sample styles). English text is the i18n key (`t('...')`), Hindi in `HI` in `i18n.js` (partial, wording is mine: needs a Hindi speaker's review). Native `Element.append` is patched in `core.js` to skip null/false.
- **Wiring:** `db/218_style_product.sql` (price row `style` at 0 = quote by hand: set the real price in Admin -> Pricing; reserves `auzsstyle`), product address `auzsstyle.auzslab.in` -> `/style.html` (tenant.js, land.html, appicon.js, index.html/site-kiln.html shared lists), admin client editor tick box + labels, account page (permissions `sty_*`, service group, staff-login link), hub tile in `start.js`, icon + launch animation (`style` mark in `tools/make-icons.mjs`), `manifest-style.json`, `sw.js` (pos-v156). Turn it on for a client in Admin -> Clients -> Edit (AUZsStyle) or `update tenant_settings set features = features || '{"style":true}' ...`.
- **Not built / not verified:** the e-commerce site; a public bill link page (the receipt is print + WhatsApp text only); thermal/Bluetooth printing (browser print only); tag printing was checked by decoding the Code 128 with the bundled ZXing reader, not with a real label printer; offline queue covers sales, receiving and returns but not catalogue edits; nothing seen on a real phone; marketing page and pricing not done; Hindi is partial. Tests: `node tests/run-local.mjs style` (24 checks: access, perms, variants, receive + average cost, sale maths, refusals, returns, damaged, cancel, transfers, count, shifts, analytics, day close, every screen on phone and desktop, till flow, barcode decode).
- **Deploy:** `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/214_style_schema.sql` then 215, 216, 217, 218 in order (`<` redirect, after `set -a; source .env; set +a`), `docker compose up -d --build api` (RPC list + error codes changed). New tables are only reachable through functions, so no `grant` is needed (production grants differ from a fresh build, see db/094's note).

### AUZsStyle polish pass (Oct 2026): layout, dark mode, inventory cards, inline customer, live demo
- **Styling lives in `app/public/style/style.fine.css`** (the "Polish pass" block at the end overrides the older rules; bump its `?v=` in `style.html` and `sw.js`). Tokens `--glass`, `--tab-on`, `--panel`, `--ring` are defined for light and dark: never hard-code `#fff`/`#ececec` on a bar or panel (that is what made the side bar and sell panel unreadable in dark mode). Topbar is solid (`var(--bg)`), the phone tab bar floats lower with a fade (`.shell::after`) so content never shows beneath it.
- Desktop: the Sell bill pane is a full-height flex column with the totals and Pay at the bottom; Overview, Analytics (host `.grid`) and Settings (`.setg`) flow into two balanced CSS columns (`column-span:all` for hero and KPIs) so the right half is never empty. KPI rows use `auto-fit` so there are no empty cells.
- Stock list is a card grid (`.igrid` / `.icard`: picture, price, stock chip, stock bar), not table rows. Charts: `hourRows()` fills every hour 9-21 so one busy hour is not one giant bar.
- Bill: customer name + phone are typed inline (`BILL.custName/custPhone`); `resolveTypedCustomer()` calls `sty_save_customer` (finds by phone, else creates) just before payment, online only. The magnifier still opens the customer picker.
- **Live demo:** `db/221_demo_style.sql` turns `style` on for the shared `demo` tenant and seeds sample styles + bills (idempotent). `site/live-demo.html` has an AUZsStyle card (demo.auzslab.in/style.html, demo@auzslab.in) and every page's Live demo menu lists AUZsStyle. The style suite's "another business" check ignores the `demo` tenant's rows.
- Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/221_demo_style.sql` (no API rebuild). Not seen on a real phone.
- **AUZsStyle home-screen icon = the shop's logo (db/222):** Settings -> Shop has a logo upload (`sty_settings.logo_url`, saved through `sty_save_settings`). `public_app_icon_context()` falls back to that logo and shop name when the business has no POS `settings` record, so `/app-icon/180.png` draws it centred on a tile; `shopIcon()` in `style/shell.js` swaps it into the apple-touch-icon as a data URI on business addresses only (shops without a logo keep the t-shirt icon). Re-add the icon on the phone after uploading. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/222_style_app_icon_logo.sql` (no API rebuild).

`site/launch.js` and its `<script>` tag on every marketing page are deleted; pages just open. The hub's own opening (`app/public/ds/launch-hub.js`) and the per-app launch animations (`ds/launch.js`) are separate and untouched. Do not re-add a site intro without asking.

### AUZsStyle: pictures per colour (db/223)
A style can have its own picture set for each colour. `sty_styles.colour_photos` (jsonb `{ "Blue": [main, extra...] }`, max 8 per colour) is returned by `sty_style_json` as `colourPhotos` and written only by `sty_set_colour_photos(p_id, p)` (needs `sty_manage`; registered in `rpc-registry.js`; colours with no pictures are dropped). Editor: Edit style -> "Pictures per colour" (one picture box per colour chip, appears as colours are added; saved right after `sty_save_style`). Till: the colour/size sheet shows `colourPics(s, col)` (lib.js; falls back to the style's own pictures), so picking a colour switches the header picture and gallery. The grid card keeps the style's main picture. "Design" was read as the colour/pattern variant; if the owner meant something else (e.g. separate print designs of one style) ask. Not built: switching pictures on the bill line, receipt or the future e-commerce site (they can read `colourPhotos`). Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/223_style_colour_photos.sql` then `docker compose up -d --build api` (RPC list changed). Test: style suite (30).

### AUZsStyle: short public bill link + better WhatsApp message (db/224)
WhatsApp on a bill now carries a short link `https://<shop>.auzslab.in/b/<10 hex>`. `sty_sales.share_token` (unique) is made by `sty_share_sale(p_id)` (needs `sty_view`, same token on every call); `public_sty_sale(p_token)` is the public read (token is the only access; returns the shop header + the bill, never staff, cost, barcode, note or the customer's phone). Page: `app/public/stybill.html` + `stybill.js` (textContent only, dark mode, print/PDF button; also works as `/stybill.html?t=<token>`). **Caddy** maps `^/b/[a-f0-9]{8,32}$` to `/stybill.html` next to the salon `/i/` rule, so the Caddyfile change needs `docker compose restart caddy`. `waText(sale, link)` / `shareWa()` in `style/lib.js`: shop name, "Hi <first name>", bill no. and date, lines with (colour, size), discount, total with payment mode, due, and the link (the return-days, footer and phone lines were removed at the owner's request; the public bill page still shows return days and the footer); no emoji. Offline (or if the link cannot be made) the message goes without the link. The window is opened inside the tap before the link is fetched (iPhone popup blocking). Not built: a shop-editable message template (placeholders), the link on the printed receipt. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/224_style_bill_link.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`, `docker compose restart caddy`, `git pull`. Test: style suite (31).

### AUZsStyle: "Limited stock" and "Sold out" labels (db/225)
`sty_styles.badge` (null | `limited` | `soldout`), set by `sty_set_badge(p_id, p_badge)` (needs `sty_manage`, audited; registered) and returned by `sty_style_json` as `badge`. **Limited stock** is only a label for exclusivity (white pill on the Sell card, chip in Stock). **Sold out** shows the same pill the real zero-stock state uses, dims the card, and `addLine()` / `pickVariant()` refuse to add that style to a bill (also by scanned tag), whatever the real stock is; stock numbers and reports are not changed. Set it in Edit style ("Show on the till as": Normal / Limited stock / Sold out) or with the two toggle buttons on the style's stock sheet. A style with zero real stock still shows Sold out by itself. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/225_style_badges.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`, `git pull`. Test: style suite (32).

### AUZsStyle: CRM + WhatsApp automation (db/226)
People page now has Customers (KPIs, segment chips New / Regular / Lapsed (60 days) / Gold 10,000 / Silver 3,000 / credit / points, customer sheet with spend, average bill, favourite sizes / colours / styles, recent bills, messages sent), Follow-ups (birthdays and anniversaries in the next 30 days, lapsed customers, each a wa.me tap) and Staff. Customers gained gender, city and `wa_optin`. RPCs `sty_crm_list`, `sty_crm_overview`, `sty_customer_detail` (registered). Automation uses `wa_enqueue`, so a message leaves only when the platform admin ticked "May use AUZslab number" AND the shop turned WhatsApp on (Settings -> WhatsApp messages card, `wa_my_status` / `wa_save_settings`, owner/manager): a trigger on `sty_sales` sends `invoice_link` with `https://<shop>.auzslab.in/b/<token>` (once per bill, skipped for opted-out customers); `sty_wa_daily_run()` (called daily from `maintenance.js` `runStyleGreetings`) sends `birthday_greeting`, `anniversary_greeting` and the new `style_winback`. **The owner must create `auzslab_style_winback` in WhatsApp Manager** (body in `wa_templates`, two variables, ends with words). Not built: gift cards / memberships, campaigns to a segment (use the Follow-ups wa.me taps), review requests. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/226_style_crm_whatsapp.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`, `git pull`. Test: style suite (33).

### AUZsStyle: collections (db/227)
A collection is a named group of styles ("Summer 2026"). Tables `sty_collections` + `sty_collection_styles` (RLS on, no policies); RPCs `sty_collections_list` (any viewer), `sty_collection_save`, `sty_collection_delete`, `sty_collection_members(p_id, p_add, p_remove)` (need `sty_manage`; registered). UI in `style/p-collections.js`: Stock -> **Collections** (create, open, add styles, remove, rename, delete), a style's stock sheet -> **Add to collection** (tick several, or create one on the spot), and Sell shows a chip per non-empty collection that filters the till. Separate from the older free-text `collection` field on a style (still searchable). Not built: showing collections on the bill or in Analytics. Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/227_style_collections.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`, `git pull`. Test: style suite (34).

### AUZsStyle: UPI QR in the pay sheet (no migration)
Settings -> **Pay by UPI** stores the shop's UPI id and payee name in `sty_settings.features.upiId` / `upiName` (the `features` jsonb already round-trips through `sty_save_settings` / `sty_settings_json`, so no DB change; the id is checked against a `name@bank` pattern). In the Sell pay sheet (`paySheet` in `style/p-sell.js`), as soon as the UPI amount is above 0 a small card shows a QR for exactly that amount (`upi://pay?pa=&pn=&am=&cu=INR&tn=<bill>`, drawn by the vendored `vendor/qrcode.js`, no CDN) and a **Payment received on UPI** tick box; **Complete sale** stays disabled until the cashier ticks it (nothing confirms the payment automatically: the cashier checks the phone/bank app). With no UPI id set, the sheet shows a hint (managers get an "Open Settings" button) and does not block. Receipt WhatsApp / print are unchanged. Deploy: static only, `git pull` (sw `pos-v185`). Test: style suite (35). Not seen on a real phone.

### AUZsStyle: online store (`app/public/store.html`, `store/store.js|css`, db/228)
Owner request (from BLUORNG phone/desktop screenshots, used as reference only): a full online store per clothing/footwear shop. Public page `https://<shop>.auzslab.in/store.html` (no login; `?shop=<slug>` works on a shared address). Plain script, own tokens (not the staff design system): very small type (10.5px body), light grey page, white rounded cards, black pills, deliberate empty cards in grids (`.blank`), floating glass phone dock (explore, shop, saved, stores, search + round brand and chat buttons), desktop menu panel (shop, Top/Bottom/Accessories grouped from category names, shop by colour families, help), hero slider from `config.heroImages` (falls back to newest styles' pictures), collection deck, "New arrivals" grid with pictures that scroll inside each card (scroll-snap, dots, arrows on hover), product page with ONE fixed primary picture and a scrollable rail of the others (desktop: rail left, primary sticky; phone: primary then a scrolling strip; clicking a thumbnail promotes it), colour dots, size pills with stock, size guide, qty, Add to bag / Buy now, tabs Details & Description / Washcare / Shipping with every detail field of the style, "Products you may like"; **Refine** sheet (sort, type, size, colour dots, budget range with a live "Show N pieces" count) instead of a torch-style filter; bag with stat pills; checkout asks for email, phone, first/last name, address, apartment, city, state, PIN, notes, "save for next time" (browser only); stores page (cards, Get direction, call), about + founders, FAQ (category buttons + accordion), contact (WhatsApp), policy pages (defaults if the shop leaves them empty), footer (store cards, connect, we are, order support). Saved items and bag live in `localStorage` per shop.
- **Data/RPCs (db/228, all registered):** `sty_store` (one jsonb `config` per shop; keys whitelisted in `sty_store_save`: on, tagline, announce, heroImages, heroTitle, heroCta, about, founders, stores, faqs, policies, shipping{fee,freeOver,note}, pay{cod,upi}, social, email, phone, brandName), `sty_web_orders` (RLS on, no policies). Public: `public_sty_store(slug)` (closed unless `config.on`; never returns cost, barcode or exact stock: `left` is capped at 9), `public_sty_place_order(slug, p)` (prices, stock and shipping are recomputed on the server from the variants, the browser's prices are ignored; max 20 per line, 40 lines, 4 orders per phone per hour; payment `cod` or `upi` only), `public_sty_order(token)`, `public_sty_order_paid(token, utr)`. Staff: `sty_web_orders_list/open/update` (`sty_sell`), `sty_store_get/save/request_payments`.
- **Stock does NOT move on a web order**; staff "Make a bill" puts the order on the till (`makeBill()` in `style/p-store.js`: refills `BILL`, name, phone, note) and stock moves when that bill is made. Overselling between order and bill is therefore possible; the order only checks stock at the moment it is placed.
- **Owner side:** AUZsStyle nav "Online store" (`style/p-store.js`): Orders tab (new / in progress / done, order sheet with Confirm, Packed, Shipped, Delivered, Mark as paid, Make a bill, Cancel, call/WhatsApp) and Store tab (open switch, store link, hero pictures, shipping and payment, stores, about, founders, FAQ, contact/social, policies). A new order also writes a `notifications` row for the shop.
- **Payments, honest status:** UPI QR uses the shop's own UPI id from Settings (QR with the exact amount, then the customer enters the UTR; staff verify in their bank app and mark paid) and cash on delivery. **Card payments through AUZslab's Razorpay are NOT built.** The owner asked for "use AUZslab's Razorpay, shops ask for integration": the shop can press "Ask for online payments" (`sty_store_request_payments`, sets `payReq='requested'` and notifies the admin), nothing more. Reason: collecting other businesses' money in AUZslab's own Razorpay account would make AUZslab a payment aggregator (RBI rules); the safe routes are Razorpay Route (linked accounts with KYC) or each shop's own Razorpay keys. Needs an owner decision before building.
- **Not built:** per-order shipping couriers/tracking numbers, order emails/WhatsApp to the customer (staff use the call/WhatsApp buttons), customer accounts/login (saved items are per browser), blogs and careers pages from the reference, coupon codes, a short store address (use `/store.html`), real-phone checks. Tests: style suite (37 checks incl. public catalogue leaks, price/stock refusals, COD + UPI orders, inbox, and the store in a real browser at 390 and 1366 wide).
- **Deploy:** `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/228_style_store.sql` (after `set -a; source .env; set +a`), `docker compose up -d --build api` (new RPCs). Then in AUZsStyle -> Online store -> Store, add pictures and switch it on.

### AUZsStyle store: foldable details and shop basics (static only, `store.js?v=4`)
Product details are three foldable sections (Details & Description, Washcare, Shipping & returns, each +/-). On a wide screen the sticky details card folds up by itself (name, price, Add to bag / Buy now stay) when the "other products" bands scroll into view and opens again at the top; "Show size, colour and details" pins it open. On phones a slim buy bar appears once the real buttons scroll out of view. Added: Recently viewed (per browser, on product, home and bag pages, Clear button), free-shipping progress bar in the bag, Share, "Notify me" on a sold-out piece (WhatsApp to the shop), My orders page (orders placed on this browser, `#/orders`), "Show more" paging (24 at a time), "% off" tag on cards, Product JSON-LD for search engines. Not built (need backend): reviews and ratings, coupon codes, customer accounts, back-in-stock emails, delivery-by-PIN estimate. Test: style suite (fold, recently viewed, My orders).

### AUZsStyle store: no sale prices unless approved, and a self-sliding "slide stack" (db/229, `store.js?v=6`)
- **Discounts are off by default on the website.** The store shows no strike-through MRP and no "% off" (cards, product page) unless the owner/manager switches "Show discounts on the website" on in AUZsStyle -> Online store -> Store (`config.showSale`, whitelisted in `sty_store_save` by db/229; only `sty_manage` can change it). Prices charged are unchanged (they always come from the variants on the server). `saleOn()` / `discPct()` in `store/store.js` are the one place to check.
- **Slide stack** (`stack()` in `store/store.js`, `.stk/.sk` in `store.css`): a deck of product cards, centre card full size, neighbours peek out, advances every 3.2 s (pauses on touch/hover/wheel for 5 s and when the tab is hidden, off under reduced motion, stops when removed from the page). On the shop page it shows the chosen category's products (`?cat=`) or, with no filter, all products; on home it shows all products. Needs 3+ products with a picture.
- Deploy: `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/229_style_store_showsale.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` is NOT needed (SQL only), `git pull`. Not seen on a real phone; the style suite (37) passes. The owner's message was cut off after "And": the missing request is still unknown.

### AUZsStyle store round 5 (db/230-232): analytics, auto-billing, coupons, reviews, returns, tracking, own-Razorpay, faster first load
- db/230 visitor analytics, db/231 paid web orders auto-bill + push. **db/232**: coupons (`sty_coupons`, first-order-only, per-phone once, checked again inside `public_sty_place_order`), reviews (delivered buyers only, auto-published, owner can hide), return/exchange requests from the order page (inbox in AUZsStyle -> Online store -> Offers & reviews), courier + tracking number on the order (courier link map `COURIERS` in `store/store.js`), order lookup by number + phone, back-in-stock list (`sty_web_notify`, WhatsApp sent by a trigger on stock moves), abandoned-bag reminders (opt-in `config.abandonWa`, daily `sty_abandoned_run()` from `maintenance.js`), size guide chart (`config.sizeGuide`, "Fit:" line in a style's details = fit note), shop-own Razorpay keys (`sty_store_secrets`, `POST /payments/sty/start|verify` in `routes/payments.js`, HMAC checked in SQL `sty_online_confirm`; the money goes straight to the shop, AUZslab never holds it), owner notification + push when a style sells out. WhatsApp order messages are only confirmed + shipped (`config.waOrder`, default on, costs the shop per message). **The owner must create four templates in WhatsApp Manager**: `auzslab_style_order_confirmed`, `auzslab_style_order_shipped`, `auzslab_style_back_in_stock`, `auzslab_style_abandoned_bag` (bodies in `wa_templates`).
- Store page: stale-while-revalidate catalogue cache (`st.cat.<slug>`), per-route title/meta/canonical (`setMeta`), share links `/store.html?p=<id>`, checkout: phone first, PIN -> city/state autofill (api.postalpincode.in, falls back to typing), COD default, coupon box.
- **Not built:** customer login with phone OTP (needs an SMS provider; the store keeps details per browser and order lookup by number + phone instead), crawler link-preview endpoint for `/store.html` (Caddy rule), real-Razorpay run (route tested only by SQL signature check, the fake Razorpay server was not used), nothing seen on a real phone. Test: style suite (39).

### AUZsStyle buying intelligence + customer requests (db/233, db/234; built locally, NOT committed or pushed: the owner's master prompt said not to)
- **Requests (db/233):** `sty_requests` + `sty_request_save/list/set/open`. Staff note an unavailable item from Sell ("Customer request" in the header, or the button shown when a search finds nothing); the owner gets a notification + push at once; the customer gets WhatsApp when it is marked Ordered and when it is In stock (a trigger on `sty_stock_moves` marks a request linked to a variant as arrived). New templates to create in WhatsApp Manager: `auzslab_style_request_ordered`, `auzslab_style_request_ready`. Page `requests` (`style/p-requests.js`). Requests also count as lost-demand evidence.
- **Intelligence (db/234, `style/p-intel.js`, nav "Buying intelligence"):** read-only SQL over the real ledger. `sty_intel_base()` gives one row per active variant (units, returns, discount, revenue, cost snapshot, stock, received, **available days** rebuilt from the stock-move ledger day by day). On it: `sty_intel_overview`, `_designs` (score 0-100 from configurable weights in `sty_settings.features.intel.weights`, components, class, lifecycle, confidence), `_design` (colour x size matrix, size curve own vs category, comparables, estimate for a new design), `_reorder` (reorder point = demand over lead time + safety days; size-curve allocation; pack size; season end blocks a reorder), `_capital`, `_lost` (estimate only with >= 7 available days, 3 sales, 2 stockout days), `_basket` (pairs need 3 bills), `_assistant` (templated text from those results), `_set_settings`, `_set_policy` (per-style policy, season end, lead time, pack, minimum, supplier: new nullable columns on `sty_styles`). Cost/profit hidden unless owner/manager or `staff_see_cost`. Periods clamp to 180 days.
- **Known limits (say them):** purchase orders are not modelled so incoming stock counts as 0; GMROI uses today's stock cost (no historical valuation); return costs beyond refunds are not recorded; no forecasting model beyond rate, trend and size-curve shares; no seasonal-history forecast, no image similarity, no AI text (assistant is deterministic); lost-demand is an estimate; Hindi labels not added; nothing seen on a real phone. Assumed AUZsStyle serves shops buying ready stock (not brands manufacturing their own).
- Test: style suite (41). Deploy: `psql ... < db/233_style_requests.sql` then `db/234_style_intelligence.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api`, `git pull`.

## Query statistics (pg_stat_statements) and scaling notes (Oct 2026)
- `docker-compose.yml` starts Postgres with `shared_preload_libraries=pg_stat_statements`. After the first restart run once: `docker compose exec -T postgres psql -U "$POSTGRES_USER" -d "$POSTGRES_DB" -c "create extension if not exists pg_stat_statements;"`. Admin -> System resources -> "Slowest database work" then fills in (`admin_load_report`, db/240). Stats reset when Postgres restarts.
- **No load balancer yet, on purpose.** The API keeps state in memory (the salon 30 s cache, the revoked-session list, rate limits, the live WebSocket tills, `metrics.js`), so two API copies behind a balancer would disagree. Scale up the server first; before running more than one API copy, move those to a shared store (Redis or the database). Postgres, not the API, is the first thing to run out.

## Server efficiency, phase 1 (Oct 2026): database memory, timeouts, migration tracker, log rotation, env backups
- `docker-compose.yml`: Postgres memory settings sized for the 4 GB server (shared_buffers 768MB, effective_cache_size 2GB, work_mem 16MB ...) and `logging: *log` (10 MB x 3 per container) on every service. Apply with `docker compose up -d --force-recreate postgres` then restart api.
- `db/242_timeouts.sql`: the `app` role gets statement_timeout 60 s, idle_in_transaction 30 s, lock_timeout 15 s. If a legitimate long job is refused with "canceling statement due to statement timeout", split it into chunks (imports already are) rather than raising the limit globally.
- **`tools/migrate.sh`** (status / baseline N / apply) records applied files in `schema_migrations`. Run `baseline 242` once on the live server, then deploys are `git pull && bash tools/migrate.sh apply`. Files 000 and 999 are not managed (first-boot and grants).
- `tools/backup-env.sh` (daily copy of .env to /root/env-backups, 14 kept) and `tools/install-cron.sh` (daily env copy + weekly `restore-test.sh`, log in /var/log/auzslab-jobs.log). Keep a password-manager copy of .env as well.

## Server efficiency, phase 2 (Oct 2026): answer cache, small pictures, compression
- `server/src/lib/pubcache.js` shares identical read-only RPC answers for a few seconds (public menu / store 15 s, price lists 60 s, analytics and reports 20-60 s per person). Any signed-in write to `sty_*`, `admin_*`, `push_record`, `save_*`, `salesman_*` or `/db` drops the `public_*` entries at once, so the owner's change shows immediately. Analytics expire on their own (up to a minute stale). Per-process, so with more than one API copy it needs a shared store.
- Uploads write `<name>.w480.webp` and `<name>.w900.webp` next to the picture (`makeThumbs` in storage.js, 2 at a time through `lib/limit.js`). Cards in the store and the AUZsStyle Sell/Stock lists load the small copy and fall back to the original on error. For old pictures run once: `docker compose exec api node src/tools/make-thumbs.js`.
- Caddy: `encode zstd gzip` on every site (snippet `compress`), and `/uploads/*` is sent with `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` (file names are unique). Needs `docker compose restart caddy`; not validated with a Caddy binary here.

## Server efficiency, phase 3 (Oct 2026): health notice, clean stops, container health, scaling runbook
- `db/243_health_alert.sql` + `runHealthAlert()` in maintenance.js: once a day, if connections > 20/35, memory reads < 95%, a big table is over 30% dead rows, or the API shows delay / memory / error / slow-request trouble, the admin gets a platform notice "Server health: needs a look" (and a push through the usual notifications path).
- The API handles SIGTERM/SIGINT: stops accepting, finishes requests in progress, exits (10 s limit). `docker-compose.yml` has healthchecks for postgres and the API (visible in `docker compose ps`) and stop grace periods.
- `docs/SCALING.md` is the plan for growing (bigger server first; shared state before a second API copy; managed Postgres last).
