### AUZsStyle's online return/exchange status: real stages, not a free-choice dropdown (db/247)
Owner report: the owner's own return/exchange status editor (`style/p-store.js`, Online store page) let them jump to ANY of the 5 statuses at any time -- `RS.filter(x => x !== r.status)` put up one button per OTHER status with no sequencing at all, and `sty_web_return_set` (db/232) only checked the new value was one of the 5 known strings, never that it was a real next step. Same complaint, same fix shape as the generic AUZslab POS's own return/exchange rework earlier this file (db/246) -- a different app and table (`sty_web_returns`, not `records` kind `returnreq`), so it needed its own migration.
- `sty_web_return_set` (db/247, redefined) now enforces the graph `requested -> approved | rejected -> received -> refunded`, one way only; a request already `rejected` or `refunded` can never be moved again (`'This request is already % and cannot be changed'`). Approving/rejecting and finishing a refund both need `sty_perm('sty_manage')`; marking an item received stays open to anyone with `sty_sell` (checking a returned item back in needs no special permission).
- `p-store.js`'s return-request sheet now only shows the real next step(s) (`RETURN_NEXT` map: requested -> Approve/Reject, approved -> Mark received, received -> Finish refund) instead of all 5 statuses; a terminal request (rejected/refunded) shows a plain "this cannot be changed" line and no action buttons at all.
- **Verified against a real scratch database**, as two different logged-in roles (a plain staffer and the owner): staff cannot self-approve ("Only a manager..."), skipping straight to refunded from approved is refused ("...one step at a time"), staff can mark received but cannot finish the refund, the owner can do both of the manager-gated steps, a refunded (or separately, a rejected) request cannot be changed again afterwards.
- `style/p-store.js?v=8`; `sw.js` bumped to `pos-v210`.
- Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/247_style_return_stages.sql` (no API rebuild -- the server code calling this function doesn't care what changed inside it).

## AUZsStyle: finishing a refund actually calls Razorpay now, not just a status label (db/248)

Owner asked, after the stage-fix above: does "Refunded" actually move money through Razorpay? It did not -- marking a return/exchange "Refunded" (either app) was always only a record-keeping step; staff still had to go refund the customer by hand wherever the money actually landed. Built the real integration for AUZsStyle specifically (the generic POS still has no payment gateway behind it at all, so there's nothing to call there).

- **The shop's OWN Razorpay account, never AUZslab's.** AUZsStyle already has a "bring your own Razorpay keys" model (db/232, `sty_store_secrets`) for taking online payments -- the money goes straight to the shop. A refund has to go out through those same keys, for the same reason: AUZslab never holds a shop's money, so it can't be the one issuing the refund either.
- **Only an order genuinely paid online through Razorpay gets an automatic refund.** A COD order, or a UPI payment the shop just confirmed by hand, has no Razorpay payment to reverse -- those keep using the plain "Finish refund" status change exactly as before (db/247). `sty_web_returns_list` (redefined) now also returns `rzpPaid` (true only when `payment_method='online' and payment_status='paid' and rzp_payment_id is not null`) so the UI can tell which path applies, per request.
- **Two columns added to `sty_web_returns`**: `refund_amount`, `rzp_refund_id` -- what was actually refunded and Razorpay's own refund id, shown on the request once done ("Refunded ₹X via Razorpay (rfnd_...)").
- **`sty_refund_prepare(p_return_id, p_amount)`** (new, server-only -- deliberately NOT in `rpc-registry.js`, same trust level as `sty_online_start`): `sty_guard('sty_manage')` (owner/manager only), checks the request is genuinely at `received`, the order was actually paid online, the amount is real and does not exceed what was charged, then returns the shop's own key/secret and the original Razorpay payment id -- it changes nothing yet.
- **`server/src/routes/payments.js`'s new `POST /payments/sty/refund/start`** (owner/manager, `withAuth`) does the real work in order: call `sty_refund_prepare` (validate, no side effect) -> call Razorpay's own `/v1/payments/{id}/refund` with the shop's keys -> only once Razorpay confirms it, call `sty_web_return_set(id, {status:'refunded', refundAmount, rzpRefundId})` -- the exact same function db/247/248 already gate with the full stage graph and the `sty_manage` permission check, so this route never writes the status itself and can't bypass either rule. A Razorpay failure leaves the request at `received` with a clear message, never half-changed.
- **`sb-client.js`** (the one shared client file every app loads) gained `sb.styRefund.start(returnId, amount)`, the same shape as the existing `autorenew`/`whatsapp` namespaces (a real HTTP call through the server, not a plain `rpc()`, since only the server can call out to Razorpay). Bumped `?v=7` everywhere it's linked (20 files) since it's one shared file.
- **`p-store.js`**: the "Finish refund" button on a `received` request now reads "Refund via Razorpay" and opens an amount-confirmation sheet (`styRazorpayRefundSheet`, prefilled with the order total, capped at it) when `r.rzpPaid`; a COD/manual order keeps the original plain confirm-and-done button. Both end up calling the one `sty_web_return_set` path either way.
- **Verified against a real scratch database** (seeded shop with real Razorpay-shaped test keys, a COD order and a Razorpay-paid order, both staff and owner logins): a COD order's request is correctly refused automatic refund ("...use 'Finish refund' instead"); a not-yet-`received` request is refused ("Mark the item received before refunding it"); an amount over the order total is refused; a plain staffer cannot prepare a refund (`sty_manage` required); a valid owner call returns the shop's own key/secret/payment id and changes nothing yet; simulating the server's own completion call (`sty_web_return_set` with the refund fields, as the route does after a real Razorpay call) correctly persists `refund_amount`/`rzp_refund_id` and moves the status to `refunded`; re-preparing a refund on an already-refunded request is correctly refused.
- **Not verified against the real Razorpay API** -- no live Razorpay Test Mode credentials were available here; only the SQL validation logic and the request/response shapes (matching Razorpay's documented refund endpoint, same pattern `/payments/sty/start` already uses for creating an order) were exercised. The first real refund through a shop's actual Razorpay Test Mode account is the true end-to-end check.
- `style/p-store.js?v=9`, `sb-client.js?v=7`, `sw.js` bumped to `pos-v211`.
- Deploy: `git pull`, `psql ... -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 < db/248_style_razorpay_refund.sql`, `docker compose up -d --build api` (server/src/routes/payments.js changed -- this one does need the rebuild, unlike the previous two).

### Cost per client (Admin -> System resources, db/246)
"What each client costs us each month" card under Product load. `GET /admin/costs` (routes/admin.js, platform admin only) joins `admin_client_usage()` (rows per client across records/guest_orders/bookings/sty_sales/acc_documents/mob_sales, WhatsApp messages sent through the AUZslab number in 30 days split utility vs marketing, online Razorpay payments in 30 days, what the client pays = `tenants.monthly_fee`), files per business on disk (`getTenantFileSizes()`: `uploads/site/<slug>` + private docs `<tenant id>`) and request count/server time per client (`metrics.js` `tenantUsage()`, keyed by the tenant id in the login token; in memory since the API last started, so it resets on restart). Fixed costs (server + failover + other tools + AUZslab's own WhatsApp messages) are shared by usage weights (default 50% server time, 30% data, 20% files; a dimension with zero total is dropped); WhatsApp (default Rs 0.12 utility / Rs 0.78 marketing; the marketing template list is in `admin_client_usage`) and Razorpay (2%) are exact per client. Demo tenants are left out. Settings are editable in the same card (`platform_flags.cost_settings`, `admin_cost_settings` / `admin_set_cost_settings`): server starts at Rs 2,150 (DigitalOcean $24 droplet, 2 vCPU / 4 GB); raise it, and fill the failover line, when the server is upgraded. Backblaze, Resend, Sentry and Firebase are on free tiers (0). It is an estimate, not an invoice. Deploy: `bash tools/migrate.sh apply`, `docker compose up -d --build api`, `git pull`. Not seen on a real screen.

## Fresh start and the Showoff Salon live demo (Oct 2026, `db_data/fresh_start.sql`)
Owner: every client so far was a trial; wipe them and start onboarding real clients. `db_data/fresh_start.sql` (dry run by default, `-v confirm=yes` to do it, backup first; see `docs/DATA_WIPE.md`) keeps `showoffsalon`, `arpittraders`, `newbookworld`, `demo`, platform admins, team members and leads; deletes every other business, its owners' and staff logins, and ALL client bills / quotes / client payments (numbering restarts); clears the real business data of `arpittraders` and `newbookworld`; keeps the Genx Clothes business (AUZsStyle demo, found by a slug or name starting "genx") completely untouched, data and images (setup and logins stay; they are NOT marked is_demo, so they show empty apps); turns `showoffsalon` into the public salon demo (real bookings, clients, bills, expenses removed; menu/photos/site kept; `is_demo = true`; logins `showoffsalon-demo@auzslab.in` + the shared demo password, staff `priya.showoffsalon` / PIN 1234). `demo-salon` is gone: every marketing page, `site/demo.html`, `tests/run-live.mjs` and `DEMO_LOGIN_EMAILS` now point at `showoffsalon`.
- **Salon demo refresh on the records engine:** the old demo reseed (`reseedDemo`) replaced the whole salon with the neutral template, which would have wiped Showoff's own menu and look. For a demo salon on the generic engine (`usesGeneric`), `refreshGenericDemo()` in `server/src/salon/storage.js` now regenerates only the fake activity (`addDemoActivity()` in `salon-seed.js`, built from the tenant's own menu, dated relative to today) every 12 h (`settings.demoSeededAt`), and the admin "Reset" button does the same. Store-engine demos (the old `demo-salon`) still use the full reseed. Deploy: `docker compose up -d --build api` (server changed), `git pull`; the script is run by hand.
- Not verified on the live database (the sandbox only ran it on a rebuilt scratch copy). Real staff logins of Showoff and any payroll employees it created are not removed by the script.

## AUZsFinance (feature key `finance`, db/250-251, `app/public/finance.html` + `app/public/finance/`), Phase 1 of 4
Business finance and control app on the AUZsLedger double-entry engine (`acc_*`), for clients and AUZslab's own books. Minimal AUZsStyle-like UI (`finance.fine.css`). Product address `auzsfinance.auzslab.in`.
- **Model:** `fin_*` tables (RLS on, no policies, only SECURITY DEFINER `fin_*` functions starting with `fin_guard(perm)`: view = acc_view, write = acc_post, admin = acc_admin; registered in `rpc-registry.js`). Money accounts wrap ledger accounts (codes `FNxxxx`); partners have capital and drawings ledgers; each loan has its own ledger (interest on repayment posts to 5113, interest received to other income). Kinds: money_in, expense, capital_in, drawing, loan_taken/given/repay/collect, transfer, credit_sale, credit_purchase, dues_in, dues_out, asset_buy. Every entry is one balanced journal (`acc_post_journal`, source `fin_txn`), idempotent on a client op id, never deleted (reverse with `fin_reverse_txn`), period lock honoured, evidence/review status per entry (`fin_mark_txn`, `fin_attach`).
- **People dues use their own ledgers** (`fin_dues_in` 1450, `fin_dues_out` 2450) so they never disturb the AUZsLedger AR/AP integrity check. `acc_storage_check()` was redefined to accept finance tenants. `fin_integrity_check()` is the books health check.
- **Wiring:** `db/251` price row `finance` (0 = quote by hand) and reserved slug `auzsfinance`; admin client editor, account page, hub tile, icons + launch animation (`finance` mark in `tools/make-icons.mjs`), `sw.js` (pos-v213). Tests: `node tests/run-local.mjs finance` (18 checks, `testfin` tenant). Not built yet: Phase 2 (P&L / balance sheet / cash flow for finance-only tenants, ageing, reconciliation, assets and depreciation, budgets), Phase 3 (receipt scan, bank import, recurring, approvals, reminders, anomalies), Phase 4 (GST/TDS, multi-company), Hindi, per-tenant demo seed.
- **Real bug fixed on the way:** the global error catch in `server/src/index.js` treated `req.destroyed` (true after the body is read in Node 22) as a closed client and returned an empty 200 for EVERY refused request after a body. It now only checks `ERR_STREAM_PREMATURE_CLOSE` / `res.destroyed`. Needs `docker compose up -d --build api`.
- Deploy: `git pull`, `bash tools/migrate.sh apply` (db/250, 251), `docker compose up -d --build api`. Not seen on a real phone.
